## Excise Act, 2001 {#excise-act-2001 .Title-of-Act}

[S.C.]{.abbr title="Statutes of Canada"} 2002, c. 22
:::

Assented to 2002-06-13

An Act respecting the taxation of spirits, wine and tobacco and the
treatment of ships' stores
::::

:::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: Enacts
Her Majesty, by and with the advice and consent of the Senate and House
of Commons of Canada, enacts as follows:
:::

## [Short Title]{.HTitleText1} {#h-178738 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Short title

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** This Act may be cited as
the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1).

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-178741 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** The definitions in this
section apply in this Act.

[[absolute ethyl alcohol]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[absolute ethyl alcohol]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the substance
    with the chemical composition C~2~H~5~OH. ([alcool éthylique
    absolu]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[accredited representative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[accredited representative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who
    is entitled under the [Foreign Missions and International
    Organizations Act](/eng/acts/F-29.4) to the tax exemptions specified
    in Article 34 of the Convention set out in Schedule I to that Act or
    in Article 49 of the Convention set out in Schedule II to that
    Act. ([représentant accrédité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[additional cannabis duty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[additional cannabis duty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a duty
    imposed under section 158.2 or 158.22. ([droit additionnel sur le
    cannabis]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[additional vaping duty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[additional vaping duty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a duty imposed
    under section 158.58. ([droit additionnel sur le
    vapotage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Canada Revenue Agency
    continued by subsection 4(1) of the [Canada Revenue Agency
    Act](/eng/acts/C-10.11). ([Agence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[alcohol]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[alcohol]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means spirits or
    wine. ([alcool]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[alcohol licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[alcohol licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who is a
    spirits licensee or a wine licensee. ([titulaire de licence
    d'alcool]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[alcohol registrant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[alcohol registrant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who holds
    an alcohol registration issued under section 17. ([détenteur
    autorisé d'alcool]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[analyst]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[analyst]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who is designated as
    an analyst under section 11. ([analyste]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[approved formulation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[approved formulation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any product made with alcohol by a licensed user
      in accordance with a formula for which the user has approval from
      the Minister; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any imported product that, in the opinion of the
      Minister, would be a product under paragraph (a) if it were made
      in Canada by a licensed user. ([préparation
      approuvée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[assessment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[assessment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an assessment under this
    Act and includes a reassessment. ([cotisation]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[beer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[beer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means beer or malt liquor as defined in
    section 4 of the [Excise
    Act](/eng/acts/E-14). ([bière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[black stock]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[black stock]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of manufactured
    tobacco, means that the tobacco is stamped but not marked in
    accordance with any statute of a province to indicate that it is
    intended for retail sale in a particular province or in particular
    provinces. ([non ciblé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[bottle-your-own premises]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[bottle-your-own premises]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means premises in
    which, in accordance with the laws of the province in which they are
    located, alcohol is supplied from a marked special container of
    alcohol for the purpose of being packaged by a purchaser. ([centre
    de remplissage libre-service]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[bulk]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[bulk]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of alcohol, means alcohol
    that is not packaged. ([en vrac]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[cannabis]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[cannabis]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
    subsection 2(1) of the [Cannabis
    Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5). ([cannabis]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[cannabis duty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[cannabis duty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a duty imposed under
    section 158.19 or 158.21. ([droit sur le cannabis]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[cannabis excise stamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[cannabis excise stamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a stamp that is
    issued by the Minister under subsection 158.03(1) and that has not
    been cancelled under section 158.07. ([timbre d'accise de
    cannabis]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[cannabis licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[cannabis licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person that holds
    a cannabis licence issued under section 14. ([titulaire de licence
    de cannabis]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[cannabis plant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[cannabis plant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
    subsection 2(1) of the [Cannabis Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5). ([plante de
    cannabis]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[cannabis product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[cannabis product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a product that is cannabis but that is not
      industrial hemp produced or imported in accordance with the
      [Cannabis Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5) or the [Industrial Hemp
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-145),

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a product that is an industrial hemp by-product,

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} anything that is made with or contains a product
      described in paragraph (a) or (b), or

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed substance, material or thing,

    but does not include a prescribed substance, material or
    thing. ([produit du cannabis]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[cigar]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[cigar]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a cigarillo or cheroot; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any roll or tubular construction intended for
      smoking that consists of a filler composed of pieces of natural or
      reconstituted leaf tobacco, a binder of natural or reconstituted
      leaf tobacco in which the filler is wrapped and a wrapper of
      natural or reconstituted leaf tobacco. ([cigare]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[cigarette]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[cigarette]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes any roll or tubular
    construction intended for smoking, other than a cigar or a tobacco
    stick. If a cigarette exceeds 102 mm in length, each portion of 76
    mm or less is considered to be a separate
    cigarette. ([cigarette]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Commissioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Commissioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Commissioner of
    Revenue appointed under section 25 of the [Canada Revenue Agency
    Act](/eng/acts/C-10.11). ([commissaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[container]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[container]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a tobacco product,
    a cannabis product or a vaping product, means a wrapper, package,
    carton, box, crate, bottle, vial or other container that contains
    the tobacco product, cannabis product or vaping
    product. ([contenant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[customs bonded carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[customs bonded carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who
    transports or causes to be transported goods in accordance with
    section 20 of the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6). ([transporteur
    cautionné]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[customs bonded warehouse]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[customs bonded warehouse]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a place that
    is licensed as a bonded warehouse under the [Customs
    Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011). ([entrepôt de
    stockage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[customs bonded warehouse licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[customs bonded warehouse licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
    person licensed under the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) to
    operate a bonded warehouse. ([exploitant agréé d'entrepôt de
    stockage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[data]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[data]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means representations, in any form, of
    information or concepts. ([données]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[denature]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[denature]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means to denature spirits into
    denatured alcohol or specially denatured alcohol using prescribed
    denaturants in the prescribed
    manner. ([dénaturation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[denatured alcohol]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[denatured alcohol]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any prescribed grade
    of denatured alcohol made from spirits in accordance with the
    prescribed specification for that grade. ([alcool
    dénaturé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[dried cannabis]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[dried cannabis]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
    subsection 2(1) of the [Cannabis Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5). ([cannabis
    séché]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[dutiable amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[dutiable amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a cannabis
    product, means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if paragraph (b) does not apply, the amount
      determined by the formula

      ::: {#178785 .Paragraph}
      A × \[100%/(100% + B + C)\]

      where

      [A]{.dfn}
      :   is the total of the following amounts that the purchaser is
          liable to pay to the vendor by reason of, or in respect of,
          the sale of the cannabis product:
          - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the consideration, as determined for the
            purposes of Part IX of the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15),
            for the cannabis product,

          - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any additional consideration, as
            determined for the purposes of that Part, for the container
            in which the cannabis product is contained, and

          - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} any amount of consideration, as
            determined for the purposes of that Part, that is in
            addition to the amounts referred to in subparagraphs (i) and
            (ii), whether payable at the same or any other time,
            including, but not limited to, any amount charged for or to
            make provision for advertising, financing, commissions or
            any other matter,

      [B]{.dfn}
      :   is the percentage set out in paragraph 2(a) of Schedule 7, and

      [C]{.dfn}
      :   is
          - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if additional cannabis duty in respect of a
            specified province is imposed on the cannabis product, the
            prescribed percentage in respect of the specified province,
            or

          - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, 0%; and
      :::

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in prescribed circumstances, the amount
      determined in prescribed manner. ([somme passible de
      droits]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[duty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[duty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, unless a contrary intention
    appears, the duty imposed under this Act and the duty levied under
    section 21.1 or 21.2 of the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011)
    and, except in Parts 3 and 4, includes special
    duty. ([droit]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[duty free shop]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[duty free shop]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a place that is
    licensed as a duty free shop under the [Customs
    Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6). ([boutique hors taxes]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[duty free shop licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[duty free shop licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person
    licensed under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) to operate a duty
    free shop. ([exploitant agréé de boutique hors
    taxes]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[duty-paid market]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[duty-paid market]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the market for goods
    in respect of which duty, other than special duty, is
    payable. ([marché des marchandises acquittées]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[duty-paid value]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[duty-paid value]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of imported cigars, the value of the
      cigars as it would be determined for the purpose of calculating an
      [ad valorem]{.otherLang lang="la"} duty on the cigars in
      accordance with the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), whether or
      not the cigars are subject to [ad valorem]{.otherLang lang="la"}
      duty, plus the amount of any duty imposed on the cigars under
      section 42 of this Act and section 20 of the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of imported cigars that, when
      imported, are contained in containers or otherwise prepared for
      sale, the total of the value of the cigars as determined in
      accordance with paragraph (a) and the value similarly determined
      of the container in which they are contained. ([valeur à
      l'acquitté]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[excise stamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[excise stamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a tobacco excise stamp, a
    cannabis excise stamp or a vaping excise stamp. ([timbre
    d'accise]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[excise warehouse]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[excise warehouse]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the premises of an
    excise warehouse licensee that are specified by the Minister as the
    excise warehouse of the licensee. ([entrepôt
    d'accise]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[excise warehouse licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[excise warehouse licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who
    holds an excise warehouse licence issued under section
    19. ([exploitant agréé d'entrepôt d'accise]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[export]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[export]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means to export from
    Canada. ([exportation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[ferment-on-premises facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[ferment-on-premises facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
    premises of a ferment-on-premises registrant that are specified by
    the Minister as the registrant's ferment-on-premises
    facility. ([vinerie libre-service]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[ferment-on-premises registrant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[ferment-on-premises registrant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
    person who holds a ferment-on-premises registration issued under
    section 15. ([exploitant autorisé de vinerie
    libre-service]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[fiscal half-year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[fiscal half-year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a fiscal half-year as
    determined under subsection 159(1.1). ([semestre
    d'exercice]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[fiscal month]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[fiscal month]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a fiscal month as
    determined under section 159. ([mois d'exercice]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[fiscal year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[fiscal year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of a person means the same
    period that is the person's fiscal year under Part IX of the [Excise
    Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15). ([exercice]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[flowering material]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[flowering material]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the whole or any
    part (other than viable seeds) of an inflorescence of a cannabis
    plant at any stage of development, including the infructescence
    stage of development. ([matière florifère]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[foreign duty free shop]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[foreign duty free shop]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a retail store
    that is located in a country other than Canada and that is
    authorized under the laws of that country to sell goods free of
    certain duties and taxes to individuals who are about to leave that
    country. ([boutique hors taxes à l'étranger]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[foreign ships' stores]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[foreign ships' stores]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means tobacco products
    taken on board a vessel or aircraft while the vessel or aircraft is
    outside Canada and that are intended for consumption by or sale to
    the passengers or crew while the passengers and crew are on board
    the vessel or aircraft. ([provisions de bord à
    l'étranger]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[fresh cannabis]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[fresh cannabis]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
    subsection 1(1) of the [Cannabis
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-144). ([cannabis
    frais]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Her Majesty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Her Majesty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means Her Majesty in right of
    Canada. ([Sa Majesté]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[immediate container]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[immediate container]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a vaping
    substance, means the container that is in direct contact with the
    vaping substance. It does not include a vaping device. ([contenant
    immédiat]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[import]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[import]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means to import into
    Canada. ([importation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[industrial hemp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[industrial hemp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means cannabis that is
    industrial hemp for the purposes of the [Cannabis
    Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5) or the [Industrial Hemp
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-145). ([chanvre
    industriel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[industrial hemp by-product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[industrial hemp by-product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means flowering
    material (other than viable achenes) or non-flowering material that
    has been removed or separated from an industrial hemp plant and that
    has not

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} been disposed of by retting or by otherwise
      rendering it into a condition such that it cannot be used for any
      purpose not permitted under the [Controlled Drugs and Substances
      Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8); or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} been disposed of in a similar manner under the
      [Cannabis Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5). ([sous-produit de chanvre
      industriel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[industrial hemp grower]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[industrial hemp grower]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person that
    holds a licence or permit under the [Controlled Drugs and Substances
    Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8) or the [Cannabis Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5)
    authorizing the person to produce industrial hemp
    plants. ([producteur de chanvre industriel]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[industrial hemp plant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[industrial hemp plant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a cannabis
    plant, including a seedling, that is industrial hemp. ([plante de
    chanvre industriel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[intoxicating liquor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[intoxicating liquor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
    in section 2 of the [Importation of Intoxicating Liquors
    Act](/eng/acts/I-3). ([boisson enivrante]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[judge]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[judge]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of any matter, means a
    judge of a superior court having jurisdiction in the province in
    which the matter arises or a judge of the Federal
    Court. ([juge]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[licensed tobacco dealer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[licensed tobacco dealer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who
    holds a tobacco dealer's licence issued under section
    14. ([commerçant de tabac agréé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[licensed user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[licensed user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who holds a
    user's licence issued under section 14. ([utilisateur
    agréé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[liquor authority]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[liquor authority]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a government board,
    commission or agency that is authorized by the laws of a province to
    sell intoxicating liquor. ([administration des
    alcools]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[listed international agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[listed international agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Convention on Mutual Administrative
      Assistance in Tax Matters, concluded at Strasbourg on January 25,
      1988, as amended from time to time by a protocol, or other
      international instrument, as ratified by Canada; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a comprehensive tax information exchange
      agreement that Canada has entered into and that has effect, in
      respect of another country or jurisdiction. ([accord international
      désigné]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[low-THC cannabis product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[low-THC cannabis product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a cannabis
    product

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} consisting entirely of

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} fresh cannabis,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} dried cannabis, or

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} oil that contains anything referred to in
        item 1 or 3 of Schedule 1 to the [Cannabis
        Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5) and that is in liquid form at a
        temperature of 22 ± 2°C; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any part of which does not have a maximum yield
      of more than 0.3% THC w/w, taking into account the potential to
      convert THCA into THC, as determined in accordance with the
      [Cannabis Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5). ([produit du cannabis à faible
      teneur en THC]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[manufacture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[manufacture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a tobacco product, any step in the
      preparation or working up of raw leaf tobacco into the tobacco
      product, including packing, stemming, reconstituting, converting
      or packaging the raw leaf tobacco or tobacco product; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a vaping product, any step in the
      production of the vaping product, including inserting a vaping
      substance into a vaping device or packaging the vaping
      product. ([fabrication]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[manufactured tobacco]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[manufactured tobacco]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means every article,
    other than a cigar or packaged raw leaf tobacco, that is
    manufactured in whole or in part from raw leaf tobacco by any
    process. ([tabac fabriqué]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[mark]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[mark]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a special container of spirits, to mark in the
      prescribed form and manner to indicate that the container is
      intended for

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} delivery to and use by a registered user, or

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} delivery to and use at a bottle-your-own
        premises; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a special container of wine, to mark in the
      prescribed form and manner to indicate that the container is
      intended for delivery to and use at a bottle-your-own
      premises. ([marquer]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Minister of National
    Revenue. ([ministre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[month]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[month]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a period beginning on a
    particular day in a calendar month and ending on

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the day immediately before the day in the next
      calendar month that has the same calendar number as the particular
      day; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the next calendar month does not have a day
      that has the same calendar number as the particular day, the last
      day of that next calendar month. ([mois]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[non-duty-paid]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[non-duty-paid]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of packaged
    alcohol or a cannabis product, means that duty (other than special
    duty in the case of alcohol) has not been paid on the alcohol or
    cannabis product. ([non acquitté]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[non-flowering material]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[non-flowering material]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any part of a
    cannabis plant other than flowering material, viable seeds and a
    part of the plant referred to in Schedule 2 to the [Cannabis
    Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5). ([matière non florifère]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, except in section 167, in the
    definition [[contact information]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in
    subsection 211(1) and in sections 226 and 296,

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a person who is appointed or employed in the
      administration or enforcement of this Act;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a member of the Royal Canadian Mounted Police or
      a member of a police force designated under subsection 10(1); and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} with respect to imported goods that have not been
      released under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), an officer as
      defined in subsection 2(1) of that
      Act. ([préposé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[packaged]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[packaged]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of raw leaf tobacco, a tobacco
      product, a cannabis product or a vaping product, packaged in a
      prescribed package; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of alcohol, packaged

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in a container of a capacity of not more than
        100 L that is ordinarily sold to consumers without the alcohol
        being repackaged, or

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in a marked special
        container. ([emballé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[partially manufactured tobacco]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[partially manufactured tobacco]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
    manufactured tobacco that is cut filler or cut rag or that is
    manufactured less fully than cut filler or cut rag. ([tabac
    partiellement fabriqué]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[peace officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[peace officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
    section 2 of the [Criminal Code](/eng/acts/C-46). ([agent de la
    paix]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual, a partnership, a
    corporation, a trust, the estate of a deceased individual, a
    government or a body that is a society, a union, a club, an
    association, a commission or another organization of any
    kind. ([personne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[personal use]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[personal use]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to the use of a
    good by an individual, means the use of the good by the individual
    or by others at the individual's expense. It does not include the
    sale or other commercial use of the good. ([usage
    personnel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a form or the manner of filing a
      form, authorized by the Minister;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the information to be given on or
      with a form, specified by the Minister; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, prescribed by regulation or
      determined in accordance with rules prescribed by
      regulation. ([Version anglaise
      seulement]{.DefinitionOtherLangOnly})

[[prescription cannabis drug]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[prescription cannabis drug]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a cannabis
    product that is a drug that has been assigned a drug identification
    number under the [Food and Drug
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870), other than

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a drug or mixture of drugs that may, under the
      [Food and Drugs Act](/eng/acts/F-27) or the [Controlled Drugs and
      Substances Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8), be sold to a
      [[consumer]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in subsection
      123(1) of the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15), without a
      [[prescription]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in section 1 of
      Part I of Schedule VI to the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15); or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed cannabis product or a cannabis
      product of a prescribed class. ([drogue de cannabis sur
      ordonnance]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[produce]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[produce]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of spirits, to bring into existence by
      distillation or other process or to recover;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of wine, to bring into existence by
      fermentation; or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a cannabis product, has the same
      meaning as in subsection 2(1) of the [Cannabis
      Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5) but also includes packaging the cannabis
      product. ([production]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[raw leaf tobacco]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[raw leaf tobacco]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means unmanufactured
    tobacco or the leaves and stems of the tobacco plant. ([tabac en
    feuilles]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[record]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[record]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any material on which data are
    recorded or marked and which is capable of being read or understood
    by a person or a computer system or other
    device. ([registre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[registered user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[registered user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who holds a
    user's registration issued under section 16. ([utilisateur
    autorisé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[reporting period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[reporting period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a reporting period as
    determined under section 159.1. ([période de
    déclaration]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[responsible]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[responsible]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a person, means
    that

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of bulk alcohol, the person is
      responsible for the bulk alcohol in accordance with sections 104
      to 121;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a cannabis product, the person is
      responsible for the cannabis product in accordance with sections
      158.17 and 158.18; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vaping product, the person is
      responsible for the vaping product in accordance with sections
      158.54 to 158.56. ([responsable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[restricted formulation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[restricted formulation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an approved
    formulation on which the Minister has imposed the condition or
    restriction under section 143 that the formulation can only be used
    by a licensed user or be exported. ([préparation assujettie à des
    restrictions]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[sale price]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[sale price]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of cigars, means the
    total of

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount charged as the price for the cigars
      before an amount payable in respect of a tax under Part IX of the
      [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15) is added,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amount charged as the price for or in respect
      of the container in which the cigars are contained,

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any amount that the purchaser is liable to pay to
      the vendor by reason of or in respect of the sale of the cigars in
      addition to the amount charged as the price, whether payable at
      the same or any other time, including, but not limited to, any
      amount charged for or to make provision for advertising,
      financing, commissions or any other matter, and

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the amount of duty imposed on the cigars under
      section 42. ([prix de vente]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[SDA registrant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[SDA registrant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who holds a
    specially denatured alcohol registration issued under section
    18. ([détenteur autorisé d'alcool spécialement
    dénaturé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[special container]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[special container]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of spirits, a container of a capacity
      of more than 100 L but not more than 1,500 L; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of wine, a container of a capacity of
      more than 100 L. ([contenant spécial]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[special duty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[special duty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a tobacco product, a special duty
      imposed under subsection 53(1), 54(2) or 56(1); and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of imported spirits, the special duty
      imposed under subsection 133(1). ([droit spécial]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[special excise warehouse]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[special excise warehouse]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the premises
    of a special excise warehouse licensee that are specified by the
    Minister as the special excise warehouse of the licensee. ([entrepôt
    d'accise spécial]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[special excise warehouse licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[special excise warehouse licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
    person who holds a special excise warehouse licence issued under
    section 20. ([exploitant agréé d'entrepôt d'accise
    spécial]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[specially denatured alcohol]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[specially denatured alcohol]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any
    prescribed grade of specially denatured alcohol made from spirits in
    accordance with the prescribed specification for that
    grade. ([alcool spécialement dénaturé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[specified premises]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[specified premises]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a licensed
    user, means the premises of the licensed user that are specified by
    the Minister under subsection 23(3). ([local
    déterminé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[specified province]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[specified province]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a prescribed
    province. ([province déterminée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[specified vaping province]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[specified vaping province]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a prescribed
    province. ([province déterminée de vapotage]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[spirits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[spirits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any material or substance
    containing more than 0.5% absolute ethyl alcohol by volume other
    than

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} wine;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} beer;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} vinegar;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} denatured alcohol;

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} specially denatured alcohol;

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} fusel oil or other refuse produced as a result of
      the distillation process;

    - [(g)]{.lawlabel} an approved formulation; or

    - [(h)]{.lawlabel} any product containing or manufactured from a
      material or substance referred to in paragraphs (b) to (g) that is
      not consumable as a beverage. ([spiritueux]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[spirits licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[spirits licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who holds a
    spirits licence issued under section 14. ([titulaire de licence de
    spiritueux]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[stamped]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[stamped]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a tobacco product, that a tobacco
      excise stamp, and all prescribed information in a prescribed
      format in respect of the tobacco product, are stamped, impressed,
      printed or marked on, indented into or affixed to the tobacco
      product or its container in the prescribed manner to indicate that
      duty, other than special duty, has been paid on the tobacco
      product;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a cannabis product, that a cannabis
      excise stamp, and all prescribed information in a prescribed
      format in respect of the cannabis product, are stamped, impressed,
      printed or marked on, indented into or affixed to the cannabis
      product or its container in the prescribed manner to indicate that
      duty has been paid on the cannabis product; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a vaping product, that a vaping
      excise stamp, and all prescribed information in a prescribed
      format in respect of the vaping product, are stamped, impressed,
      printed or marked on, indented into or affixed to the vaping
      product or its container in the prescribed manner to indicate that
      duty has been paid on the vaping
      product. ([estampillé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[sufferance warehouse]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[sufferance warehouse]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
    in subsection 2(1) of the [Customs
    Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6). ([entrepôt d'attente]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[sufferance warehouse licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[sufferance warehouse licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person
    licensed under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) to operate a
    sufferance warehouse. ([exploitant agréé d'entrepôt
    d'attente]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[take for use]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[take for use]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of alcohol, to consume, analyze or
      destroy alcohol or to use alcohol for any purpose that results in
      a product other than alcohol; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a cannabis product or a vaping
      product, to consume, analyze or destroy the cannabis product or
      vaping product. ([utilisation pour soi]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[Tax Court]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Tax Court]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Tax Court of
    Canada. ([Cour de l'impôt]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[THC]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[THC]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means Δ^9^-tetrahydrocannabinol ((6aR,
    10aR)-6a, 7,8,10a-tetrahydro-6,6,9-trimethyl-3-pentyl-6H-dibenzo
    \[b,d\] pyran-1-ol). ([THC]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[THCA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[THCA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means delta-9-tetrahydrocannabinolic
    acid. ([ATHC]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[tobacco dealer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[tobacco dealer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person, other than a
    tobacco licensee, who purchases for resale, sells or offers to sell
    raw leaf tobacco on which duty is not imposed under this
    Act. ([commerçant de tabac]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[tobacco excise stamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[tobacco excise stamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a stamp that is
    issued by the Minister under subsection 25.1(1) and that has not
    been cancelled under section 25.5. ([timbre d'accise de
    tabac]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[tobacco licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[tobacco licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who holds a
    tobacco licence issued under section 14. ([titulaire de licence de
    tabac]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[tobacco manufacturing equipment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[tobacco manufacturing equipment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any
    machinery or equipment designed or modified specifically for the
    manufacture of a tobacco product. ([matériel de fabrication du
    tabac]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[tobacco marking]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[tobacco marking]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means prescribed information
    that is required under this Act to be printed on or affixed to a
    container of tobacco products that are not required under this Act
    to be stamped. ([mention obligatoire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[tobacco product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[tobacco product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means manufactured tobacco,
    packaged raw leaf tobacco or cigars. ([produit du
    tabac]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[tobacco stick]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[tobacco stick]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any roll or tubular
    construction of tobacco intended for smoking, other than a cigar,
    that requires further preparation to be consumed. If a tobacco stick
    exceeds 90 mm in length or 800 mg, each portion of 60 mm or less or
    each portion of 650 mg or less, respectively, is considered to be a
    separate stick. ([bâtonnet de tabac]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[total THC]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[total THC]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of a cannabis product means the
    total quantity of THC, in milligrams, that the cannabis product
    could yield, taking into account the potential to convert THCA into
    THC, as determined in accordance with the [Cannabis
    Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5). ([THC total]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[vaping device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[vaping device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means property (other than
    prescribed property) that is

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a device that produces emissions in the form of
      an aerosol and is intended to be brought to the mouth for
      inhalation of the aerosol;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a vaping pod or another part that may be used
      with a device referred to in paragraph (a); or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed property. ([dispositif de
      vapotage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[vaping duty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[vaping duty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a duty imposed under
    section 158.57. ([droit sur le vapotage]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[vaping excise stamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[vaping excise stamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a stamp that is
    issued by the Minister under subsection 158.36(1) and that has not
    been cancelled under section 158.4. ([timbre d'accise de
    vapotage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[vaping product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[vaping product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a vaping substance that is not contained within a
      vaping device; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a vaping device that contains a vaping substance.

    It does not include a cannabis product or a tobacco
    product. ([produit de vapotage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[vaping product drug]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[vaping product drug]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vaping product
    (other than a prescribed vaping product) that is

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a drug that has been assigned a drug
      identification number under the [Food and Drug
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870); or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed vaping product. ([drogue de produit
      de vapotage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[vaping product licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[vaping product licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person that
    holds a vaping product licence issued under section 14. ([titulaire
    de licence de produits de vapotage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[vaping product marking]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[vaping product marking]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means prescribed
    information that is required under this Act to be printed on, or
    affixed to, a container of vaping products that are not required
    under this Act to be stamped. ([mention obligatoire pour
    vapotage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[vaping substance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[vaping substance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a substance or mixture of substances, whether or
      not it contains nicotine, that is produced to be used, or sold for
      use, with a vaping device to produce emissions in the form of an
      aerosol; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed substance, material or thing.

    It does not include a prescribed substance, material or
    thing. ([substance de vapotage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[vegetative cannabis plant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[vegetative cannabis plant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a cannabis
    plant, including a seedling, that has not yet produced reproductive
    structures, including flowers, fruits or seeds. ([plante de cannabis
    à l'état végétatif]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[viable seed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[viable seed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a viable seed of a
    cannabis plant that is not an industrial hemp plant. ([graine
    viable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[wine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[wine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a beverage, containing more than 0.5% absolute
      ethyl alcohol by volume, that is produced without distillation,
      other than distillation to reduce the absolute ethyl alcohol
      content, by the alcoholic fermentation of

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an agricultural product other than grain,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a plant or plant product, other than grain,
        that is not an agricultural product, or

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a product wholly or partially derived from an
        agricultural product or plant or plant product other than grain;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} sake; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a beverage described by paragraph (a) or (b) that
      is fortified not in excess of 22.9% absolute ethyl alcohol by
      volume. ([vin]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[wine licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[wine licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who holds a
    wine licence issued under section 14. ([titulaire de licence de
    vin]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})
