[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dutiable amount --- consideration

**[[2.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.1 .sectionLabel}** The following rules
apply in determining an amount of consideration for the purposes of the
definition [[dutiable amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} if a provision of Part IX of the [Excise Tax
  Act](/eng/acts/E-15) deems the consideration, or part of the
  consideration, for a supply not to be consideration for the supply, a
  supply to be made for no consideration or a supply not to have been
  made by a person, that deeming does not apply for that determination;
  and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} subsection 155(1) of that Act is to be read without
  reference to "and the recipient of the supply is not a registrant who
  is acquiring the property or service for consumption, use or supply
  exclusively in the course of commercial activities of the recipient,".

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 27, s. 63
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}References to other enactments

**[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** A reference in this Act to
a repealed enactment, or a portion of it, of the legislature of a
province or territory shall, with respect to a subsequent transaction,
matter or thing, be read as a reference to the provisions of any
enactment replacing the repealed enactment or portion that relate to the
same subject-matter as the repealed enactment or portion. If there is no
replacement enactment or portion, or if there are no provisions in the
replacement enactment that relate to the same subject-matter, the
repealed enactment or portion shall be read as unrepealed in so far as
is necessary to maintain or give effect to the reference.

Meaning of [[administration or enforcement of this
Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

**[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** For greater certainty, a
reference in this Act to [[administration or enforcement of this
Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes the collection of any amount payable
under this Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Constructive possession

- **[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of section 25.2, subsections 25.3(1), 30(1), 32(1) and
  32.1(1), section 61, subsections 70(1) and 88(1), section 158.04,
  subsections 158.05(1) and 158.11(1) and (2), section 158.37,
  subsections 158.38(1) and 158.44(1) and (2), sections 230 and 231 and
  subsection 238.1(1), if one of two or more persons, with the knowledge
  and consent of the rest of them, has anything in the person's
  possession, it is deemed to be in the custody and possession of each
  and all of them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
  [[possession]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In this section and in section 25.2, subsections
  25.3(1), 30(1), 32(1) and 32.1(1), section 61, subsections 70(1) and
  88(1), section 158.04, subsections 158.05(1) and 158.11(1) and (2),
  section 158.37 and subsections 158.38(1), 158.44(1) and (2) and
  238.1(1), [[possession]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means not only having in
  one's own personal possession but also knowingly

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} having in the actual possession or custody of
    another person; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} having in any place, whether belonging to or
    occupied by one's self or not, for one's own use or benefit or that
    of another person.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 5
- 2008, c. 28, s. 51
- 2010, c. 12, s. 39
- 2018, c. 12, s. 70
- [2022, c. 10, s. 55](#1356375-1366739){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356375-1366739.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-5-20180621.html "Link to previous version of section 5"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Arm's length

- **[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of this Act,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} related persons are deemed not to deal with each
    other at arm's length; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is a question of fact whether persons not
    related to each other were, at any particular time, dealing with
    each other at arm's length.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Related persons

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, persons are related to
  each other if they are related persons within the meaning of
  subsections 251(2) to (6) of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3),
  except that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a reference in those subsections to "corporation"
    shall be read as a reference to "corporation or partnership"; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a reference in those subsections to "shares" or
    "shareholders" shall, in respect of a partnership, be read as a
    reference to "rights" or "partners", respectively.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Associated persons

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, a particular
  corporation is associated with another corporation if, by reason of
  subsections 256(1) to (6) of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3),
  the particular corporation is associated with the other corporation
  for the purposes of that Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Corporations controlled by same person
  or group

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, a person other than a
  corporation is associated with a particular corporation if the
  particular corporation is controlled by the person or by a group of
  persons of which the person is a member and each of whom is associated
  with each of the others.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Partnership or trust

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, a person is associated
  with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a partnership if the total of the shares of the
    profits of the partnership to which the person and all other persons
    who are associated with the person are entitled is more than half of
    the total profits of the partnership, or would be more than half of
    the total profits of the partnership if it had profits; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a trust if the total of the values of the interests
    in the trust of the person and all other persons who are associated
    with the person is more than half of the total value of all
    interests in the trust.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Association with third person

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, a person is associated
  with another person if each of them is associated with the same third
  person.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 6
- 2010, c. 25, s. 108
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-6-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 6"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 1]{.HLabel1}[Application and Administrative Matters]{.HTitleText1} {#h-178973 .Part}

### [Application to Her Majesty]{.HTitleText2} {#h-178974 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Act binding on Her Majesty

**[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** This Act is binding on Her
Majesty and Her Majesty in right of a province.

### [Administration and Officers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-178977 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister's duty

**[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** The Minister shall
administer and enforce this Act and the Commissioner may exercise the
powers and perform the duties of the Minister under this Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Officers and employees

- **[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  officers, employees and agents that are necessary to administer and
  enforce this Act shall be appointed, employed or engaged in the manner
  authorized by law.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delegation of powers

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may authorize a designated officer or
  agent or a class of officers or agents to exercise powers or perform
  duties of the Minister, including any judicial or quasi-judicial power
  or duty of the Minister, under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorization

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister of Public Safety and Emergency
  Preparedness may authorize a designated officer or agent, or a class
  of officers or agents, to exercise powers and perform duties of that
  Minister under section 68.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 9
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 93, 145
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-9-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 9"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation of police forces

- **[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister and the Minister of Public Safety and Emergency Preparedness
  may designate any police force in Canada for the purposes of the
  enforcement of any of the provisions of this Act that are specified in
  the designation, subject to any terms and conditions specified in the
  designation, for any period specified in the designation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Persons to have powers and duties of
  officers

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} All members of a police force designated under
  subsection (1) have the powers and duties of an officer for the
  purposes of the enforcement of the provisions of this Act specified in
  the designation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation to be published

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A designation under subsection (1) or any variation
  or cancellation of that designation must be published in the [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) and is not effective before it is
  so published.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 10
- 2005, c. 10, s. 34
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 10"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation of analysts

**[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may
designate any person or class of persons as an analyst for the purposes
of this Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Administration of oaths

**[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** Any officer, if
designated by the Minister for the purpose, may administer oaths and
take and receive affidavits, declarations and affirmations for the
purposes of or incidental to the administration or enforcement of this
Act, and every officer so designated has for those purposes all the
powers of a commissioner for administering oaths or taking affidavits.

### [Inquiries]{.HTitleText2} {#h-179002 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorization of inquiry

- **[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may, for any purpose related to the administration or
  enforcement of this Act, authorize any person, whether or not the
  person is an officer, to make any inquiry that the Minister considers
  necessary with reference to anything relating to the administration or
  enforcement of this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appointment of hearing officer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister authorizes a person to make an
  inquiry, the Minister shall without delay apply to the Tax Court for
  an order appointing a hearing officer before whom the inquiry will be
  held.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of hearing officer

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of an inquiry, a hearing officer has
  all of the powers conferred on a commissioner under sections 4 and 5
  of the [Inquiries Act](/eng/acts/I-11) and that may be conferred on a
  commissioner under section 11 of that Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When powers to be exercised

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A hearing officer shall exercise the powers conferred
  on a commissioner under section 4 of the [Inquiries
  Act](/eng/acts/I-11) in relation to any person that the person
  authorized to make the inquiry considers appropriate for the conduct
  of the inquiry, but the hearing officer shall not exercise the power
  to punish any person unless, on application by the hearing officer, a
  judge certifies that the power may be exercised in the matter
  disclosed in the application and the applicant has given to the person
  in respect of whom the power is proposed to be exercised 24 hours
  notice of the hearing of the application, or any shorter notice that
  the judge considers reasonable.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rights of witnesses

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Any person who gives evidence in an inquiry is
  entitled to be represented by counsel and, on request made by the
  person to the Minister, to receive a transcript of that evidence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rights of person investigated

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Any person whose affairs are investigated in the
  course of an inquiry is entitled to be present and to be represented
  by counsel throughout the inquiry unless the hearing officer, on
  application by the Minister or a person giving evidence, orders
  otherwise in relation to the whole or any part of the inquiry, on the
  ground that the presence of the person and their counsel, or either of
  them, would be prejudicial to the effective conduct of the inquiry.

## [PART 2]{.HLabel1}[Licences and Registrations]{.HTitleText1} {#h-179016 .Part}

### [Licences]{.HTitleText2} {#h-179017 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuance

- **[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, on
  application, the Minister may issue to a person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a spirits licence, authorizing the person to
    produce or package spirits;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a wine licence, authorizing the person to produce
    or package wine;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a user's licence, authorizing the person to use
    bulk alcohol, non-duty-paid packaged alcohol or a restricted
    formulation;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a tobacco licence, authorizing the person to
    manufacture tobacco products;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a tobacco dealer's licence, authorizing the person
    to carry on the activity of a tobacco dealer; or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a vaping product licence, authorizing the person to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} manufacture vaping products, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} import packaged vaping products for stamping by
      the person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cannabis licence

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, on application, the
  Minister may issue to a person a cannabis licence for the purposes of
  this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cannabis licence --- effect

  [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} A cannabis licence issued to a person shall not
  have effect before a licence or permit issued to the person under
  subsection 62(1) of the [Cannabis Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5) comes into
  effect.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed packaging excluded

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person is not entitled to a licence under paragraph
  (1)(a) or (b) by reason only of having been deemed to have packaged
  alcohol under section 77 or 82.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Production excluded

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person is not entitled to a licence under paragraph
  (1)(a) by reason only of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} having been deemed to have produced spirits under
    section 131.2; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} having produced spirits for the purpose or as a
    consequence of the analysis of the composition of a substance
    containing absolute ethyl alcohol.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuance of a wine licence

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, on application by a
  person who is the holder of a spirits licence and a user's licence,
  the Minister may issue to the person a wine licence, authorizing the
  person to fortify wine.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 14
- 2007, c. 18, s. 68
- 2018, c. 12, s. 71
- [2022, c. 10, s. 56](#1356380-1366743){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356380-1366743.html"
  role="button"}
- [2024, c. 15, s. 145](#1462436-1484168){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1462436-1484168.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-14-20220623.html "Link to previous version of section 14"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Registrations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-179040 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ferment-on-premises registration

**[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** Subject to the
regulations, on application, the Minister may issue a
ferment-on-premises registration to a person authorizing the person to
possess at their ferment-on-premises facility bulk wine produced at the
premises by an individual and owned by the individual.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}User's registration

**[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** Subject to the
regulations, on application, the Minister may issue a user's
registration authorizing the use of non-duty-paid packaged spirits by

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a scientific and research laboratory in receipt
  annually of aid from the Government of Canada or a province, for
  scientific purposes;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a university or other post-secondary educational
  institution recognized by a province, for scientific purposes;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a health care facility, for medicinal and scientific
  purposes; or

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} a health institution in receipt annually of aid from
  the Government of Canada or a province, for medicinal and scientific
  purposes.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alcohol registration

**[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** Subject to the
regulations, on application, the Minister may issue an alcohol
registration to a person authorizing the person to store or transport
bulk alcohol, specially denatured alcohol or a restricted formulation.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 17
- 2007, c. 18, s. 69
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-17-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 17"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}SDA registration

- **[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, on
  application, the Minister may issue a specially denatured alcohol
  registration to a person authorizing the person to possess and use
  specially denatured alcohol.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restrictions on grades of SDA

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may impose restrictions on the use of
  particular grades of specially denatured alcohol.

### [Excise Warehouses]{.HTitleText2} {#h-179058 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuance of licence

- **[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, on
  application, the Minister may issue an excise warehouse licence to a
  person that is not a retailer of alcohol authorizing the person to
  possess in their excise warehouse manufactured tobacco, cigars or
  vaping products that are not stamped or non-duty-paid packaged
  alcohol.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Eligible retailers of alcohol

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Even if they are retailers of alcohol, the following
  persons may be issued an excise warehouse licence under subsection
  (1):

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an alcohol licensee;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a liquor authority; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any person who supplies goods in accordance with
    the [Ships' Stores Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-40).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 19
- 2007, c. 18, s. 70
- [2022, c. 10, s. 57](#1356382-1366745){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356382-1366745.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-19-20070622.html "Link to previous version of section 19"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Special Excise Warehouses]{.HTitleText2} {#h-179069 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuance of licence

- **[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, on
  application, the Minister may issue a special excise warehouse licence
  to a person who is authorized by a tobacco licensee to be the only
  person, other than the licensee, who is entitled to distribute to an
  accredited representative manufactured tobacco or cigars manufactured
  by the licensee.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Single licence

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall not issue to the same person more
  than one special excise warehouse licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Licence limited to one premises

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall not specify more than one premises
  of a special excise warehouse licensee as a special excise warehouse.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 20
- 2007, c. 18, s. 71
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-20-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 20"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of tobacco

- **[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  person ceases to be authorized by a tobacco licensee to distribute to
  an accredited representative manufactured tobacco or cigars
  manufactured by the tobacco licensee,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person shall immediately return the tobacco or
    cigars of that licensee that are stored in the person's special
    excise warehouse to the excise warehouse of the tobacco licensee;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the tobacco licensee shall immediately notify the
    Minister in writing that the person has ceased to be so authorized.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall cancel the special excise
  warehouse licence of the person if the person is no longer authorized
  by any tobacco licensee to distribute to an accredited representative
  manufactured tobacco or cigars.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 21
- 2007, c. 18, s. 72
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-21-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 21"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Duty Free Shops]{.HTitleText2} {#h-179088 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuance of licence

**[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22 .sectionLabel}** Subject to the
regulations, the Minister may, on application, issue to a person who is
licensed under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) to operate a duty
free shop a licence authorizing the person to possess and sell imported
manufactured tobacco that is subject to a special duty under section 53.

### [General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-179091 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal to issue licence or registration

- **[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may refuse to issue a licence or registration to a person if
  the Minister has reason to believe

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that access to the person's premises will be denied
    or impeded by any person; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that the refusal is otherwise in the public
    interest.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendment or renewal

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, the Minister may amend,
  suspend, renew, cancel or reinstate any licence or registration.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation, etc. --- access to
  premises

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may amend, suspend or cancel any
  licence or registration of a person if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} access to the premises of the licensee or
    registrant is denied or impeded by any person;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a cannabis licence, a licence or
    permit issued to the person under subsection 62(1) of the [Cannabis
    Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5) is amended, suspended or revoked; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is otherwise in the public interest.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions imposed by Minister

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On issuing a licence or registration, or at any later
  time, the Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} may, subject to the regulations, specify the
    activities that may be carried on under the licence or registration
    and the premises where those activities may be carried on;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} shall, in the case of a spirits licence, a tobacco
    licence, a cannabis licence or a vaping product licence, require
    security in a form satisfactory to the Minister and in an amount
    determined in accordance with the regulations; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} may impose any other conditions that the Minister
    considers appropriate with respect to the carrying on of activities
    under the licence or registration.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 23
- 2008, c. 28, s. 52
- 2018, c. 12, s. 72
- [2022, c. 10, s. 58](#1356384-1366747){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356384-1366747.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-23-20180621.html "Link to previous version of section 23"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance with Act

**[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24 .sectionLabel}** A licensee or registrant
shall not carry on any activity under their licence or registration
otherwise than in accordance with this Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Licences and registrations not statutory
instruments

**[[24.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24.1 .sectionLabel}** For greater
certainty, a licence or registration issued under this Act is not a
statutory instrument for the purposes of the [Statutory Instruments
Act](/eng/acts/S-22).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 18, s. 73
:::

## [PART 3]{.HLabel1}[Tobacco]{.HTitleText1} {#h-179117 .Part}

### [Regulation of Tobacco]{.HTitleText2} {#h-179118 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manufacturing tobacco product without a
licence prohibited

- **[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall, other than in accordance with a tobacco licence issued
  to the person, manufacture a tobacco product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed manufacturer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person who, whether for consideration or otherwise,
  provides or offers to provide in their place of business equipment for
  use in that place by another person in the manufacture of a tobacco
  product is deemed to be manufacturing the tobacco product and the
  other person is deemed not to be manufacturing the tobacco product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- manufacturing for
  personal use

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An individual who is not a tobacco licensee may
  manufacture manufactured tobacco or cigars

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} from packaged raw leaf tobacco or manufactured
    tobacco on which the duty has been paid, if the tobacco or cigars
    are for their personal use; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} from raw leaf tobacco grown on land on which the
    individual resides, if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the tobacco or cigars are for their personal use
      or that of the members of their family who reside with the
      individual and who are 18 years of age or older, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of tobacco or cigars manufactured
      in any year does not exceed 15 kg for the individual and each
      member of the individual's family who resides with the individual
      and who is 18 years of age or older.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 25
- 2007, c. 18, s. 74
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-25-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 25"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuance of excise stamps

- **[[25.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On
  application in the prescribed form and manner, the Minister may issue,
  to a tobacco licensee or a prescribed person who is importing tobacco
  products, stamps the purpose of which is to indicate that duty, other
  than special duty, has been paid on a tobacco product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quantity of tobacco excise stamps

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may limit the quantity of tobacco excise
  stamps that may be issued to a person under subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person shall be issued a tobacco excise stamp
  unless the person has provided security in a form satisfactory to the
  Minister and in an amount determined in accordance with the
  regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Supply of tobacco excise stamps

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may authorize a producer of tobacco
  excise stamps to supply, on the direction of the Minister, tobacco
  excise stamps to a person who is issued those stamps under subsection
  (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Design and construction

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The design and construction of tobacco excise stamps
  shall be subject to the approval of the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2010, c. 12, s. 40
- 2018, c. 12, s. 94
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-25.1-20100712.html "Link to previous version of section 25.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Counterfeiting tobacco excise stamps

**[[25.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25.2 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
produce, possess, sell or otherwise supply, or offer to supply, without
lawful justification or excuse the proof of which lies on the person,
anything that is intended to resemble or pass for a tobacco excise
stamp.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2010, c. 12, s. 40
- 2018, c. 12, s. 94
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-25.2-20100712.html "Link to previous version of section 25.2"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unlawful possession of tobacco excise
stamps

- **[[25.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25.3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall possess a tobacco excise stamp that has not been affixed
  to a tobacco product or its container in the manner prescribed for the
  purposes of the definition [[stamped]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in
  section 2 to indicate that duty, other than special duty, has been
  paid on the product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions --- possession

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to the possession of a
  tobacco excise stamp by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person who lawfully produced the tobacco excise
    stamp;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person who is issued the tobacco excise stamp;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a sufferance warehouse licensee who possesses the
    tobacco excise stamp in their sufferance warehouse on behalf of a
    person described under paragraph (b); or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed person.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2010, c. 12, s. 40
- 2018, c. 12, s. 94
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-25.3-20100712.html "Link to previous version of section 25.3"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unlawful supply of tobacco excise stamps

**[[25.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25.4 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
dispose of, sell or otherwise supply, or offer to supply, a tobacco
excise stamp otherwise than in accordance with this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2010, c. 12, s. 40
- 2018, c. 12, s. 94
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-25.4-20100712.html "Link to previous version of section 25.4"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation, return and destruction of
tobacco excise stamps

**[[25.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25.5 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} cancel a tobacco excise stamp that has been issued;
  and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} direct that it be returned or destroyed in a manner
  specified by the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2010, c. 12, s. 40
- 2018, c. 12, s. 94
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-25.5-20100712.html "Link to previous version of section 25.5"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tobacco dealer

**[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** No person shall carry on
the activity of a tobacco dealer except in accordance with a tobacco
dealer's licence issued to the person.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unlawful packaging or stamping

**[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27 .sectionLabel}** No person shall package
or stamp any raw leaf tobacco or tobacco product unless the person

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} is a tobacco licensee; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} is the importer or owner of the tobacco or product
  and it has been placed in a sufferance warehouse for the purpose of
  being stamped.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unlawful removal

- **[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except
  as permitted under section 40, no person shall remove raw leaf tobacco
  or a tobacco product from the premises of a tobacco licensee unless
  the tobacco or product is packaged and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the product is intended for the duty-paid
    market, it is stamped; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the product is not intended for the duty-paid
    market, all tobacco markings that are required under this Act to be
    printed on or affixed to its container are so printed or affixed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a tobacco licensee
  who removes from their premises

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} raw leaf tobacco for

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} return to a licensed tobacco dealer or a tobacco
      grower,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} delivery to another tobacco licensee, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} export; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} partially manufactured tobacco for delivery to
    another tobacco licensee or for export.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 28
- 2007, c. 18, s. 75
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-28-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 28"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unlawful removal from premises of tobacco
dealer

- **[[28.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall remove raw leaf tobacco from the premises of a licensed
  tobacco dealer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a licensed tobacco
  dealer who removes from their premises raw leaf tobacco for

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} return to a tobacco grower;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} delivery to a tobacco licensee or to another
    licensed tobacco dealer; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} export.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 18, s. 76
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- certain tobacco products
for sale, etc.

**[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** No person shall purchase
or receive for sale a tobacco product

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} from a manufacturer who the person knows, or ought to
  know, is not a tobacco licensee;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} that is required under this Act to be packaged and
  stamped unless it is so packaged and stamped; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} that the person knows, or ought to know, is
  fraudulently stamped.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Selling, etc., unstamped raw leaf tobacco

- **[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall dispose of, sell, offer for sale, purchase or have in
  their possession raw leaf tobacco unless the tobacco is packaged and
  stamped.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a person who is a tobacco licensee or a licensed
    tobacco dealer; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the possession of raw leaf tobacco

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in a customs bonded warehouse or a sufferance
      warehouse by the licensee of that warehouse,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} by a body established under provincial law for
      the marketing of raw leaf tobacco grown in the province, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} by a prescribed person who is transporting the
      tobacco under prescribed circumstances and conditions.

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2007, c. 18, s. 77\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 30
- 2007, c. 18, s. 77
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-30-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 30"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions to sections 26 and 30

**[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31 .sectionLabel}** A tobacco grower does not
contravene section 26 or 30 by reason only that the grower deals in or
has in their possession raw leaf tobacco

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} grown by the grower on their property for sale to a
  tobacco licensee or a licensed tobacco dealer or for other disposition
  to a tobacco licensee, if the tobacco is either on the grower's
  property or is being transported by the grower

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in connection with the curing of the tobacco,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for delivery to or return from a tobacco licensee
    or a licensed tobacco dealer, or

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} for delivery to or return from a body established
    under provincial law for the marketing of raw leaf tobacco grown in
    the province;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} grown by any other person, if the grower operates a
  tobacco drying kiln on the grower's property and the grower's
  possession is solely for the purpose of curing the tobacco and
  returning it to the other person immediately after completion of the
  curing process or exporting it in accordance with paragraph (c); or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} that is to be exported, if the grower has the written
  approval of the Minister and complies with any conditions that the
  Minister considers appropriate.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 31
- 2007, c. 18, s. 78
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-31-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 31"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unlawful possession or sale of tobacco
products

- **[[32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall sell, offer for sale or have in their possession a
  tobacco product unless it is stamped.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions --- possession

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to the possession of a
  tobacco product by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a tobacco licensee at the place of manufacture of
    the product;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} in the case of manufactured tobacco or cigars, a
    tobacco licensee who manufactured the tobacco or cigars, at their
    excise warehouse;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of cigars or imported manufactured
    tobacco, an excise warehouse licensee at the excise warehouse of the
    licensee;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of manufactured tobacco or cigars, a
    special excise warehouse licensee at the special excise warehouse of
    the licensee, if the licensee is permitted under this Act to
    distribute the tobacco or cigars;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an imported tobacco product, a
    prescribed person who is transporting the product under prescribed
    circumstances and conditions;

  - [(d.1)]{.lawlabel} in the case of manufactured tobacco or cigars
    manufactured in Canada, a prescribed person who is transporting the
    tobacco or cigars under prescribed circumstances and conditions;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an imported tobacco product, a
    sufferance warehouse licensee in their sufferance warehouse;

  - [(e.1)]{.lawlabel} in the case of imported manufactured tobacco or
    cigars, a customs bonded warehouse licensee in their customs bonded
    warehouse;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in the case of cigars, a duty free shop licensee in
    their duty free shop;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} in the case of imported manufactured tobacco, a
    duty free shop licensee in their duty free shop if the licensee
    holds a licence issued under section 22;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} in the case of manufactured tobacco or cigars, an
    accredited representative for their personal or official use;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of cigars or imported manufactured
    tobacco, a person as ships' stores if the acquisition and possession
    of the product by that person are in accordance with the [Ships'
    Stores Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-40);

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} an individual who has imported the product for
    their personal use in quantities not in excess of prescribed limits;
    or

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} in the case of manufactured tobacco or cigars, an
    individual who has manufactured the tobacco or cigars in accordance
    with subsection 25(3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions --- sale or offer for sale

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a tobacco licensee sells or offers to sell
    manufactured tobacco or cigars that are exported by the licensee in
    accordance with this Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a tobacco licensee sells or offers to sell

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} manufactured tobacco or cigars to a special
      excise warehouse licensee, if the special excise warehouse
      licensee is permitted under this Act to distribute the tobacco or
      cigars,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} manufactured tobacco or cigars to an accredited
      representative for their personal or official use,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} cigars to an excise warehouse licensee for
      delivery as ships' stores in accordance with the [Ships' Stores
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-40),

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} cigars to a duty free shop for sale or offer for
      sale in accordance with the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), or

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} cigars as ships' stores in accordance with the
      [Ships' Stores Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-40);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a special excise warehouse licensee sells or offers
    to sell manufactured tobacco or cigars to an accredited
    representative for their personal or official use, if the licensee
    is permitted under this Act to distribute the tobacco or cigars;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an excise warehouse licensee sells or offers to
    sell

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} imported manufactured tobacco or cigars that are
      exported by the licensee in accordance with this Act,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} imported manufactured tobacco or cigars to an
      accredited representative for their personal or official use or to
      a duty free shop, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} cigars or imported manufactured tobacco as
      ships' stores in accordance with the [Ships' Stores
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-40);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a duty free shop licensee sells or offers to sell
    cigars in accordance with the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6);

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a duty free shop licensee who holds a licence
    issued under section 22 sells or offers to sell imported
    manufactured tobacco in accordance with the [Customs
    Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6);

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a customs bonded warehouse licensee sells or offers
    to sell imported manufactured tobacco or cigars that are exported by
    the licensee in accordance with this Act;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a customs bonded warehouse licensee sells or offers
    to sell imported manufactured tobacco or cigars

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to an accredited representative for their
      personal or official use,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to a duty free shop for sale or offer for sale
      in accordance with the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} as ships' stores in accordance with the [Ships'
      Stores Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-40); and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a person sells or offers for sale cigars or
    imported manufactured tobacco as ships' stores in accordance with
    the [Ships' Stores Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-40).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 32
- 2007, c. 18, s. 79
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-32-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 32"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- possession of tobacco
manufacturing equipment

- **[[32.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall possess tobacco manufacturing equipment with the intent
  of manufacturing a tobacco product unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person is a tobacco licensee; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person is an individual manufacturing
    manufactured tobacco or cigars for their personal use as permitted
    under subsection 25(3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- importation of tobacco
  manufacturing equipment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall import tobacco manufacturing
  equipment unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person is a tobacco licensee;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the equipment is designed for use by an individual
    manufacturing manufactured tobacco or cigars for their personal use
    as permitted under subsection 25(3) and is not designed for
    commercial manufacturing;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person provides evidence satisfactory to the
    Minister of Public Safety and Emergency Preparedness that the
    equipment is imported

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} on a tobacco licensee's behalf,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for the sole purpose of maintenance, alteration
      or repair in Canada, if the equipment is intended to be exported
      immediately after the maintenance, alteration or repair is
      completed,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} by or on behalf of a person who carries on a
      business of supplying that equipment, or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} for in-transit movement through Canada; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} it is imported in prescribed circumstances or under
    prescribed terms and conditions.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2008, c. 28, s. 53
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No sale or distribution except in
original package

**[[33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33 .sectionLabel}** No person shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} sell or offer for sale cigars otherwise than in or
  from the original package;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} sell or offer for sale manufactured tobacco otherwise
  than in the original package; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} distribute free of charge for advertising purposes
  any tobacco product otherwise than in or from the original package.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Packaging and stamping of tobacco

**[[34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-34 .sectionLabel}** A tobacco licensee who
manufactures a tobacco product shall not enter the tobacco product into
the duty-paid market unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the product has been packaged by the licensee;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the package has printed on it prescribed information;
  and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the product is stamped at the time of packaging.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Packaging and stamping of imported
tobacco

- **[[35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  tobacco product or raw leaf tobacco is imported, it must, before it is
  released under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) for entry into the
  duty-paid market,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be packaged in a package that has printed on it
    prescribed information; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} be stamped.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception for certain importations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} partially manufactured tobacco that is imported by
    a tobacco licensee for further manufacturing by the licensee;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} manufactured tobacco or cigars that a tobacco
    licensee is authorized to import under subsection 41(2);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a tobacco product that is imported by an individual
    for their personal use in quantities not in excess of prescribed
    limits; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} raw leaf tobacco that is imported by a tobacco
    licensee or a licensed tobacco dealer.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 35
- 2007, c. 18, s. 80
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-35-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 35"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Absence of stamping --- notice

**[[36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36 .sectionLabel}** The absence of stamping
on a tobacco product is notice to all persons that duty has not been
paid on the product.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unstamped products to be warehoused

**[[37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37 .sectionLabel}** If manufactured tobacco
or cigars manufactured in Canada are not stamped by a tobacco licensee,
the tobacco licensee shall immediately enter the tobacco or cigars into
the licensee's excise warehouse.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 37
- 2007, c. 18, s. 81
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-37-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 37"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No warehousing of tobacco without
markings

- **[[38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (3) and (4),
  no person shall enter into an excise warehouse a container of
  manufactured tobacco or cigars unless the container has printed on it
  or affixed to it tobacco markings and other prescribed information.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No delivery of imported tobacco without
  markings

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2.1) and (3), no person shall
  deliver a container of imported manufactured tobacco or cigars that
  does not have printed on it or affixed to it tobacco markings and
  other prescribed information to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a duty free shop for sale or offer for sale in
    accordance with the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an accredited representative; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a customs bonded warehouse.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delivery of imported stamped tobacco

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} A container of imported manufactured tobacco that
  was manufactured outside Canada and is stamped may be delivered to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a duty free shop for sale or offer for sale in
    accordance with the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a customs bonded warehouse.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception for specified manufactured
  tobacco brands

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A container of manufactured tobacco does not require
  tobacco markings to be printed on or affixed to it if the brand of the
  tobacco is not commonly sold in Canada and is specified by the
  Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception for prescribed cigarettes

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A container of cigarettes does not require tobacco
  markings to be printed on or affixed to it if the cigarettes are of a
  particular type or formulation manufactured in Canada and exported
  under a brand that is also applied to cigarettes of a different type
  or formulation that are manufactured and sold in Canada and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} cigarettes of the particular type or formulation
    exported under that brand are cigarettes specified by the Minister;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} cigarettes of the particular type or formulation
    have never been sold in Canada under that brand or any other brand.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Distinguishing different cigarettes

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subsection (4), a cigarette of a
  particular type or formulation sold under a brand may be considered to
  be different from another cigarette sold under that brand if it is
  reasonable to consider them to be different having regard to their
  physical characteristics before and during consumption.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 38
- 2007, c. 18, s. 82
- 2008, c. 28, s. 54
- [2024, c. 17, s. 117](#1467261-1485750){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1467261-1485750.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-38-20080618.html "Link to previous version of section 38"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-compliant imports

**[[39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-39 .sectionLabel}** If an imported tobacco
product or imported raw leaf tobacco intended for the duty-paid market
is not stamped when it is reported under the [Customs
Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), it shall be placed in a sufferance warehouse for
the purpose of being stamped.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal of raw leaf tobacco or waste
tobacco

- **[[40]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-40 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person other than a tobacco licensee shall remove raw leaf tobacco or
  tobacco that is waste from the premises of the licensee.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal requirements

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If raw leaf tobacco or tobacco that is waste is
  removed from the premises of a tobacco licensee, the licensee shall
  deal with the tobacco in the manner authorized by the Minister.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Re-working or destruction of tobacco

- **[[41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  tobacco licensee may re-work or destroy a tobacco product in the
  manner authorized by the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Importation for re-working or
  destruction

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may authorize a tobacco licensee to
  import any manufactured tobacco or cigars manufactured in Canada by
  the licensee for re-working or destruction by the licensee in
  accordance with subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 41
- 2007, c. 18, s. 83
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-41-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 41"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Duty on Tobacco]{.HTitleText2} {#h-179353 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imposition

- **[[42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Duty
  is imposed on tobacco products manufactured in Canada or imported and
  on imported raw leaf tobacco at the rates set out in Schedule 1 and is
  payable

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of tobacco products manufactured in
    Canada, by the tobacco licensee who manufactured the tobacco
    products, at the time they are packaged; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of imported tobacco products or raw
    leaf tobacco, by the importer, owner or other person who is liable
    under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) to pay duty levied under
    section 20 of the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) or who would
    be liable to pay that duty on the tobacco or products if they were
    subject to that duty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imported partially manufactured tobacco

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The following rules apply to partially manufactured
  tobacco that is imported by a tobacco licensee for further
  manufacture:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of this Act, the tobacco is deemed
    to be manufactured in Canada by the licensee; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} paragraph (1)(a) applies to the tobacco and
    paragraph (1)(b) and section 44 do not apply.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 42
- 2014, c. 20, s. 62(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-42-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 42"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional duty on cigars

**[[43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43 .sectionLabel}** In addition to the duty
imposed under section 42, duty is imposed on cigars at the rates set out
in Schedule 2 and is payable

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of cigars manufactured and sold in
  Canada, by the tobacco licensee who manufactured the cigars, at the
  time of their delivery to a purchaser; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of imported cigars, by the importer,
  owner or other person who is liable under the [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) to pay duty levied under section 20 of the
  [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) or who would be liable to pay
  that duty on the cigars if they were subject to that duty.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 43
- 2014, c. 20, s. 63(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-43-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 43"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[inflationary adjusted
year]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[43.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section, [[inflationary adjusted year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
  2019 and every year after that year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Annual adjustments

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Each rate of duty set out in sections 1 to 4 of
  Schedule 1 and paragraph (a) of Schedule 2 in respect of a tobacco
  product is to be adjusted on April 1 of an inflationary adjusted year
  so that the rate is equal to the greater of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the rate determined by the formula

    ::: {#179377 .Paragraph}
    A × B

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the rate of duty applicable to the tobacco product on March
        31 of the inflationary adjusted year, and

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is the amount, rounded to the nearest one-thousandth, or, if the
        amount is equidistant from two consecutive one-thousandths,
        rounded to the higher one-thousandth, determined by the formula
        ::: {#179381 .NestedFormula}
        C/D

        where

        [C]{.dfn}
        :   is the Consumer Price Index for the 12-month period ending
            on September 30 of the particular year preceding the
            inflationary adjusted year, and

        [D]{.dfn}
        :   is the Consumer Price Index for the 12-month period ending
            on September 30 of the year preceding the particular year;
            and
        :::
    :::

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the rate of duty referred to in the description of
    A in paragraph (a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The adjusted rate determined under subsection (2) is
  to be rounded to the nearest one-hundred-thousandth or, if the
  adjusted rate is equidistant from two consecutive
  one-hundred-thousandths, to the higher one-hundred-thousandth.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consumer Price Index

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In this section, the Consumer Price Index for any
  12-month period is the result arrived at by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} aggregating the Consumer Price Index for Canada, as
    published by Statistics Canada under the authority of the
    [Statistics Act](/eng/acts/S-19), for each month in that period;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} dividing the aggregate obtained under paragraph (a)
    by 12; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} rounding the result obtained under paragraph (b) to
    the nearest one-thousandth or, if the result obtained is equidistant
    from two consecutive one-thousandths, to the higher one-thousandth.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 20, s. 64
- 2018, c. 12, s. 47
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-43.1-20140619.html "Link to previous version of section 43.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of [Customs
Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6)

**[[44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-44 .sectionLabel}** The duties imposed under
sections 42 and 43 on imported raw leaf tobacco and tobacco products
shall be paid and collected under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6),
and interest and penalties shall be imposed, calculated, paid and
collected under that Act, as if the duties were a duty levied under
section 20 of the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011), and, for those
purposes, the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) applies with any
modifications that the circumstances require.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty relieved

- **[[45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  duties imposed under sections 42 and 43 are relieved on a tobacco
  product that is not stamped.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tobacco imported by an individual for
  personal use

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to the importation of
  tobacco products by an individual for their personal use to the extent
  that the quantity of the products imported exceeds the quantity
  permitted under Chapter 98 of the List of Tariff Provisions set out in
  the schedule to the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) to be
  imported without the payment of duties (as defined in Note 4 to that
  Chapter).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty relieved --- raw leaf tobacco

**[[46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46 .sectionLabel}** The duty imposed under
section 42 is relieved on raw leaf tobacco that is imported by a tobacco
licensee or a licensed tobacco dealer.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 46
- 2007, c. 18, s. 84
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-46-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 46"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty relieved --- stamped tobacco
imported by individual

- **[[47]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  duty imposed under section 42 is relieved on manufactured tobacco
  imported by an individual for their personal use if it was
  manufactured in Canada and is stamped.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty relieved --- reimportation of
  stamped tobacco by individual

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The duty imposed under section 42 is relieved on
  manufactured tobacco imported by an individual for their personal use
  if it was manufactured outside Canada, was previously imported into
  Canada and is stamped.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 47
- 2008, c. 28, s. 55
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-47-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 47"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty relieved --- importation for
destruction

**[[48]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-48 .sectionLabel}** The duty imposed under
paragraph 42(1)(b) is relieved on stamped manufactured tobacco that was
manufactured in Canada by a tobacco licensee and that is imported by the
licensee for re-working or destruction in accordance with section 41.

### [Excise Warehouses]{.HTitleText2} {#h-179415 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction --- entering tobacco

**[[49]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49 .sectionLabel}** No person shall enter
into an excise warehouse

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a tobacco product that is stamped; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any other tobacco product except in accordance with
  this Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[50]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-50 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  definitions in this subsection apply in this section.

  [[Canadian manufactured tobacco]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Canadian manufactured tobacco]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      manufactured tobacco that is manufactured in Canada but does not
      include partially manufactured tobacco or foreign brand
      tobacco. ([tabac fabriqué canadien]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[foreign brand tobacco]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[foreign brand tobacco]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means manufactured
      tobacco in respect of which the special duty imposed under section
      56 is relieved because of section 58. ([tabac de marque
      étrangère]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Categories of Canadian manufactured
  tobacco

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (5), each of the
  following constitutes a category of Canadian manufactured tobacco:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} cigarettes;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} tobacco sticks; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} manufactured tobacco other than cigarettes and
    tobacco sticks.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition on removal

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person shall remove from an excise warehouse or a
  special excise warehouse manufactured tobacco or cigars manufactured
  in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception for Canadian manufactured
  tobacco

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, Canadian manufactured
  tobacco may be removed from the excise warehouse of the tobacco
  licensee who manufactured it only if it is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for export by the licensee in accordance with
    subsection (5) and not for delivery to a foreign duty free shop or
    as foreign ships' stores;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for delivery to the special excise warehouse of a
    special excise warehouse licensee, if the special excise warehouse
    licensee is permitted under this Act to distribute the Canadian
    manufactured tobacco; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for delivery to an accredited representative for
    their personal or official use.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum quantity permitted to be
  exported from excise warehouse

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A tobacco licensee shall not, at a particular time in
  a calendar year, remove a particular quantity of a category of
  Canadian manufactured tobacco from the licensee's excise warehouse for
  export if the total quantity of that category removed in the year up
  to that time by the licensee from the warehouse for export, plus the
  particular quantity, exceeds 1.5% of the total quantity of that
  category manufactured by the licensee in the preceding calendar year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quantities to be excluded for the
  purposes of subsection (5)

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} In subsection (5), the total quantity of a category
  of Canadian manufactured tobacco manufactured by a licensee in the
  preceding calendar year does not include any quantity of that category
  that was exported by the licensee for delivery to a foreign duty free
  shop or as foreign ships' stores.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception for cigars

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, cigars manufactured in
  Canada may be removed from the excise warehouse of the tobacco
  licensee who manufactured them only if they are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for export by the licensee in accordance with this
    Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for delivery to the special excise warehouse of a
    special excise warehouse licensee, if the special excise warehouse
    licensee is permitted under this Act to distribute the cigars;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for delivery to an accredited representative for
    their personal or official use;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} for delivery as ships' stores in accordance with
    the [Ships' Stores Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-40);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} for delivery to another excise warehouse, if the
    excise warehouse licensee of the other excise warehouse certifies in
    the prescribed form to the tobacco licensee that the cigars are for
    delivery as ships' stores in accordance with the [Ships' Stores
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-40); or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} for delivery to a duty free shop for sale or offer
    for sale in accordance with the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception for partially manufactured
  tobacco or foreign brand tobacco

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, partially manufactured
  tobacco or foreign brand tobacco may be removed from the excise
  warehouse of the tobacco licensee who manufactured it only if it is
  exported by the licensee and not for delivery to a foreign duty free
  shop or as foreign ships' stores.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal from warehouse --- ships'
  stores

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, cigars manufactured in
  Canada may be removed from an excise warehouse referred to in
  paragraph (7)(e) for delivery as ships' stores in accordance with the
  [Ships' Stores Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-40).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal from warehouse for re-working
  or destruction

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, manufactured tobacco or
  cigars manufactured in Canada may be removed from the excise warehouse
  of the tobacco licensee who manufactured them if they are removed for
  re-working or destruction by the licensee in accordance with section
  41.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal from special excise warehouse
  --- accredited representatives

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, Canadian manufactured
  tobacco and cigars may be removed from a special excise warehouse for
  delivery to an accredited representative for their personal or
  official use if the special excise warehouse licensee is permitted
  under this Act to distribute the tobacco or cigars.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 50
- 2007, c. 18, s. 85
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-50-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 50"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal of imported tobacco

- **[[51]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall remove imported manufactured tobacco or cigars from an
  excise warehouse.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, imported manufactured
  tobacco or cigars may be removed from an excise warehouse

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for delivery to another excise warehouse;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for delivery to an accredited representative for
    their personal or official use;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for delivery as ships' stores, in accordance with
    the [Ships' Stores Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-40);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} for delivery to a duty free shop for sale or offer
    for sale in accordance with the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6); or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} for export in accordance with this Act by the
    excise warehouse licensee.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 51
- 2007, c. 18, s. 86
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-51-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 51"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction --- special excise warehouse

**[[52]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52 .sectionLabel}** No special excise
warehouse licensee shall store manufactured tobacco or cigars that are
manufactured in Canada in their special excise warehouse for any purpose
other than the sale and distribution of the tobacco or cigars to an
accredited representative for the personal or official use of the
representative.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 52
- 2007, c. 18, s. 87
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-52-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 52"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Special Duties on Tobacco Products]{.HTitleText2} {#h-179475 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Special duty on imported manufactured
tobacco delivered to duty free shop

- **[[53]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  special duty is imposed, at the rates set out in section 1 of Schedule
  3, on imported manufactured tobacco that is delivered to a duty free
  shop and that is not stamped.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When and by whom duty is payable

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The special duty is payable at the time of delivery
  and is payable by the duty free shop licensee.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 53
- 2008, c. 28, s. 56
- 2014, c. 20, s. 65(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-53-20080618.html "Link to previous version of section 53"){.PITLink}
:::

Meaning of [[traveller's tobacco]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

- **[[54]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-54 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section, [[traveller's tobacco]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
  manufactured tobacco that is imported by a person at any time and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is classified under tariff item No. 9804.10.00,
    9804.20.00, 9805.00.00 or 9807.00.00 in the List of Tariff
    Provisions set out in the schedule to the [Customs
    Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} would be classified under tariff item No.
    9804.10.00 or 9804.20.00 in that List but for the fact that the
    total value for duty as determined under section 46 of the [Customs
    Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) of all goods imported by the person at that
    time exceeds the maximum value specified in that tariff item.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Special duty on traveller's tobacco

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A special duty is imposed on traveller's tobacco at
  the time it is imported at the rates set out in section 2 of Schedule
  3.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When and how duty is payable

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The special duty shall be paid and collected under
  the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), and interest and penalties shall
  be imposed, calculated, paid and collected under that Act, as if the
  special duty were a duty levied under section 20 of the [Customs
  Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011), and for those purposes, the [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) applies with any modifications that the
  circumstances require.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The special duty is not imposed on traveller's
  tobacco imported by an individual for their personal use if duty under
  section 42 was previously imposed on the tobacco and the tobacco is
  stamped.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 54
- 2008, c. 28, s. 57
- 2014, c. 20, s. 66(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-54-20080618.html "Link to previous version of section 54"){.PITLink}
:::

Definition of [[tobacco product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

**[[55]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-55 .sectionLabel}** In sections 56 to 58,
[[tobacco product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means manufactured tobacco other
than partially manufactured tobacco.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imposition

- **[[56]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-56 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  special duty is imposed on a tobacco product that is manufactured in
  Canada and exported

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the export is in accordance with paragraph
    50(4)(a) by the tobacco licensee who manufactured it, at the rates
    set out in section 3 of Schedule 3; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, at the rates set out in section
    4 of Schedule 3.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When and by whom duty is payable

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to sections 57 and 58, the special duty is
  payable, at the time the tobacco product is exported, by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if paragraph (1)(a) applies, the tobacco licensee
    who manufactured it; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if paragraph (1)(b) applies, the person who
    exported it.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 56
- 2014, c. 20, s. 67(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-56-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 56"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty relieved --- deliveries to a foreign
duty free shop and as foreign ships' stores

**[[57]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-57 .sectionLabel}** The special duty imposed
under section 56 is relieved on a tobacco product that is exported by
the tobacco licensee who manufactured it for delivery to a foreign duty
free shop or as foreign ships' stores.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty relieved --- prescribed tobacco
product

- **[[58]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  special duty imposed under section 56 is relieved on a tobacco product
  of a particular brand if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the tobacco product of that brand is specified by
    the Minister;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} during the three-year period before the year in
    which the tobacco product of that brand is exported, the tobacco
    product of that brand was not sold in Canada, other than in a duty
    free shop, except in quantities not significantly greater than the
    minimum quantities sufficient for the purposes of registering the
    trademark for that brand; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} during any year before the three-year period
    referred to in paragraph (b), sales in Canada of the product of that
    brand never exceeded

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} 0.5% of total sales in Canada of similar
      products, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if another percentage that is less than 0.5% is
      prescribed for the purposes of this subsection, that percentage of
      total sales in Canada of similar products.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty relieved --- prescribed cigarettes

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The special duty imposed under section 56 is relieved
  on cigarettes of a particular type or formulation manufactured in
  Canada and exported under a brand that is also applied to cigarettes
  of a different type or formulation that are manufactured and sold in
  Canada if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} cigarettes of the particular type or formulation
    exported under that brand are cigarettes specified by the Minister;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} cigarettes of the particular type or formulation
    have never been sold in Canada under that brand or any other brand.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Distinguishing different cigarettes

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subsection (2), a cigarette of a
  particular type or formulation sold under a brand may be considered to
  be different from another cigarette sold under that brand if it is
  reasonable to consider them to be different having regard to their
  physical characteristics before and during consumption.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 58
- [2014, c. 20, s. 366(E)](#1034903-1145629){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1034903-1145629.html"
  role="button"}
- [2024, c. 17, s. 118](#1467267-1485753){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1467267-1485753.html"
  role="button"}
:::

## [PART 3.1]{.HLabel1}[Cigarette Inventory Tax]{.HTitleText1} {#h-179525 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[58.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58.1 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Part.

[[adjustment day]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[adjustment day]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} March 23, 2017;

    - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} February 28, 2018;

    - [(a.2)]{.lawlabel} April 20, 2021;

    - [(a.3)]{.lawlabel} April 17, 2024; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an inflationary adjusted year,
      April 1 of that year. ([date d'ajustement]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[inflationary adjusted year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[inflationary adjusted year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
    meaning as in subsection 43.1(1). ([année
    inflationniste]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[loose tobacco]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[loose tobacco]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2014, c. 20, s.
    69\]]{.Repealed}

[[separate retail establishment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[separate retail establishment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of a person
    means a shop or store of the person

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that is geographically separate from other places
      of business of the person;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at which, in the ordinary course of the person's
      business, the person regularly sells, otherwise than through
      vending machines, tobacco products to consumers, within the
      meaning of section 123 of the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15),
      attending at the shop or store; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of which separate records are
      maintained. ([établissement de détail distinct]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[taxed cigarettes]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[taxed cigarettes]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of a person means
    cigarettes in respect of which duty has been imposed under section
    42 or 53 at the rate applicable on the day before an adjustment day,
    and that, at the beginning of the adjustment day,

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} were owned by that person for sale in the
      ordinary course of a business of the person;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} were not held in a vending machine; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} were not relieved from that duty under this
      Act. ([cigarettes imposées]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[taxed tobacco]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[taxed tobacco]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2014, c. 20, s.
    69\]]{.Repealed}

[[unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2014, c. 20, s.
    69\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 2006, c. 4, s. 34
- 2007, c. 35, s. 197
- 2014, c. 20, s. 69
- 2017, c. 20, s. 45
- 2018, c. 12, s. 48
- [2021, c. 23, s. 117](#1306921-1311702){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1306921-1311702.html"
  role="button"}
- [2024, c. 17, s. 119](#1467273-1484301){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1467273-1484301.html"
  role="button"}
:::
