[Previous
Version](section-58.1-20210629.html "Link to previous version of section 58.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imposition of tax --- 2017 increase

- **[[58.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 58.3, every
  person shall pay to Her Majesty a tax on all taxed cigarettes of the
  person held at the beginning of March 23, 2017 at the rate of
  \$0.00265 per cigarette.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imposition of tax --- 2018 increase

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 58.3, every person shall pay to
  Her Majesty a tax on all taxed cigarettes of the person held at the
  beginning of February 28, 2018 at the rate of \$0.011468 per
  cigarette.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imposition of tax --- 2021 increase

  [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 58.3, every person shall pay to
  Her Majesty a tax on all taxed cigarettes of the person held at the
  beginning of April 20, 2021 at the rate of \$0.02 per cigarette.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imposition of tax --- 2024 increase

  [(1.3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 58.3, every person shall pay to
  His Majesty a tax on all taxed cigarettes of the person held at the
  beginning of April 17, 2024 at the rate of \$0.02 per cigarette.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imposition of tax --- inflationary
  adjusted years

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 58.3, every person shall pay to
  Her Majesty a tax on all taxed cigarettes of the person held at the
  beginning of April 1 of an inflationary adjusted year at a rate per
  cigarette equal to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of cigarettes in respect of which duty
    has been imposed under section 42, the amount determined by the
    formula

    ::: {#179554 .Paragraph}
    (A -- B)/5

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the rate of duty applicable under section 1 of Schedule 1 for
        each five cigarettes on April 1 of the inflationary adjusted
        year, and

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is the rate of duty applicable under section 1 of Schedule 1 for
        each five cigarettes on March 31 of the inflationary adjusted
        year; and
    :::

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of cigarettes in respect of which duty
    has been imposed under section 53, the amount determined by the
    formula

    ::: {#179559 .Paragraph}
    C -- D

    where

    [C]{.dfn}
    :   is the rate of duty applicable under paragraph 1(a) of Schedule
        3 per cigarette on April 1 of the inflationary adjusted year,
        and

    [D]{.dfn}
    :   is the rate of duty applicable under paragraph 1(a) of Schedule
        3 per cigarette on March 31 of the inflationary adjusted year.
    :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The amount determined under paragraph (2)(a) or (b)
  is to be rounded to the nearest one-hundred-thousandth or, if the
  amount is equidistant from two consecutive one-hundred-thousandths, to
  the higher one-hundred-thousandth.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2006, c. 4, s. 34
- 2007, c. 35, s. 198
- 2014, c. 20, s. 70
- 2017, c. 20, s. 46
- 2018, c. 12, s. 49
- [2021, c. 23, s. 118](#1306925-1311705){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1306925-1311705.html"
  role="button"}
- [2024, c. 17, s. 120](#1467277-1484304){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1467277-1484304.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-58.2-20210629.html "Link to previous version of section 58.2"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption for small retail inventory

**[[58.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58.3 .sectionLabel}** Tax under this Part
in respect of the inventory of all taxed cigarettes of a person that is
held at the beginning of an adjustment day at a separate retail
establishment of the person is not payable if that retail establishment
holds inventory of 30,000 or fewer cigarettes.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2006, c. 4, s. 34
- 2007, c. 35, s. 199
- 2014, c. 20, s. 70
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-58.3-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 58.3"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Taking of inventory

**[[58.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58.4 .sectionLabel}** Every person liable
to pay tax under this Part shall, for the purposes of this Part,
determine that person's inventory of all taxed cigarettes held at the
beginning of an adjustment day.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2006, c. 4, s. 34
- 2014, c. 20, s. 70
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-58.4-20060701.html "Link to previous version of section 58.4"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Returns

- **[[58.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58.5
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person liable to pay tax under
  this Part shall file a return with the Minister in the prescribed form
  and manner on or before,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the tax imposed under subsection
    58.2(1), May 31, 2017;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the tax imposed under subsection
    58.2(1.1), April 30, 2018;

  - [(a.2)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the tax imposed under subsection
    58.2(1.2), June 30, 2021;

  - [(a.3)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the tax imposed under subsection
    58.2(1.3), June 30, 2024; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the tax imposed under subsection
    58.2(2) in respect of an inflationary adjusted year, May 31 of the
    inflationary adjusted year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Separate returns

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person authorized under subsection 239(2) of the
  [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15) to file separate returns in respect
  of a separate branch or division may file separate returns under this
  Part in respect of that branch or division.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2006, c. 4, s. 34
- 2007, c. 35, s. 200
- 2014, c. 20, s. 71
- 2017, c. 20, s. 47
- 2018, c. 12, s. 50
- [2021, c. 23, s. 119](#1306929-1311708){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1306929-1311708.html"
  role="button"}
- [2024, c. 17, s. 121](#1467281-1484307){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1467281-1484307.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-58.5-20210629.html "Link to previous version of section 58.5"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment

- **[[58.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58.6
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person shall pay to the
  Receiver General the total tax payable by the person under this Part
  on or before

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the tax imposed under subsection
    58.2(1), May 31, 2017;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the tax imposed under subsection
    58.2(1.1), April 30, 2018;

  - [(a.2)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the tax imposed under subsection
    58.2(1.2), June 30, 2021;

  - [(a.3)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the tax imposed under subsection
    58.2(1.3), June 30, 2024; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the tax imposed under subsection
    58.2(2) in respect of an inflationary adjusted year, May 31 of the
    inflationary adjusted year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minimum interest

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No interest in respect of an amount payable by a
  person under this Part is payable if, at the time the person pays the
  amount, the total of that interest otherwise payable is less than
  \$25.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may at any time extend in writing the
  time for filing a return, or paying the tax payable, under this Part
  and, where the Minister so extends the time,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the return shall be filed or tax payable shall be
    paid within the time as so extended; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} interest is payable under section 170 as if the
    time had not been extended.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2006, c. 4, s. 34
- 2007, c. 35, s. 201
- 2014, c. 20, s. 72
- 2017, c. 20, s. 48
- 2018, c. 12, s. 51
- [2021, c. 23, s. 120](#1306933-1311711){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1306933-1311711.html"
  role="button"}
- [2024, c. 17, s. 122](#1467285-1484309){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1467285-1484309.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-58.6-20210629.html "Link to previous version of section 58.6"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 4]{.HLabel1}[Alcohol]{.HTitleText1} {#h-179599 .Part}

### [General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-179600 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application --- [Importation of
Intoxicating Liquors Act](/eng/acts/I-3)

**[[59]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-59 .sectionLabel}** For greater certainty,
the [Importation of Intoxicating Liquors Act](/eng/acts/I-3) continues
to apply to the importation, sending, taking and transportation of
intoxicating liquor into a province.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Importations involving a provincial
authority

**[[59.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-59.1 .sectionLabel}** If alcohol is
imported under circumstances in which subsection 3(1) of the
[Importation of Intoxicating Liquors Act](/eng/acts/I-3) applies, the
alcohol is deemed, for the purposes of this Act and subsection 21.2(3)
of the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011), to have been imported by
the person who would have been the importer in the absence of that
subsection 3(1) and not by Her Majesty in right of a province or a
liquor authority.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 18, s. 88
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- production and packaging
of spirits

- **[[60]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-60 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall, except in accordance with a spirits licence issued to
  the person, produce or package spirits.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the packaging of spirits from a marked special
    container by a purchaser at a bottle-your-own premises; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the production of spirits for the purpose or as a
    consequence of the analysis of the composition of a substance
    containing absolute ethyl alcohol.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 60
- 2007, c. 18, s. 89
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-60-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 60"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- possession of still

**[[61]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-61 .sectionLabel}** No person shall possess a
still or other equipment suitable for the production of spirits with the
intent of producing spirits unless the person

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} is a spirits licensee;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} has a pending application for a spirits licence; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} possesses the still or equipment solely for the
  purpose of producing spirits for the purpose or as a consequence of
  the analysis of the composition of a substance containing absolute
  ethyl alcohol.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 61
- 2007, c. 18, s. 90
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-61-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 61"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- production and packaging
of wine

- **[[62]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-62 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall, except in accordance with a wine licence issued to the
  person, produce or package wine.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the production of wine by an individual for their
    personal use;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the packaging of wine referred to in paragraph (a)
    by an individual for their personal use; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the packaging of wine from a marked special
    container by a purchaser at a bottle-your-own premises.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- fortification of wine

**[[62.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-62.1 .sectionLabel}** Except as permitted
under section 130, no person shall use bulk spirits to fortify bulk
wine.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 18, s. 91
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- sale of wine produced for
personal use

**[[63]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-63 .sectionLabel}** No person shall sell or
put to a commercial use wine that was produced, or produced and
packaged, by an individual for their personal use.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Wine produced by individual

**[[64]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of this
Act, wine is not produced or packaged by an individual if it has been
produced or packaged by a person acting on their behalf.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- ferment-on-premises
facility

**[[65]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-65 .sectionLabel}** No person shall carry on
at a ferment-on-premises facility any activity specified in a licence or
registration issued under this Act other than an activity specified in a
ferment-on-premises registration.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application --- in-transit and
transhipped alcohol

**[[66]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66 .sectionLabel}** Sections 67 to 72, 75,
76, 80, 85, 88, 97 to 100 and 102 do not apply to imported alcohol or
specially denatured alcohol that is, in accordance with the [Customs
Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) and the
regulations made under those Acts,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} transported by a customs bonded carrier from a place
  outside Canada to another place outside Canada;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} stored in a customs bonded warehouse or sufferance
  warehouse for subsequent delivery to a place outside Canada; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} transported by a customs bonded carrier

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} from a place outside Canada to a customs bonded
    warehouse or a sufferance warehouse for subsequent delivery to a
    place outside Canada, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} from a customs bonded warehouse or a sufferance
    warehouse to a place outside Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 66
- 2007, c. 18, s. 92
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-66-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 66"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- sale of alcohol

**[[67]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-67 .sectionLabel}** No person shall sell

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} bulk alcohol unless it was produced or imported in
  accordance with this Act;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} packaged alcohol unless it was, in accordance with
  this Act,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} produced and packaged in Canada,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} imported and packaged in Canada, or

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} imported; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a marked special container of alcohol unless it was
  marked in accordance with this Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Availability and sampling of imported DA
and SDA

- **[[68]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-68 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  person who imports a product that is reported under the [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) as being denatured alcohol or specially
  denatured alcohol shall make the product available for sampling and
  the product is required to be sampled by the Minister of Public Safety
  and Emergency Preparedness before it is released under that Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Testing

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The samples must be tested to verify that the product
  is denatured alcohol or specially denatured alcohol.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister may waive

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister of Public Safety and Emergency
  Preparedness may at any time waive the requirement to sample an
  imported product under subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fees

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister of Public Safety and Emergency
  Preparedness may fix fees to be paid by the importer of the product
  but those fees must not exceed an amount determined by that Minister
  to be the costs to Her Majesty in respect of the sampling and testing.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 68
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 94, 145
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-68-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 68"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Bulk Alcohol]{.HTitleText2} {#h-179669 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- ownership of bulk alcohol

**[[69]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-69 .sectionLabel}** No person shall own bulk
alcohol unless it was produced or imported in accordance with this Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- possession

- **[[70]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-70 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall possess bulk alcohol.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to a spirits licensee or a licensed user who
    possesses bulk spirits that were produced or imported by a spirits
    licensee;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to a wine licensee or a licensed user who possesses
    bulk wine that was produced or imported by a wine licensee;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to a licensed user who possesses bulk alcohol that
    was imported by the licensed user;

  - [(c.1)]{.lawlabel} in the case of bulk spirits produced for the
    purpose or as a consequence of the analysis of the composition of a
    substance containing absolute ethyl alcohol, to a person who, having
    produced those spirits, possesses them during the period of
    analysis;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} to an alcohol registrant who possesses, for the
    purposes of storage or transportation, bulk alcohol that was
    produced by an alcohol licensee or imported by an alcohol licensee
    or licensed user;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} to a sufferance warehouse licensee who possesses in
    their sufferance warehouse bulk alcohol that was imported by a
    person permitted to do so under this Act;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} to a ferment-on-premises registrant who possesses
    bulk wine that was produced at the registrant's ferment-on-premises
    facility by an individual for the individual's personal use; or

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} to an individual who possesses less than 500 L of
    bulk wine that was lawfully produced at a residence or a
    ferment-on-premises facility for an individual's personal use.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 70
- 2007, c. 18, s. 93
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-70-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 70"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- supply of spirits

**[[71]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-71 .sectionLabel}** No person shall give
possession of bulk spirits to a person other than a spirits licensee, a
licensed user or an alcohol registrant.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- supply of wine

- **[[72]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall give possession of bulk wine to a person other than a
  wine licensee, a licensed user or an alcohol registrant.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to an individual who,
  in connection with their personal use of it, gives possession of bulk
  wine lawfully produced by an individual for their personal use.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction --- licensed user

**[[73]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73 .sectionLabel}** A licensed user shall not
use or dispose of bulk alcohol other than to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} use it in an approved formulation;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} use it in a process in which the absolute ethyl
  alcohol is destroyed to the extent approved by the Minister;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} use it to produce vinegar;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} use it in accordance with section 130, 131 or 131.1;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} return it to

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of alcohol returned in circumstances to
    which paragraph 105(1)(a) or 114(1)(a) applies, a licensee referred
    to in that paragraph, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the alcohol licensee who
    supplied it;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} subject to section 76, export it;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} use it for analysis in a manner approved by the
  Minister; or

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} destroy it in a manner approved by the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 73
- 2007, c. 18, s. 94
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-73-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 73"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disposal of bulk spirits

**[[74]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-74 .sectionLabel}** A person who possesses
bulk spirits produced for the purpose or as a consequence of the
analysis of the composition of a substance containing absolute ethyl
alcohol shall, immediately after the analysis is complete, destroy or
dispose of the spirits in a manner approved by the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 74
- 2007, c. 18, s. 95
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-74-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 74"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Importation --- bulk spirits

- **[[75]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall import bulk spirits other than a spirits licensee, a
  licensed user or, if the spirits are in a special container, an excise
  warehouse licensee in accordance with section 80.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Importation --- bulk wine

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall import bulk wine other than a wine
  licensee, a licensed user or, if the wine is in a special container,
  an excise warehouse licensee in accordance with section 85.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 75
- 2007, c. 18, s. 95
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-75-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 75"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unauthorized export

**[[76]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76 .sectionLabel}** No person shall export
bulk alcohol other than

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the alcohol licensee who is responsible for the
  alcohol;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the licensed user who imported the alcohol; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a person required to do so under section 101.

### [Special Containers of Spirits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-179724 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Marked container deemed packaged

**[[77]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77 .sectionLabel}** If a special container of
spirits is marked, the spirits are deemed to be packaged at the time the
container is marked.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Marking

- **[[78]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-78 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall mark a special container of spirits unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person is a spirits licensee; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the container is one that has been placed in a
    sufferance warehouse in accordance with section 80 and the person
    marks the container in the circumstances described in that section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Container to be warehoused

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a spirits licensee marks a special container of
  spirits, the licensee shall immediately enter it into an excise
  warehouse.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Importation

**[[79]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-79 .sectionLabel}** No person, other than an
excise warehouse licensee, shall import a marked special container of
spirits.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Marking of imported container

**[[80]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-80 .sectionLabel}** If a special container of
spirits that is imported by an excise warehouse licensee is not marked
when it is reported under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), it shall
be placed in a sufferance warehouse for the purpose of being marked.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imported container to be warehoused

**[[81]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-81 .sectionLabel}** If a marked special
container of spirits is released under the [Customs
Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), the excise warehouse licensee who imported it
shall immediately enter it into their excise warehouse.

### [Special Containers of Wine]{.HTitleText2} {#h-179740 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Marked container deemed packaged

**[[82]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-82 .sectionLabel}** If a special container of
wine is marked, the wine is deemed to be packaged at the time the
container is marked.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Marking

- **[[83]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-83 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall mark a special container of wine unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person is a wine licensee; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the container is one that has been placed in a
    sufferance warehouse in accordance with section 85 and the person
    marks the container in the circumstances described in that section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Container to be warehoused

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a wine licensee marks a special container of wine,
  the licensee shall immediately enter it into an excise warehouse.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Importation

**[[84]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-84 .sectionLabel}** No person, other than an
excise warehouse licensee, shall import a marked special container of
wine.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Marking of imported container

**[[85]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85 .sectionLabel}** If a special container of
wine that is imported by an excise warehouse licensee is not marked when
it is reported under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), it shall be
placed in a sufferance warehouse for the purpose of being marked.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imported container to be warehoused

**[[86]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-86 .sectionLabel}** If a marked special
container of wine is released under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6),
the excise warehouse licensee who imported it shall immediately enter it
into their excise warehouse.

### [Packaged Alcohol]{.HTitleText2} {#h-179756 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information on container

**[[87]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-87 .sectionLabel}** Every alcohol licensee
who packages alcohol shall cause all prescribed information to be
displayed on the container containing the alcohol and on any packaging
encasing the container

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of wine that is entered into an excise
  warehouse immediately after it is packaged, before the wine is removed
  from the warehouse; and

- [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2022, c. 10, s. 129\]]{.Repealed}

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, immediately after the alcohol is
  packaged.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 87
- 2007, c. 2, s. 55
- [2022, c. 10, s. 129](#1356646-1370442){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356646-1370442.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-87-20070221.html "Link to previous version of section 87"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- possession

- **[[88]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-88 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall possess non-duty-paid packaged alcohol.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Non-duty-paid packaged alcohol, other than alcohol in
  a marked special container,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that is packaged by an alcohol licensee or imported
    by an excise warehouse licensee may be possessed by

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an excise warehouse licensee in their excise
      warehouse,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a licensed user in their specified premises,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a registered user for use in accordance with
      their registration,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed person who is transporting the
      alcohol under prescribed circumstances and conditions,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a duty free shop licensee, in their duty free
      shop,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} an accredited representative, for their personal
      or official use, or

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} a person as ships' stores, if the acquisition
      and possession of the alcohol by that person are in accordance
      with the [Ships' Stores Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-40);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that is imported may be possessed by a sufferance
    warehouse licensee, in their sufferance warehouse;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that is imported by a licensed user may be
    possessed by

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the licensed user in their specified premises, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed person who is transporting the
      alcohol under prescribed circumstances and conditions;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} that is imported by an accredited representative
    may be possessed by

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the accredited representative, for their personal
      or official use, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed person who is transporting the
      alcohol under prescribed circumstances and conditions;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} that is imported for sale in a duty free shop, for
    sale to an accredited representative or for use as ships' stores,
    may be possessed by

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a customs bonded warehouse licensee, in their
      customs bonded warehouse,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a duty free shop licensee, in their duty free
      shop,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} an accredited representative, for their
      personal or official use,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a customs bonded carrier in accordance with the
      [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), or

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a person as ships' stores, if the acquisition and
      possession of the alcohol by that person are in accordance with
      the [Ships' Stores Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-40);

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} that is imported for supply to an air carrier that
    is licensed under section 69 or 73 of the [Canada Transportation
    Act](/eng/acts/C-10.4) to operate an international air service may
    be possessed by a customs bonded warehouse licensee, in their
    customs bonded warehouse;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} that is imported by an individual in accordance
    with the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) and the [Customs
    Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) for their personal use may be possessed
    by an individual;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} that is wine that is produced and packaged by an
    individual for their personal use may be possessed by an individual;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that is wine referred to in paragraph 135(2)(b) may
    be possessed by any person; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} that is wine produced or packaged by a wine
    licensee and removed from the excise warehouse of the licensee and
    that is to be provided free of charge to individuals as a sample
    consumed at the premises where the licensee produces or packages
    wine may be possessed by the licensee or those individuals at those
    premises.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- special containers

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A non-duty-paid marked special container of alcohol
  may be possessed by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an excise warehouse licensee in their excise
    warehouse;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed person who is transporting the alcohol
    under prescribed circumstances and conditions;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an imported special container of
    alcohol, a sufferance warehouse licensee in their sufferance
    warehouse; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a special container of spirits that
    is marked for delivery to and use by a registered user, a registered
    user for use in accordance with their registration.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 88
- 2007, c. 18, ss. 96, 157
- [2022, c. 10, s. 130](#1356649-1370444){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356649-1370444.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-88-20070622.html "Link to previous version of section 88"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage

**[[89]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-89 .sectionLabel}** A ferment-on-premises
registrant shall not store packaged wine at their ferment-on-premises
facility.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction --- licensed user

**[[90]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-90 .sectionLabel}** A licensed user shall not
use or dispose of non-duty-paid packaged alcohol other than to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} use it in an approved formulation;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} use it in a process in which the absolute ethyl
  alcohol is destroyed to the extent approved by the Minister;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} use it to produce vinegar;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} return it under prescribed conditions to the excise
  warehouse licensee who supplied it;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} export it, if the alcohol was imported by the
  licensed user;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} use it for analysis in a manner approved by the
  Minister; or

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} destroy it in a manner approved by the Minister.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction --- registered user

**[[91]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-91 .sectionLabel}** A registered user shall
not use or dispose of non-duty-paid packaged spirits other than to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} use them in accordance with their registration;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} use them for analysis in a manner approved by the
  Minister;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} return them under prescribed conditions to the excise
  warehouse licensee who supplied them; or

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} destroy them in a manner approved by the Minister.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unauthorized removal --- spirits

- **[[92]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-92 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall remove spirits from a marked special container of spirits
  other than

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a registered user, in the case of a container that
    is marked for delivery to and use by a registered user; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a purchaser of the spirits at a bottle-your-own
    premises, in the case of a container that is marked for delivery to
    and use at a bottle-your-own premises.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal of spirits from returned
  container

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the operator of a bottle-your-own premises returns
  a marked special container of spirits to the excise warehouse licensee
  who supplied the container to the operator, the licensee may remove
  the spirits from the container for the purpose of destroying the
  spirits in a manner approved by the Minister.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unauthorized removal --- wine

- **[[93]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-93 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall remove wine from a marked special container of wine other
  than a purchaser of the wine at a bottle-your-own premises.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal of wine from returned container

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the operator of a bottle-your-own premises returns
  a marked special container of wine to the excise warehouse licensee
  who supplied the container to the operator, the licensee may remove
  the wine from the container for the purpose of destroying the wine in
  a manner approved by the Minister.

### [Restricted Formulations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-179835 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction --- licensed user

**[[93.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-93.1 .sectionLabel}** A licensed user shall
not use or dispose of a restricted formulation other than in accordance
with the conditions or restrictions imposed by the Minister under
section 143.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 18, s. 97
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- possession of restricted
formulation

**[[93.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-93.2 .sectionLabel}** No person other than
a licensed user or an alcohol registrant shall possess a restricted
formulation.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 18, s. 97
:::

### [Denatured Alcohol and Specially Denatured Alcohol]{.HTitleText2} {#h-179844 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- denaturing of spirits

**[[94]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-94 .sectionLabel}** No person, other than a
spirits licensee, shall denature spirits.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- sale as beverage

- **[[95]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-95 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall sell or provide denatured alcohol or specially denatured
  alcohol for use in or as a beverage.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- use as beverage

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall use denatured alcohol or specially
  denatured alcohol in or as a beverage.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- use of SDA

**[[96]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-96 .sectionLabel}** No person shall, except
in accordance with a specially denatured alcohol registration issued to
the person, use specially denatured alcohol.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- possession of SDA

- **[[97]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-97 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall possess specially denatured alcohol.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a spirits licensee or an SDA registrant who
    possesses specially denatured alcohol produced by a spirits
    licensee;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a spirits licensee, an SDA registrant or a
    sufferance warehouse licensee who possesses specially denatured
    alcohol imported by a spirits licensee;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an SDA registrant who possesses specially denatured
    alcohol that they imported;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a sufferance warehouse licensee who possesses
    specially denatured alcohol imported by an SDA registrant; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} an alcohol registrant who possesses specially
    denatured alcohol only for the purposes of its storage and
    transportation, if the alcohol was produced by a spirits licensee or
    imported by a spirits licensee or an SDA registrant.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- supply of SDA

**[[98]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-98 .sectionLabel}** No person shall give
possession of specially denatured alcohol to a person who is not a
spirits licensee, an SDA registrant or an alcohol registrant.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- sale of SDA

- **[[99]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-99 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall sell specially denatured alcohol.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a spirits licensee sells specially denatured
    alcohol to another spirits licensee or to an SDA registrant; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an SDA registrant returns specially denatured
    alcohol in accordance with paragraph 103(a) or exports it in
    accordance with paragraph 103(b).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- importing of SDA

**[[100]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-100 .sectionLabel}** No person shall import
specially denatured alcohol other than a spirits licensee or an SDA
registrant.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Spirits mistakenly imported as DA or SDA

- **[[101]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-101 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  person, other than a spirits licensee or licensed user, who has
  imported a product that was reported under the [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) as being denatured alcohol or specially
  denatured alcohol learns that the product is spirits and not denatured
  alcohol or specially denatured alcohol, the person shall without delay

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} export it for return to the person from whom it was
    acquired; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} dispose of or destroy it in the manner specified by
    the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Spirits mistakenly possessed as DA or
  SDA

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a person, other than a spirits licensee, licensed
  user or alcohol registrant, who possesses a product that the person
  believed was denatured alcohol or specially denatured alcohol learns
  that the product is spirits and not denatured alcohol or specially
  denatured alcohol, the person shall without delay

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} return it to the spirits licensee who produced or
    supplied the product; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} dispose of or destroy it in the manner specified by
    the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}If product used

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the person is unable to comply with subsection (1)
  or (2) in respect of an amount of the product because they had already
  used it in the production of another product before they learned that
  the product was not denatured alcohol or specially denatured alcohol,
  the person shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} dispose of or destroy the other product in the
    manner specified by the Minister; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} pay any penalty imposed under section 254 for which
    they are liable under section 244 in respect of the amount.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (3) does not apply if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the other product is not, in the opinion of the
    Minister, spirits;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Minister deems the other product to have been
    produced using denatured alcohol or specially denatured alcohol, as
    the case may be; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person complies with any conditions imposed by
    the Minister.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- exporting of SDA

**[[102]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-102 .sectionLabel}** No person shall export
specially denatured alcohol other than the SDA registrant who imported
it or a spirits licensee.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on disposal

**[[103]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-103 .sectionLabel}** An SDA registrant shall
not dispose of specially denatured alcohol other than to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} return it to the spirits licensee who supplied it;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} export it, if it was imported by the SDA registrant;
  or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} destroy it in a manner approved by the Minister.

### [Responsibility for Bulk Spirits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-179900 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Responsibility

**[[104]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-104 .sectionLabel}** Subject to sections 105
to 107, 111 and 112, the person who is responsible for bulk spirits at
any time is

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the spirits licensee or licensed user who owns the
  spirits at that time;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the spirits are not owned at that time by a
  spirits licensee or licensed user, the spirits licensee or licensed
  user who last owned them; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the spirits were never owned by a spirits licensee
  or licensed user, the spirits licensee who imported or produced them
  or the licensed user who imported them.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of spirits purchased from
unlicensed person

- **[[105]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-105 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This
  section applies if a spirits licensee or licensed user (in this
  section referred to as the "purchaser") purchases bulk spirits from a
  person who is not a spirits licensee or licensed user (in this section
  referred to as the "unlicensed person"), and, within 30 days after the
  purchaser receives the spirits,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the purchaser returns the spirits to the spirits
    licensee who was responsible for them immediately before they were
    purchased by the purchaser (in this section referred to as the
    "previously responsible licensee") or to the spirits licensee who
    supplied them (in this section referred to as the "supplier"); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the ownership of the spirits reverts to the
    unlicensed person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination of person responsible for
  returned spirits

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} At the later of the time at which the previously
  responsible licensee or supplier receives the spirits and the time at
  which the ownership of the spirits reverts to the unlicensed person,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the previously responsible licensee becomes again
    responsible for the spirits; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the purchaser of the spirits ceases to be
    responsible for them.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- provincial ownership

**[[106]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-106 .sectionLabel}** If, at any time, the
government of a province or a liquor authority that is a spirits
licensee or a licensed user owns bulk spirits for a purpose not related
to its licence, section 104 applies as though that ownership by the
government or the liquor authority did not exist at that time.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Spirits imported by licensed user

**[[107]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-107 .sectionLabel}** A licensed user who
imports bulk spirits is responsible for them.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Blended spirits --- joint and several or
solidary responsibility

- **[[108]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-108 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  bulk spirits are blended with other bulk spirits, or if bulk spirits
  are blended with bulk wine and the resulting product is spirits, every
  person who is a person responsible for any of the spirits or who is a
  licensed user responsible for any of the bulk wine is jointly and
  severally or solidarily responsible for the resulting blended spirits.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Responsibility for wine ceases

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The wine licensee or licensed user who was
  responsible for the bulk wine before it was blended with bulk spirits
  as described in subsection (1) ceases to be responsible for the wine
  as of the time of blending.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Person not responsible

**[[109]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-109 .sectionLabel}** A person who is
responsible for bulk spirits ceases to be responsible for them if they
are

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} taken for use and the duty on them is paid;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} taken for use in an approved formulation;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} taken for use for a purpose described in section 145
  or subsection 146(1);

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} denatured into denatured alcohol or specially
  denatured alcohol;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} exported in accordance with this Act; or

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} lost in prescribed circumstances, if the person
  fulfills any prescribed conditions.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification of change of ownership

**[[110]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-110 .sectionLabel}** If a spirits licensee
or licensed user (in this section referred to as the "purchaser")
purchases bulk spirits from a person who is not a spirits licensee or
licensed user, the purchaser shall, except in respect of bulk spirits
that are to be imported,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} at the time of the purchase, obtain from the vendor
  the name and address of the spirits licensee who is responsible for
  the spirits immediately before they are sold to the purchaser; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} without delay, notify in writing that licensee of the
  purchase.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal of special container

**[[111]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-111 .sectionLabel}** If an unmarked special
container of spirits is removed by a spirits licensee from their excise
warehouse in accordance with section 156, the licensee is responsible
for the spirits unless they are owned by another spirits licensee or a
licensed user, in which case the other spirits licensee or the licensed
user is responsible for them.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal of spirits

**[[112]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-112 .sectionLabel}** If spirits are removed
by a spirits licensee from their excise warehouse in accordance with
section 158, the licensee is responsible for the spirits unless they are
owned by another spirits licensee or a licensed user, in which case the
other spirits licensee or the licensed user is responsible for them.

### [Responsibility for Bulk Wine]{.HTitleText2} {#h-179940 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Responsibility

**[[113]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-113 .sectionLabel}** Subject to sections 114
to 116, 120 and 121, the person who is responsible for bulk wine at any
time is

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the wine licensee or licensed user who owns the wine
  at that time;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the wine is not owned at that time by a wine
  licensee or licensed user, the wine licensee or licensed user who last
  owned it; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the wine was never owned by a wine licensee or
  licensed user, the wine licensee who imported or produced it or the
  licensed user who imported it.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of wine purchased from unlicensed
person

- **[[114]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-114 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This
  section applies if a wine licensee or licensed user (in this section
  referred to as the "purchaser") purchases bulk wine from a person who
  is not a wine licensee or licensed user (in this section referred to
  as the "unlicensed person"), and, within 30 days after the purchaser
  receives the wine,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the purchaser returns the wine to the wine licensee
    who was responsible for it immediately before it was purchased by
    the purchaser (in this section referred to as the "previously
    responsible licensee") or to the wine licensee who supplied it (in
    this section referred to as the "supplier"); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the ownership of the wine reverts to the unlicensed
    person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination of person responsible for
  returned wine

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} At the later of the time at which the previously
  responsible licensee or supplier receives the wine and the time at
  which the ownership of the wine reverts to the unlicensed person,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the previously responsible licensee becomes again
    responsible for the wine; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the purchaser of the wine ceases to be responsible
    for it.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- provincial ownership

**[[115]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-115 .sectionLabel}** If, at any time, the
government of a province or a liquor authority that is a wine licensee
or a licensed user owns bulk wine for a purpose not related to its
licence, section 113 applies as though that ownership by the government
or the liquor authority did not exist at that time.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Wine imported by licensed user

**[[116]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-116 .sectionLabel}** A licensed user who
imports bulk wine is responsible for it.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Blended wine --- joint and several or
solidary responsibility

- **[[117]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-117 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  bulk wine is blended with other bulk wine, or if bulk wine is blended
  with bulk spirits and the resulting product is wine, every person who
  is a person responsible for any of the wine or who is a licensed user
  responsible for any of the bulk spirits is jointly and severally or
  solidarily responsible for the resulting blended wine.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Responsibility for spirits ceases

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The spirits licensee or licensed user who was
  responsible for the bulk spirits before they were blended with bulk
  wine as described in subsection (1) ceases to be responsible for the
  spirits as of the time of blending.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Responsibility for wine ceases

**[[117.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-117.1 .sectionLabel}** If bulk spirits are
produced from bulk wine, the wine licensee or licensed user who was
responsible for the wine before it was used to produce the spirits
ceases to be responsible for the wine at the time the spirits are
produced.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 18, s. 98
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Person not responsible

**[[118]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-118 .sectionLabel}** A person who is
responsible for bulk wine ceases to be responsible for it if it is

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} taken for use and the duty on it is paid;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} taken for use in an approved formulation;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} taken for use for a purpose described in section 145
  or subsection 146(1);

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} exported in accordance with this Act; or

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} lost, if the loss is recorded in a manner authorized
  by the Minister.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification of change of ownership

**[[119]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-119 .sectionLabel}** If a wine licensee or
licensed user (in this section referred to as the "purchaser") purchases
bulk wine from a person who is not a wine licensee or licensed user, the
purchaser shall, except in respect of bulk wine that is to be imported,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} at the time of the purchase, obtain from the vendor
  the name and address of the wine licensee who is responsible for the
  wine immediately before it is sold to the purchaser; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} without delay, notify in writing that licensee of the
  purchase.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal of special container

**[[120]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-120 .sectionLabel}** If an unmarked special
container of wine is removed by a wine licensee from their excise
warehouse in accordance with section 156, the licensee is responsible
for the wine unless the wine is owned by another wine licensee or a
licensed user, in which case the other wine licensee or the licensed
user is responsible for it.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal of wine

**[[121]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-121 .sectionLabel}** If wine is removed by a
wine licensee from their excise warehouse in accordance with section
157, the licensee is responsible for the wine unless it is owned by
another wine licensee or a licensed user, in which case the other wine
licensee or the licensed user is responsible for it.

### [Imposition and Payment of Duty on Alcohol]{.HTitleText2} {#h-179983 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imposition --- domestic spirits

- **[[122]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-122 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Duty
  is imposed on spirits produced in Canada at the rate set out in
  section 1 of Schedule 4.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time of imposition

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The duty is imposed at the time the spirits are
  produced.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imposition --- low alcoholic strength
spirits

**[[123]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-123 .sectionLabel}** If spirits do not
contain more than 7% of absolute ethyl alcohol by volume at the time
that they are packaged,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the duty imposed on the spirits under section 122 or
  levied under section 21.1 of the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011)
  is relieved; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} duty is imposed on the spirits at the rate set out in
  section 2 of Schedule 4.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[123.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-123.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply in
  this section.

  [[inflationary adjusted year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[inflationary adjusted year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means 2018 and
      every year after that year. ([année
      inflationniste]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[reference year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[reference year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a 12-month period
      that begins on April 1 of a year and ends on March 31 of the
      following year. ([année de référence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Annual adjustments

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Each rate of duty set out in Schedule 4 applicable in
  respect of a litre of absolute ethyl alcohol or in respect of a litre
  of spirits is to be adjusted on April 1 of an inflationary adjusted
  year so that the rate is equal to the greater of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the rate determined by the formula

    ::: {#180001 .Paragraph}
    A × B

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the rate of duty applicable to the litre of absolute ethyl
        alcohol or the litre of spirits, as the case may be, on March 31
        of the inflationary adjusted year, and

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is the amount, rounded to the nearest one-thousandth, or, if the
        amount is equidistant from two consecutive one-thousandths,
        rounded to the higher one-thousandth, determined by the formula
        ::: {#180005 .NestedFormula}
        C/D

        where

        [C]{.dfn}
        :   is the Consumer Price Index for the 12-month period ending
            on September 30 of the particular year preceding the
            inflationary adjusted year, and

        [D]{.dfn}
        :   is the Consumer Price Index for the 12-month period ending
            on September 30 of the year preceding the particular year,
            and
        :::
    :::

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the rate of duty referred to in the description of
    A in paragraph (a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adjustment --- 2023

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} In respect of the inflationary adjusted year that
  is 2023, the description of B in paragraph (2)(a) is deemed to be
  equal to 1.02.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adjustment --- 2024

  [(2.2)]{.lawlabel} In respect of the inflationary adjusted year that
  is 2024, the description of B in paragraph (2)(a) is deemed to be
  equal to 1.02.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adjustment --- 2025

  [(2.3)]{.lawlabel} In respect of the inflationary adjusted year that
  is 2025, if the amount determined for B in paragraph (2)(a) without
  reference to this subsection is greater than 1.02, the description of
  B in that paragraph is deemed to be equal to 1.02.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The adjusted rate determined under subsection (2) is
  to be rounded to the nearest one-thousandth or, if the adjusted rate
  is equidistant from two consecutive one-thousandths, to the higher
  one-thousandth.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consumer Price Index

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In this section, the Consumer Price Index for any
  12-month period is the result arrived at by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} aggregating the Consumer Price Index for Canada, as
    published by Statistics Canada under the authority of the
    [Statistics Act](/eng/acts/S-19), for each month in that period;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} dividing the aggregate obtained under paragraph (a)
    by 12; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} rounding the result obtained under paragraph (b) to
    the nearest one-thousandth or, if the result obtained is equidistant
    from two consecutive one-thousandths, to the higher one-thousandth.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of adjusted rate

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If duties on spirits are imposed in a reference year
  but become payable in another reference year that begins in an
  inflationary adjusted year, those duties are determined at the rate of
  duty as adjusted under subsection (2) on the first day of the other
  reference year.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2017, c. 20, s. 49
- [2023, c. 26, s. 125](#1409340-1414283){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1409340-1414283.html"
  role="button"}
- [2024, c. 17, s. 123](#1467289-1484159){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1467289-1484159.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-123.1-20230622.html "Link to previous version of section 123.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty payable when packaged

- **[[124]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-124
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to sections 126 and 127, the
  duty imposed on spirits is payable at the time the spirits are
  packaged unless, immediately after packaging, they are entered into an
  excise warehouse.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payable by responsible person

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Duty is payable by the person who is responsible for
  the spirits immediately before they are packaged.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Spirits licensee ceases to be liable
  for duty

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If an excise warehouse licensee becomes liable under
  section 140 for duty on the spirits, the person required under
  subsection (2) to pay the duty ceases to be liable to pay it.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty payable when removed from warehouse

**[[125]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-125 .sectionLabel}** If packaged spirits are
removed from an excise warehouse for entry into the duty-paid market,
duty is payable on the spirits at the time of their removal and is
payable by the excise warehouse licensee of the warehouse.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty payable on bulk spirits taken for
use

**[[126]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-126 .sectionLabel}** Subject to sections 144
to 146, if bulk spirits are taken for use, duty is payable at the time
the spirits are taken for use by the person who is responsible for the
spirits at that time.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty payable on unaccounted bulk spirits

- **[[127]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-127 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Duty
  is payable by the person who is responsible for bulk spirits on any
  portion of the spirits that cannot be accounted for by the person as
  being in the possession of a spirits licensee, a licensed user or an
  alcohol registrant.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When duty payable

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The duty is payable at the time the spirits cannot be
  accounted for.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in circumstances where
  the person is convicted of an offence under section 218 or is liable
  to pay a penalty under section 241.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty payable on packaged spirits taken
for use

**[[128]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-128 .sectionLabel}** Subject to sections 144
to 146, if non-duty-paid packaged spirits that are in the possession of
an excise warehouse licensee or a licensed user are taken for use, duty
is payable at the time the spirits are taken for use and is payable by
the licensee or user.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty payable on unaccounted packaged
spirits

- **[[129]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-129 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Duty
  is payable on non-duty-paid packaged spirits that have been received
  by an excise warehouse licensee or a licensed user but cannot be
  accounted for by the licensee or user

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} as being in the excise warehouse of the licensee or
    the specified premises of the user;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} as having been removed, used or destroyed in
    accordance with this Act; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} as having been lost in prescribed circumstances, if
    the licensee or user fulfills any prescribed conditions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When duty payable

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Duty is payable by the licensee or user at the time
  the spirits cannot be accounted for.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fortifying wine

- **[[130]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-130 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  licensed user who is also a wine licensee may use bulk spirits to
  fortify wine to an alcoholic strength not in excess of 22.9% absolute
  ethyl alcohol by volume.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty relieved on spirits

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The duty imposed under section 122 or levied under
  section 21.1 of the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) on the
  spirits that were used to fortify the wine is relieved.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Blending wine with spirits

**[[131]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-131 .sectionLabel}** A licensed user who is
also a spirits licensee may blend bulk wine with spirits if the
resulting product is spirits.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 131
- 2007, c. 18, s. 99
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-131-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 131"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Producing spirits from wine

**[[131.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-131.1 .sectionLabel}** A licensed user who
is also a spirits licensee may use bulk wine to produce spirits.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 18, s. 99
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed production of spirits --- blending
wine

- **[[131.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-131.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If wine is blended with bulk spirits
  and the resulting product is spirits,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the duty imposed under section 122 or levied under
    section 21.1 of the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) on the
    spirits that were blended with the wine is relieved; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the resulting spirits are deemed to be produced at
    the time of the blending.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed production of spirits --- other
  blending

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a material or substance, other than spirits or
  wine, containing absolute ethyl alcohol is blended with bulk spirits
  or wine and the resulting product is spirits,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the duty imposed under section 122 or levied under
    section 21.1 of the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) on the
    spirits that were blended with the material or substance is
    relieved; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the resulting spirits are deemed to be produced at
    the time of the blending.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 18, s. 99
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty relieved --- DA and SDA

**[[132]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132 .sectionLabel}** If a spirits licensee
denatures bulk spirits into denatured alcohol or specially denatured
alcohol, the duty imposed on the spirits under section 122 or levied
under section 21.1 of the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) is
relieved.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imposition of special duty

- **[[133]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-133 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  addition to the duty levied under section 21.1 or 21.2 of the [Customs
  Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011), a special duty is imposed on imported
  spirits delivered to or imported by a licensed user at the rate set
  out in Schedule 5.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Bulk spirits

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a spirits licensee imports bulk spirits that are
  delivered to a licensed user, the special duty is payable at the time
  the spirits are delivered to the licensed user and is payable

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} by the spirits licensee who is responsible for the
    spirits at that time;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the licensed user is responsible for the spirits
    at that time and there was a spirits licensee who was responsible
    immediately before that time, by that spirits licensee; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the licensed user is responsible for the spirits
    at that time and there was not a spirits licensee who was
    responsible immediately before that time, by the spirits licensee
    who delivered the spirits.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Packaged spirits

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If imported packaged spirits or imported spirits that
  have been packaged in Canada are removed from an excise warehouse for
  delivery to a licensed user, the special duty is payable by the excise
  warehouse licensee at the time the spirits are removed from the excise
  warehouse.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Spirits imported by licensed user

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If bulk or packaged spirits are imported by a
  licensed user, the special duty

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is payable by the licensed user at the time the
    spirits are imported; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} shall be paid and collected under the [Customs
    Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), and interest and penalties shall be imposed,
    calculated, paid and collected under that Act, as if the duty were a
    duty levied on the spirits under section 20 of the [Customs
    Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011), and, for those purposes, the [Customs
    Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) applies with any modifications that the
    circumstances require.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imposition --- bulk wine taken for use

- **[[134]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-134 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Duty
  is imposed on bulk wine that is taken for use at the rates set out in
  Schedule 6.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payable by responsible person

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to sections 144 to 146, the duty is payable
  at the time the wine is taken for use and is payable by the person who
  is responsible for the wine at that time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to wine

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that is produced by an individual for their
    personal use and that is consumed in the course of that use; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that is produced in Canada from honey or apples and
    composed wholly of agricultural or plant product grown in Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 134
- 2007, c. 2, s. 56
- [2022, c. 10, s. 131](#1356653-1370449){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356653-1370449.html"
  role="button"}
:::

Version](section-134-20070221.html "Link to previous version of section 134"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imposition --- wine packaged in Canada

- **[[135]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-135 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Duty
  is imposed on wine that is packaged in Canada at the rates set out in
  Schedule 6.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Wine produced for personal use and by
  small producers

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to wine that is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} produced in Canada from honey or apples and
    composed wholly of agricultural or plant product grown in Canada;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} produced and packaged by an individual for their
    personal use; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} produced by a wine licensee and packaged by or on
    behalf of the licensee during a fiscal month in a particular fiscal
    year of the licensee if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the total sales by the licensee of products that
      are subject to duty under subsection (1), or that would have been
      so subject to duty in the absence of this subsection, in the
      fiscal year ending immediately before the particular fiscal year
      did not exceed \$50,000, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the total sales by the licensee of those
      products during the particular fiscal year before the fiscal month
      did not exceed \$50,000.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time of imposition

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The duty is imposed at the time the wine is packaged.
  It is also payable at that time unless the wine is entered into an
  excise warehouse immediately after packaging.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payable by responsible person

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The duty is payable by the person who is responsible
  for the wine immediately before it is packaged.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Wine licensee ceases to be liable for
  duty

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If an excise warehouse licensee becomes liable under
  section 140 for duty on the wine, the person required under
  subsection (4) to pay the duty ceases to be liable to pay it.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 135
- 2007, c. 2, s. 57, c. 18, s. 100
- [2022, c. 10, s. 132](#1356657-1370451){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356657-1370451.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[135.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-135.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply in
  this section.

  [[inflationary adjusted year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[inflationary adjusted year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means 2018 and
      every year after that year. ([année
      inflationniste]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[reference year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[reference year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a 12-month period
      that begins on April 1 of a year and ends on March 31 of the
      following year. ([année de référence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Annual adjustments

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Each rate of duty set out in Schedule 6 applicable in
  respect of a litre of wine is to be adjusted on April 1 of an
  inflationary adjusted year so that the rate is equal to the greater of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the rate determined by the formula

    ::: {#180126 .Paragraph}
    A × B

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the rate of duty applicable to the litre on March 31 of the
        inflationary adjusted year, and

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is the amount, rounded to the nearest one-thousandth, or, if the
        amount is equidistant from two consecutive one-thousandths,
        rounded to the higher one-thousandth, determined by the formula
        ::: {#180130 .NestedFormula}
        C/D

        where

        [C]{.dfn}
        :   is the Consumer Price Index for the 12-month period ending
            on September 30 of the particular year preceding the
            inflationary adjusted year, and

        [D]{.dfn}
        :   is the Consumer Price Index for the 12-month period ending
            on September 30 of the year preceding the particular year,
            and
        :::
    :::

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the rate of duty referred to in the description of
    A in paragraph (a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adjustment --- 2023

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} In respect of the inflationary adjusted year that
  is 2023, the description of B in paragraph (2)(a) is deemed to be
  equal to 1.02.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adjustment --- 2024

  [(2.2)]{.lawlabel} In respect of the inflationary adjusted year that
  is 2024, the description of B in paragraph (2)(a) is deemed to be
  equal to 1.02.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adjustment --- 2025

  [(2.3)]{.lawlabel} In respect of the inflationary adjusted year that
  is 2025, if the amount determined for B in paragraph (2)(a) without
  reference to this subsection is greater than 1.02, the description of
  B in that paragraph is deemed to be equal to 1.02.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The adjusted rate determined under subsection (2) is
  to be rounded to the nearest one-thousandth or, if the adjusted rate
  is equidistant from two consecutive one-thousandths, to the higher
  one-thousandth.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consumer Price Index

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In this section, the Consumer Price Index for any
  12-month period is the result arrived at by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} aggregating the Consumer Price Index for Canada, as
    published by Statistics Canada under the authority of the
    [Statistics Act](/eng/acts/S-19), for each month in that period;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} dividing the aggregate obtained under paragraph (a)
    by 12; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} rounding the result obtained under paragraph (b) to
    the nearest one-thousandth or, if the result obtained is equidistant
    from two consecutive one-thousandths, to the higher one-thousandth.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of adjusted rate

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If duties on wine are imposed in a reference year but
  become payable in another reference year that begins in an
  inflationary adjusted year, those duties are determined at the rate of
  duty as adjusted under subsection (2) on the first day of the other
  reference year.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2017, c. 20, s. 50
- [2023, c. 26, s. 126](#1409344-1414286){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1409344-1414286.html"
  role="button"}
- [2024, c. 17, s. 124](#1467293-1484164){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1467293-1484164.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-135.1-20230622.html "Link to previous version of section 135.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty payable on removal from warehouse

- **[[136]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-136
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), if
  packaged wine is removed from an excise warehouse for entry into the
  duty-paid market, duty is payable on the wine at the time of its
  removal and is payable by the excise warehouse licensee.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removals for consignment sales

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a small wine licensee removes packaged wine that
  the licensee produced or packaged from the excise warehouse of the
  licensee for delivery and sale on a consignment basis at a retail
  store operated on behalf of two or more small wine licensees and that
  is not located on the premises of a wine licensee, the wine is deemed
  to be removed from the warehouse for entry into the duty-paid market
  at the time the wine is sold.

- Meaning of [[small wine licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In this section, a wine licensee is a small wine
  licensee during a fiscal year of the licensee if, in the previous
  fiscal year, the total amount of wine sold by the licensee did not
  exceed 60,000 litres.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 136
- 2007, c. 18, s. 101
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-136-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 136"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty payable on packaged wine taken for
use

**[[137]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-137 .sectionLabel}** Subject to sections 144
to 146, if non-duty-paid packaged wine that is in the possession of an
excise warehouse licensee or a licensed user is taken for use, duty is
payable on the wine at the time it is taken for use and is payable by
the licensee or user.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty payable on unaccounted packaged wine

- **[[138]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-138 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Duty
  is payable on non-duty-paid packaged wine that has been received by an
  excise warehouse licensee or a licensed user but that cannot be
  accounted for by the licensee or user

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} as being in the excise warehouse of the licensee or
    the specified premises of the user;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} in the case of packaged wine described by
    subsection 136(2), as being in a store described by that subsection;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} as having been removed, used or destroyed in
    accordance with this Act; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} as having been lost in prescribed circumstances, if
    the licensee or user fulfills any prescribed conditions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When duty payable

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Duty is payable by the licensee or user at the time
  the wine cannot be accounted for.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 138
- 2007, c. 18, s. 102
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-138-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 138"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty on wine in marked special container
relieved

- **[[139]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-139 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Duty
  imposed under subsection 135(1) is relieved on wine that is contained
  in a marked special container of wine from which the marking has been
  removed in accordance with section 156.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty on returned wine relieved

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Duty imposed under subsection 135(1) or levied under
  subsection 21.2(2) of the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) is
  relieved on wine that is returned to the bulk wine inventory of a wine
  licensee in accordance with section 157.

### [Liability of Excise Warehouse Licensees and Licensed Users]{.HTitleText2} {#h-180173 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-duty-paid packaged alcohol

**[[140]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-140 .sectionLabel}** If non-duty-paid
packaged alcohol is entered into an excise warehouse immediately after
being packaged, the excise warehouse licensee is liable for the duty on
the alcohol at the time it is entered into the warehouse.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imported packaged alcohol

**[[141]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-141 .sectionLabel}** If, in accordance with
subsection 21.2(3) of the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011), imported
packaged alcohol is, without the payment of duty, released under the
[Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) to the excise warehouse licensee or
licensed user who imported it, the excise warehouse licensee or licensed
user is liable for the duty on the alcohol.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transfer between warehouse licensees

- **[[142]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-142 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  non-duty-paid packaged alcohol is removed from the excise warehouse of
  an excise warehouse licensee (in this subsection referred to as the
  "transferor") to the excise warehouse of another excise warehouse
  licensee, at the time the alcohol is entered into the warehouse of the
  other licensee,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the other licensee becomes liable for the duty on
    the alcohol; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the transferor ceases to be liable for the duty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transfer to licensed user

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If non-duty-paid packaged alcohol is removed from an
  excise warehouse to the specified premises of a licensed user, at the
  time the alcohol is entered into those premises,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the licensed user becomes liable for the duty on
    the alcohol; and
