  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the excise warehouse licensee ceases to be liable
    for the duty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transfer from licensed user

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If non-duty-paid packaged alcohol is removed from the
  specified premises of a licensed user to an excise warehouse, at the
  time the alcohol is entered into the warehouse,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the excise warehouse licensee becomes liable for
    the duty on the alcohol; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the licensed user ceases to be liable for the duty.

### [Non-dutiable Uses and Removals of Alcohol]{.HTitleText2} {#h-180191 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approved formulations

**[[143]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-143 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may impose
any conditions or restrictions that the Minister considers necessary in
respect of the making, importation, packaging, use or sale of, or other
dealing with, an approved formulation.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-dutiable uses --- approved
formulations

**[[144]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144 .sectionLabel}** Duty is relieved on
bulk alcohol and non-duty-paid packaged alcohol used by a licensed user
in an approved formulation.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty not payable --- bulk alcohol

- **[[145]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-145 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Duty
  is not payable on bulk alcohol

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} taken for analysis by an alcohol licensee or a
    licensed user in a manner approved by the Minister;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} destroyed by an alcohol licensee or a licensed user
    in a manner approved by the Minister; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} used by a licensed user in a process in which the
    absolute ethyl alcohol is destroyed to the extent approved by the
    Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty not payable --- packaged alcohol

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Duty is not payable on non-duty-paid packaged alcohol

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} taken for analysis by an excise warehouse licensee
    or a licensed user in a manner approved by the Minister;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} destroyed by an excise warehouse licensee or a
    licensed user in a manner approved by the Minister;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} used by a licensed user in a process in which the
    absolute ethyl alcohol is destroyed to the extent approved by the
    Minister; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of wine, taken for use by an excise
    warehouse licensee if that licensee is also the wine licensee who
    produced or packaged the wine and the wine is provided free of
    charge to individuals for consumption as a sample at the premises
    where the licensee produces or packages wine.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No duty payable --- alcohol taken for
  analysis or destroyed

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Duty is not payable on bulk alcohol or non-duty-paid
  packaged alcohol that is taken for analysis or destroyed by the
  Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 145
- 2007, c. 18, s. 103
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-145-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 145"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty not payable --- vinegar

- **[[146]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-146 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Duty
  is not payable on alcohol that is used by a licensed user to produce
  vinegar if not less than 0.5 kg of acetic acid is produced from every
  litre of absolute ethyl alcohol used.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed taken for use if deficiency

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a licensed user uses alcohol to produce vinegar
  and less than 0.5 kg of acetic acid is produced from every litre of
  absolute ethyl alcohol used, the licensed user is deemed to have taken
  for use, at the time the vinegar is produced, the number of litres of
  that alcohol that is equivalent to the number determined by the
  formula

  ::: {#180217 .Subsection}
  A - (2 × B)

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the number of litres of absolute ethyl alcohol used, and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the number of kilograms of acetic acid produced.
  :::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty not payable --- packaged alcohol

- **[[147]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-147 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Duty
  is not payable on non-duty-paid packaged alcohol, other than alcohol
  contained in a marked special container, that is removed from an
  excise warehouse

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for delivery

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to an accredited representative for their
      personal or official use,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to a duty free shop for sale in accordance with
      the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6),

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} to a registered user for use in accordance with
      their registration, or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} as ships' stores in accordance with the [Ships'
      Stores Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-40); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for export by the excise warehouse licensee in
    accordance with this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty not payable --- special container
  of spirits

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Duty is not payable on spirits contained in a marked
  special container that is removed from an excise warehouse

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for delivery to a registered user for use in
    accordance with their registration, if the container is marked for
    delivery to and use by a registered user; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for export by the excise warehouse licensee in
    accordance with this Act, if the container was imported.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty not payable --- special container
  of wine

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Duty is not payable on wine imported in a marked
  special container that is removed from an excise warehouse for export
  by the excise warehouse licensee in accordance with this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty not payable --- wine samples

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Duty is not payable on non-duty-paid packaged wine,
  other than wine contained in a marked special container, that is
  removed from the excise warehouse of the wine licensee who produced or
  packaged the wine if the wine is to be provided free of charge to
  individuals as a sample consumed at the premises where the licensee
  produces or packages wine.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 147
- 2007, c. 18, s. 104
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-147-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 147"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Determining Volume of Alcohol]{.HTitleText2} {#h-180240 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Volume of alcohol

- **[[148]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-148 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  volume and absolute ethyl alcohol content of alcohol shall be
  determined in a manner specified by the Minister using approved
  instruments.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval of instrument

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may examine and approve an instrument or
  a class, type or design of instruments for the measurement of the
  volume and absolute ethyl alcohol content of alcohol.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Re-examination

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may direct in writing that any
  instrument previously examined and approved, or of a class, type or
  design previously examined and approved, by the Minister be submitted
  to the Minister for re-examination and, if the Minister so directs,
  the person who has the custody and control of the instrument shall
  immediately submit it to the Minister for re-examination.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation of approval

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} After re-examining an instrument, the Minister may,
  in writing, revoke the Minister's approval of that instrument or
  instruments of the same class, type or design as that instrument.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Indicating instrument is approved

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Every approved instrument the approval of which has
  not been revoked shall indicate the approval in a manner acceptable to
  the Minister.

### [Excise Warehouses]{.HTitleText2} {#h-180252 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction --- entering into warehouse

**[[149]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-149 .sectionLabel}** No person shall enter
non-duty-paid packaged alcohol into an excise warehouse except in
accordance with this Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Import by warehouse licensee

- **[[150]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-150 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  imported packaged alcohol is, without the payment of duty, released
  under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) to the excise warehouse
  licensee who imported it, the licensee shall immediately enter it into
  the excise warehouse of the licensee.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Import by licensed user

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If imported packaged alcohol is, without the payment
  of duty, released under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) to the
  licensed user who imported it, the licensed user shall immediately
  enter it into the specified premises of the licensed user.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on removal

- **[[151]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-151 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall remove non-duty-paid packaged alcohol from an excise
  warehouse.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, a person may remove from
  an excise warehouse

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} non-duty-paid packaged alcohol, other than alcohol
    in a marked special container, for

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} entry into the duty-paid market,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} delivery to another excise warehouse,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} delivery to an accredited representative for
      their personal or official use,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} delivery as ships' stores, in accordance with
      the [Ships' Stores Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-40),

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} delivery to a duty free shop for sale in
      accordance with the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) to persons who
      are about to leave Canada,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} delivery to a licensed user,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} delivery to a registered user for use in
      accordance with their registration,

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of packaged wine described in
      subsection 136(2), delivery to a store described in that
      subsection, or

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} export;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} non-duty-paid packaged wine, other than wine in a
    marked special container, if the warehouse is the excise warehouse
    of the wine licensee who produced or packaged the wine and the wine
    is to be provided free of charge to individuals as a sample consumed
    at the premises where the licensee produces or packages wine;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a non-duty-paid marked special container of wine
    for

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} delivery to another excise warehouse, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} entry into the duty-paid market for delivery to
      a bottle-your-own premises;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a non-duty-paid marked special container of spirits
    for

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} delivery to another excise warehouse,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the container is marked for delivery to and
      use by a registered user, delivery to a registered user for use in
      accordance with their registration, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} if the container is marked for delivery to and
      use at a bottle-your-own premises, entry into the duty-paid market
      for delivery to a bottle-your-own premises; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an imported non-duty-paid marked special container
    of alcohol, for export.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 151
- 2007, c. 18, s. 105
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-151-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 151"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of duty-paid alcohol

**[[152]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152 .sectionLabel}** If packaged alcohol
that has been removed from an excise warehouse for entry into the
duty-paid market is returned to that warehouse under prescribed
conditions, the alcohol may be entered into the warehouse as
non-duty-paid packaged alcohol.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of non-duty-paid alcohol

**[[153]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153 .sectionLabel}** If non-duty-paid
packaged alcohol that has been removed from an excise warehouse in
accordance with section 147 is returned to an excise warehouse under
prescribed conditions, the alcohol may be entered into the warehouse as
non-duty-paid packaged alcohol.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of non-duty-paid wine

**[[153.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.1 .sectionLabel}** If non-duty-paid
packaged wine that has been removed from an excise warehouse under
subparagraph 151(2)(a)(viii) is returned to that warehouse under
prescribed conditions, the wine may, if it had not been entered into the
duty-paid market, be entered into the warehouse as non-duty-paid
packaged wine.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 18, s. 106
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Supplying packaged alcohol to retail
store

- **[[154]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-154
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) and
  155(1), an excise warehouse licensee shall not, during a calendar
  year, supply from a particular premises specified in the excise
  warehouse licence of the licensee to a retail store more than 30% of
  the total volume of packaged alcohol supplied from those premises to
  all retail stores during the year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception for certain retail stores

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An excise warehouse licensee who is an alcohol
  licensee may supply from the particular premises to a retail store of
  the licensee more than 30% of that total volume if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the store is located at a place at which the
    licensee produces or packages alcohol; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} not less than 90% of the volume of packaged alcohol
    supplied to the store from the particular premises in the year
    consists of alcohol that was packaged by, or, if the licensee was
    responsible for the alcohol immediately before it was packaged, on
    behalf of, the licensee.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception for remote stores

- **[[155]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On
  application in the prescribed form and manner by an excise warehouse
  licensee who is a liquor authority or a non-retailer of alcohol, the
  Minister may authorize the licensee to supply during a calendar year
  from a particular premises specified in their excise warehouse licence
  to a retail store more than 30% of the total volume of packaged
  alcohol to be supplied from the premises to all retail stores during
  the year if the Minister is satisfied that the delivery of packaged
  alcohol by railway, truck or water vessel to the store is not possible
  for five consecutive months in every year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may revoke an authorization under
  subsection (1) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the licensee makes a written request to the
    Minister to revoke the authorization;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the licensee fails to comply with any condition
    imposed in respect of the authorization or any provision of this
    Act;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Minister is no longer satisfied that the
    requirements of subsection (1) are met; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the Minister considers that the authorization is no
    longer required.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of revocation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister revokes an authorization of a
  licensee, the Minister shall notify the licensee in writing of the
  revocation and its effective date.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal of special container

**[[156]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-156 .sectionLabel}** An alcohol licensee who
has marked a special container of alcohol may remove the container from
their excise warehouse to return it to the bulk alcohol inventory of the
licensee if the marking on the container is removed by the licensee in
the manner approved by the Minister.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal of packaged wine from excise
warehouse

**[[157]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-157 .sectionLabel}** A wine licensee may
remove non-duty-paid packaged wine from their excise warehouse to return
it to the bulk wine inventory of the licensee.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal of packaged spirits from excise
warehouse

**[[158]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158 .sectionLabel}** A spirits licensee may
remove non-duty-paid packaged spirits from their excise warehouse to
return them to the bulk spirits inventory of the licensee.

## [PART 4.1]{.HLabel1}[Cannabis]{.HTitleText1} {#h-180318 .Part}

### [Exclusions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-180319 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

**[[158.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.01 .sectionLabel}** This Part does
not apply to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} cannabis products that are produced in Canada by an
  individual for the personal use of the individual and in accordance
  with the [Cannabis Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5), but only to the extent that
  those cannabis products are used in activities that are not prohibited
  for those cannabis products under that Act;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} cannabis products that are produced in Canada by an
  individual for the medical purposes of the individual and in
  accordance with the [Controlled Drugs and Substances
  Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8) or the [Cannabis Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5), as
  applicable, but only to the extent that those cannabis products are
  used by the individual in activities that are not prohibited for those
  cannabis products under whichever of those Acts is applicable;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} cannabis products that are produced in Canada by a
  designated person --- being an individual who is authorized under the
  [Controlled Drugs and Substances Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8) or the
  [Cannabis Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5) to produce cannabis for the medical
  purposes of another individual --- for the medical purposes of the
  other individual and in accordance with whichever of those Acts is
  applicable, but only to the extent that those cannabis products are
  used by the designated person or the other individual in activities
  that are not prohibited for those cannabis products under whichever of
  those Acts is applicable; or

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} cannabis products that are in the possession of a
  holder of a licence referred to in paragraph 8(1)(e) or (f) of the
  [Cannabis Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-144), but only to the
  extent that those cannabis products are used by the holder of the
  licence in activities that are not prohibited for those cannabis
  products under the conditions of the licence or the [Cannabis
  Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
- [2022, c. 19, s. 94](#1383945-1385766){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1383945-1385766.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-158.01-20180621.html "Link to previous version of section 158.01"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Cannabis Production and Stamping]{.HTitleText2} {#h-180327 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Production without licence prohibited

- **[[158.02]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.02
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No person shall, other than in
  accordance with a cannabis licence issued to the person, produce
  cannabis products.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed producer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person that, whether for consideration or
  otherwise, provides or offers to provide in their place of business
  equipment for use in that place by another person in the production of
  a cannabis product is deemed to be producing the cannabis product and
  the other person is deemed not to be producing the cannabis product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the production of industrial hemp by-products by an
    industrial hemp grower; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed person that produces prescribed
    cannabis products, or cannabis products of a prescribed class, in
    prescribed circumstances or for a prescribed purpose.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-158.02-20180621.html "Link to previous version of section 158.02"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuance of cannabis excise stamps

- **[[158.03]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.03
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On application in the prescribed
  form and manner, the Minister may issue, to a cannabis licensee,
  stamps the purpose of which is to indicate that cannabis duty and, if
  applicable, additional cannabis duty have been paid on a cannabis
  product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quantity of cannabis excise stamps

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may limit the quantity of cannabis
  excise stamps that may be issued to a person under subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person shall be issued a cannabis excise stamp
  unless the person has provided any security required by regulation in
  a form satisfactory to the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Supply of cannabis excise stamps

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may authorize a producer of cannabis
  excise stamps to supply, on the direction of the Minister, cannabis
  excise stamps to a person to which those stamps are issued under
  subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Design and construction

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The design and construction of cannabis excise stamps
  shall be subject to the approval of the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Counterfeit cannabis excise stamps

**[[158.04]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.04 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
produce, possess, sell or otherwise supply, or offer to supply, without
lawful justification or excuse the proof of which lies on the person,
anything that is intended to resemble or pass for a cannabis excise
stamp.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unlawful possession of cannabis excise
stamps

- **[[158.05]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.05
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No person shall possess a cannabis
  excise stamp that has not been affixed to a packaged cannabis product
  in the manner prescribed for the purposes of the definition
  [[stamped]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 to indicate that duty
  has been paid on the cannabis product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions --- possession

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to the possession of a
  cannabis excise stamp by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person that lawfully produced the cannabis
    excise stamp;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person to which the cannabis excise stamp is
    issued; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed person.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unlawful supply of cannabis excise stamps

**[[158.06]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.06 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
dispose of, sell or otherwise supply, or offer to supply, a cannabis
excise stamp otherwise than in accordance with this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation of cannabis excise stamps

**[[158.07]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.07 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} cancel a cannabis excise stamp that has been issued;
  and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} direct that it be returned or destroyed in a manner
  specified by the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unlawful packaging or stamping

**[[158.08]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.08 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
package or stamp a cannabis product unless the person is a cannabis
licensee or a prescribed person.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unlawful removal

- **[[158.09]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.09
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except as permitted under section
  158.15, no person shall remove a cannabis product from the premises of
  a cannabis licensee unless it is packaged and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the cannabis product is intended for the
    duty-paid market,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} it is stamped to indicate that cannabis duty has
      been paid, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if additional cannabis duty in respect of a
      specified province is imposed on the cannabis product, it is
      stamped to indicate that the additional cannabis duty has been
      paid; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the cannabis product is not intended for the
    duty-paid market, all prescribed information is printed on or
    affixed to its container in a prescribed manner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a cannabis licensee
  that removes from their premises a cannabis product

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the cannabis product is being removed

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for delivery to another cannabis licensee,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for export as permitted under the [Cannabis
      Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5),

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} for delivery to a person for sterilization in
      accordance with subparagraph 158.11(3)(a)(ii), or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} for delivery to a person for analysis or
      destruction in accordance with subparagraph 158.3(a)(v);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the cannabis product is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a low-THC cannabis product,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a prescription cannabis drug, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed cannabis product or a cannabis
      product of a prescribed class; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in prescribed circumstances or for a prescribed
    purpose.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal by Minister

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to the removal of a
  cannabis product for analysis or destruction

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} by the Minister; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} by the [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as
    defined in subsection 2(1) of the [Cannabis Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- cannabis for sale

**[[158.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.1 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
purchase or receive for sale

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a cannabis product from a producer that the person
  knows, or ought to know, is not

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a cannabis licensee, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an industrial hemp by-product, an
    industrial hemp grower;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a cannabis product that is required under this Act to
  be packaged and stamped unless it is packaged and stamped in
  accordance with this Act; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a cannabis product that the person knows, or ought to
  know, is fraudulently stamped.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Selling, etc., unstamped cannabis

- **[[158.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.11
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No person, other than a cannabis
  licensee, shall dispose of, sell, offer for sale, purchase or have in
  their possession a cannabis product unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is packaged; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is stamped to indicate that cannabis duty has
    been paid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Selling, etc., unstamped cannabis ---
  specified province

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person, other than a cannabis licensee, shall
  dispose of, sell, offer for sale, purchase or have in their possession
  a cannabis product in a specified province unless it is stamped to
  indicate that additional cannabis duty in respect of the specified
  province has been paid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- possession of cannabis

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (2) do not apply to the
  possession of a cannabis product

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} by a person that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is a prescribed person that is transporting the
      cannabis product under prescribed circumstances and conditions,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is a prescribed person that is sterilizing the
      cannabis product under prescribed circumstances and conditions,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} is an individual if the cannabis product was
      imported for their medical purposes in accordance with the
      [Controlled Drugs and Substances Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8) or the
      [Cannabis Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5), as applicable, or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} possesses the cannabis product for analysis or
      destruction in accordance with subparagraph 158.3(a)(v);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the cannabis product is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a low-THC cannabis product,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a prescription cannabis drug, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed cannabis product or a cannabis
      product of a prescribed class; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in prescribed circumstances or for a prescribed
    purpose.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- disposal, sale, etc.

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (2) do not apply to the disposal,
  sale, offering for sale or purchase of a cannabis product

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the cannabis product is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a low-THC cannabis product,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a prescription cannabis drug, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed cannabis product or a cannabis
      product of a prescribed class; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in prescribed circumstances or for a prescribed
    purpose.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- industrial hemp

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (2) do not apply to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the possession of an industrial hemp by-product by
    the industrial hemp grower that produced it, if the industrial hemp
    by-product

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is on the industrial hemp grower's property, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is being transported by the industrial hemp
      grower for delivery to or return from a cannabis licensee; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the disposal, sale or offering for sale of an
    industrial hemp by-product to a cannabis licensee by the industrial
    hemp grower that produced it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- specified province

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) does not apply to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the possession of a cannabis product in prescribed
    circumstances or for a prescribed purpose; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the disposal, sale, offering for sale or purchase
    of a cannabis product in prescribed circumstances or for a
    prescribed purpose.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale or distribution by licensee

- **[[158.12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.12
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No cannabis licensee shall
  distribute a cannabis product or sell or offer for sale a cannabis
  product to a person unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is packaged;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is stamped to indicate that cannabis duty has
    been paid; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if additional cannabis duty in respect of a
    specified province is imposed on the cannabis product, it is stamped
    to indicate that the additional cannabis duty has been paid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to the distribution,
  sale or offering for sale of a cannabis product

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to a cannabis licensee;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the cannabis product is exported by the cannabis
    licensee in accordance with the [Cannabis Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the cannabis product is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a low-THC cannabis product,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a prescription cannabis drug, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed cannabis product or a cannabis
      product of a prescribed class; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in prescribed circumstances or for a prescribed
    purpose.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Packaging and stamping of cannabis

**[[158.13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.13 .sectionLabel}** A particular
cannabis licensee shall not enter a cannabis product into the duty-paid
market unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cannabis product is packaged;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the package has printed on it prescribed information;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the cannabis product is stamped --- using a cannabis
  excise stamp that has been issued to the particular cannabis licensee
  and that indicates that cannabis duty has been paid --- by

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the particular cannabis licensee, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} another cannabis licensee if prescribed conditions
    are met;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the cannabis product is to be entered in the
  duty-paid market of a specified province, the cannabis product is
  stamped --- using a cannabis excise stamp that has been issued to the
  particular cannabis licensee and that indicates that additional
  cannabis duty in respect of the specified province has been paid ---
  by

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the particular cannabis licensee, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} another cannabis licensee if prescribed conditions
    are met; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the particular cannabis licensee is responsible for
  the cannabis product immediately before it is stamped.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
- [2022, c. 19, s. 95](#1383947-1385778){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1383947-1385778.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-158.13-20180621.html "Link to previous version of section 158.13"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice --- absence of stamping

- **[[158.14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.14
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The absence on a cannabis product of
  stamping that indicates that cannabis duty has been paid is notice to
  all persons that cannabis duty has not been paid on the cannabis
  product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice --- absence of stamping

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The absence on a cannabis product of stamping that
  indicates that additional cannabis duty in respect of a specified
  province has been paid is notice to all persons that additional
  cannabis duty in respect of the specified province has not been paid
  on the cannabis product.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cannabis --- waste removal

- **[[158.15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.15
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No person shall remove a cannabis
  product that is waste from the premises of a cannabis licensee other
  than the cannabis licensee or a person authorized by the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal requirements

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a cannabis product that is waste is removed from
  the premises of a cannabis licensee, it shall be dealt with in the
  manner authorized by the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Re-working or destruction of cannabis

**[[158.16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.16 .sectionLabel}** A cannabis
licensee may re-work or destroy a cannabis product in the manner
authorized by the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

### [Responsibility for Cannabis]{.HTitleText2} {#h-180495 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Responsibility

**[[158.17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.17 .sectionLabel}** Subject to
section 158.18, a person is responsible for a cannabis product at any
time if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person is

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the cannabis licensee that owns the cannabis
    product at that time, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the cannabis product is not owned at that time
    by a cannabis licensee, the cannabis licensee that last owned it; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person is a prescribed person or a person that
  meets prescribed conditions.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Person not responsible

**[[158.18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.18 .sectionLabel}** A person that is
responsible for a cannabis product ceases to be responsible for it

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} if it is packaged and stamped and the duty on it is
  paid;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if it is consumed or used in the production of a
  cannabis product that is

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a low-THC cannabis product,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a prescription cannabis drug, or

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed cannabis product or a cannabis
    product of a prescribed class;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if it is taken for use and the duty on it is paid;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} if it is taken for use in accordance with any of
  subparagraphs 158.3(a)(i) to (v);

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} if it is exported in accordance with the [Cannabis
  Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5);

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} if it is lost in prescribed circumstances and the
  person fulfils any prescribed conditions; or

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} in prescribed circumstances or if prescribed
  conditions are met.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

### [Imposition and Payment of Duty on Cannabis]{.HTitleText2} {#h-180518 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imposition --- flat-rate duty

- **[[158.19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.19
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Duty is imposed on cannabis products
  produced in Canada at the time they are packaged in the amount
  determined under section 1 of Schedule 7.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imposition --- [ad valorem]{.otherLang
  lang="la"} duty

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Duty is imposed on packaged and stamped cannabis
  products produced in Canada at the time of their delivery to a
  purchaser in the amount determined under section 2 of Schedule 7.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty payable

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The greater of the duty imposed under subsection (1)
  and the duty imposed under subsection (2) is payable by the cannabis
  licensee that is responsible for the cannabis products immediately
  before those products are stamped and those products are relieved of
  the lesser of those duties. The greater of those duties is payable at
  the time of the delivery of the cannabis products to a purchaser.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Equal duties

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the amount of duty imposed under subsection (1) is
  equal to the amount of duty imposed under subsection (2), the duty
  imposed under subsection (1) is payable by the cannabis licensee that
  is responsible for the cannabis products immediately before those
  products are stamped and those products are relieved of the duty
  imposed under subsection (2). The duty imposed under subsection (1) is
  payable at the time of the delivery of the cannabis products to a
  purchaser.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
- [2022, c. 19, s. 96](#1383949-1385785){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1383949-1385785.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-158.19-20180621.html "Link to previous version of section 158.19"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imposition --- additional cannabis duty

- **[[158.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In addition to the duty imposed
  under section 158.19, a duty in respect of a specified province is
  imposed on cannabis products produced in Canada in prescribed
  circumstances in the amount determined in a prescribed manner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty payable

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The duty imposed under subsection (1) is payable by
  the cannabis licensee that is responsible for the cannabis products
  immediately before those products are stamped and that duty is payable
  at the time of their delivery to a purchaser.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
- [2022, c. 19, s. 97](#1383951-1385788){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1383951-1385788.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-158.2-20180621.html "Link to previous version of section 158.2"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty on imported cannabis

- **[[158.21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.21
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Duty is imposed on imported cannabis
  products in the amount that is equal to the greater of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount determined in respect of the cannabis
    products under section 1 of Schedule 7, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amount determined in respect of the cannabis
    products under section 3 of Schedule 7.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty payable

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The duty imposed under subsection (1) is payable by
  the importer, owner or other person that is liable under the [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) to pay duty levied under section 20 of the
  [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) or that would be liable to pay
  that duty on the cannabis products if they were subject to that duty.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional cannabis duty on imported
cannabis

- **[[158.22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.22
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In addition to the duty imposed
  under section 158.21, a duty in respect of a specified province is
  imposed on imported cannabis products in prescribed circumstances in
  the amount determined in a prescribed manner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty payable

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The duty imposed under subsection (1) is payable by
  the importer, owner or other person that is liable under the [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) to pay duty levied under section 20 of the
  [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) or that would be liable to pay
  that duty on the cannabis products if they were subject to that duty.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of [Customs
Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6)

**[[158.23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.23 .sectionLabel}** The duties
imposed under sections 158.21 and 158.22 on imported cannabis products
shall be paid and collected under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6),
and interest and penalties shall be imposed, calculated, paid and
collected under that Act, as if the duties were a duty levied under
section 20 of the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011), and, for those
purposes, the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) applies with any
modifications that the circumstances require.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Value for duty

**[[158.24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.24 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes
of section 3 of Schedule 7 and of any regulations made for the purposes
of section 158.22 in respect of imported cannabis products,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the value of a cannabis product is equal to the value
  of the cannabis product, as it would be determined under the [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) for the purpose of calculating duties imposed
  under the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) on the cannabis product
  at a percentage rate, whether the cannabis product is in fact subject
  to duty under the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} despite paragraph (a), the value of a cannabis
  product imported in prescribed circumstances shall be determined in
  prescribed manner.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty on cannabis taken for use

- **[[158.25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.25
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a particular person is
  responsible for cannabis products at a particular time when the
  cannabis products are taken for use, the following rules apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the cannabis products are packaged, they are
    relieved of the duty imposed under subsection 158.19(1); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} duty is imposed on the cannabis products in the
    amount that is equal to the greater of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the amount determined in respect of the cannabis
      products under section 1 of Schedule 7, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the amount determined in respect of the cannabis
      products under section 4 of Schedule 7.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Specified province --- duty on cannabis
  taken for use

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a particular person is responsible for cannabis
  products at a particular time when the cannabis products are taken for
  use, a duty in respect of a specified province is imposed on the
  cannabis products in prescribed circumstances in the amount determined
  in prescribed manner. This duty is in addition to the duty imposed
  under subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty payable

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The duty imposed under subsection (1) or (2) is
  payable at the particular time, and by the particular person, referred
  to in that subsection.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty on unaccounted cannabis

- **[[158.26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.26
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a particular person that is
  responsible at a particular time for cannabis products cannot account
  for the cannabis products as being, at the particular time, in the
  possession of a cannabis licensee or in the possession of another
  person in accordance with subsection 158.11(3) or paragraph
  158.11(5)(a), the following rules apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the cannabis products are packaged, they are
    relieved of the duty imposed under subsection 158.19(1); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} duty is imposed on the cannabis products in the
    amount that is equal to the greater of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the amount determined in respect of the cannabis
      products under section 1 of Schedule 7, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the amount determined in respect of the cannabis
      products under section 4 of Schedule 7.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Specified province --- duty on
  unaccounted cannabis

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a particular person that is responsible at a
  particular time for cannabis products cannot account for the cannabis
  products as being, at the particular time, in the possession of a
  cannabis licensee or in the possession of another person in accordance
  with subsection 158.11(3) or paragraph 158.11(5)(a), a duty in respect
  of a specified province is imposed on the cannabis products in
  prescribed circumstances in the amount determined in prescribed
  manner. This duty is in addition to the duty imposed under subsection
  (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty payable

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The duty imposed under subsection (1) or (2) is
  payable at the particular time, and by the particular person, referred
  to in that subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in circumstances in
  which the particular person referred to in that subsection is
  convicted of an offence under section 218.1.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[commencement
day]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[158.27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.27
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section,
  [[commencement day]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
  section 152 of the [Cannabis Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty on cannabis --- production before
  commencement day

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Duty is imposed on cannabis products that are
  produced in Canada and delivered to a purchaser before commencement
  day for sale or distribution on or after that day in the amount that
  is equal to the greater of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount determined in respect of the cannabis
    product under section 1 of Schedule 7, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amount determined in respect of the cannabis
    product under section 2 of Schedule 7.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional cannabis duty --- production
  before commencement day

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In addition to the duty imposed under subsection (2),
  a duty in respect of a specified province is imposed on cannabis
  products that are produced in Canada and delivered to a purchaser
  before commencement day for sale or distribution on or after that day
  in prescribed circumstances in the amount determined in a prescribed
  manner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty payable

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The duty imposed under subsection (2) or (3) is
  payable on commencement day by the cannabis licensee that packaged the
  cannabis product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) does not apply to a prescribed
  cannabis product, or a cannabis product of a prescribed class, that is
  delivered to a prescribed person in prescribed circumstances or for a
  prescribed purpose.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty relieved --- cannabis imported by
licensee

**[[158.28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.28 .sectionLabel}** The duties
imposed under sections 158.21 and 158.22 are relieved on

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a cannabis product that is not packaged and that is
  imported by a cannabis licensee; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed cannabis product, or a cannabis product
  of a prescribed class, that is imported by a prescribed person in
  prescribed circumstances or for a prescribed purpose.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty relieved --- prescribed
circumstances

**[[158.29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.29 .sectionLabel}** The duties
imposed under any of sections 158.19 to 158.22 and 158.27 are relieved
on a prescribed cannabis product, or a cannabis product of a prescribed
class, in prescribed circumstances or if prescribed conditions are met.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty not payable

**[[158.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.3 .sectionLabel}** Duty is not payable
on

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a cannabis product that

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is taken for analysis or destroyed by the Minister,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is taken for analysis or destroyed by the
    [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in subsection 2(1) of
    the [Cannabis Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5),

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} is taken for analysis by a cannabis licensee in a
    manner approved by the Minister,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} is destroyed by a cannabis licensee in a manner
    approved by the Minister,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} is delivered by a cannabis licensee to another
    person for analysis or destruction by that person in a manner
    approved by the Minister,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} is a low-THC cannabis product,

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} is a prescription cannabis drug, or

  - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} is a prescribed cannabis product or a cannabis
    product of a prescribed class;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a non-duty-paid cannabis product that is removed from
  the premises of a cannabis licensee for export in accordance with the
  [Cannabis Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5); or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed cannabis product, or a cannabis product
  of a prescribed class, that is delivered by a cannabis licensee to a
  prescribed person in prescribed circumstances or for a prescribed
  purpose.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quantity of cannabis

**[[158.31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.31 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes
of determining an amount of duty in respect of a cannabis product under
section 1 of Schedule 7, the following rules apply:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of flowering material and non-flowering
  material included in the cannabis product or used in the production of
  the cannabis product is to be determined in a prescribed manner in
  prescribed circumstances; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if paragraph (a) does not apply in respect of the
  cannabis product,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of flowering material and
    non-flowering material included in the cannabis product or used in
    the production of the cannabis product is to be determined at the
    time the flowering material and non-flowering material are so
    included or used and in a manner satisfactory to the Minister, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the quantity of flowering material included in
    the cannabis product or used in the production of the cannabis
    product is determined in accordance with subparagraph (i), the
    particular quantity of that flowering material that is industrial
    hemp by-product is deemed to be non-flowering material if that
    particular quantity is determined in a manner satisfactory to the
    Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delivery to purchaser

**[[158.32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.32 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes
of sections 158.19, 158.2 and 158.27 and for greater certainty, delivery
to a purchaser includes

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} delivering cannabis products, or making them
  available, to a person other than the purchaser on behalf of or under
  the direction of the purchaser;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} delivering cannabis products, or making them
  available, to a person that obtains them otherwise than by means of a
  purchase; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} delivering cannabis products or making them available
  in prescribed circumstances.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time of delivery

**[[158.33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.33 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes
of sections 158.19, 158.2 and 158.27, a cannabis product is deemed to be
delivered to a purchaser by a cannabis licensee at the earliest of

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the time at which the cannabis licensee delivers the
  cannabis product or makes it available to the purchaser,

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the time at which the cannabis licensee causes
  physical possession of the cannabis product to be transferred to the
  purchaser, and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the time at which the cannabis licensee causes
  physical possession of the cannabis product to be transferred to a
  carrier --- being a person that provides a service of transporting
  goods including, for greater certainty, a service of delivering mail
  --- for delivery to the purchaser.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dutiable amount

**[[158.34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.34 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose
of section 2 of Schedule 7, the dutiable amount of a cannabis product is
deemed to be equal to the fair market value of the cannabis product

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the cannabis product is delivered or made
  available to a person that obtains it otherwise than by means of a
  purchase; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in prescribed circumstances.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 73
:::

## [PART 4.2]{.HLabel1}[Vaping Products]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1366960 .Part}

### [Manufacturing and Stamping]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1377027 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manufacturing without licence prohibited

- **[[158.35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.35
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No person shall, other than in
  accordance with a vaping product licence issued to the person,
  manufacture vaping products.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed manufacturer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person that, whether for consideration or
  otherwise, provides or offers to provide in their place of business
  equipment for use in that place by another person in the manufacturing
  of a vaping product is deemed to be manufacturing the vaping product
  and the other person is deemed not to be manufacturing the vaping
  product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- manufacture for personal
  use

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An individual who is not a vaping product licensee
  may manufacture vaping products for their personal use.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- regulations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of a
  prescribed person that manufactures prescribed vaping products in
  prescribed circumstances or for a prescribed purpose.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1377038){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1377038.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuance of vaping excise stamps

- **[[158.36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.36
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On application in the prescribed
  form and manner, the Minister may issue, to a vaping product licensee
  or to a prescribed person that is importing vaping products, stamps
  the purpose of which is to indicate that vaping duty and, if
  applicable, additional vaping duty have been paid on a vaping product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quantity of vaping excise stamps

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may limit the quantity of vaping excise
  stamps that may be issued to a person under subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person shall be issued a vaping excise stamp
  unless the person has provided security in a form satisfactory to the
  Minister and in an amount determined in accordance with the
  regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Supply of vaping excise stamps

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may authorize a producer of vaping
  excise stamps to supply, on the direction of the Minister, vaping
  excise stamps to a person to which those stamps are issued under
  subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Design and construction

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The design and construction of vaping excise stamps
  shall be subject to the approval of the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1366760){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1366760.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Counterfeit vaping excise stamps

**[[158.37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.37 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
produce, possess, sell or otherwise supply, or offer to supply, without
lawful justification or excuse the proof of which lies on the person,
anything that is intended to resemble or pass for a vaping excise stamp.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1366764){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1366764.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unlawful possession of vaping excise
stamps

- **[[158.38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.38
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No person shall possess a vaping
  excise stamp that has not been affixed to the container of a vaping
  product in the manner prescribed for the purposes of the definition
  [[stamped]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 to indicate that duty
  has been paid on the vaping product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions --- possession

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to the possession of a
  vaping excise stamp by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person that lawfully produced the vaping excise
    stamp;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person to which the vaping excise stamp is
    issued;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a sufferance warehouse licensee that possesses the
    vaping excise stamp in their sufferance warehouse on behalf of the
    person described under paragraph (b); or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed person.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1366775){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1366775.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unlawful supply of vaping excise stamps

**[[158.39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.39 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
dispose of, sell or otherwise supply, or offer to supply, a vaping
excise stamp otherwise than in accordance with this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1366779){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1366779.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation of vaping excise stamps

**[[158.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.4 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} cancel a vaping excise stamp that has been issued;
  and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} direct that it be returned or destroyed in a manner
  specified by the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1366785){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1366785.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unlawful packaging or stamping

**[[158.41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.41 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
package or stamp a vaping product unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person is a vaping product licensee;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person is the importer or owner of the vaping
  product and the vaping product has been placed in a sufferance
  warehouse for the purpose of being stamped; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person is a prescribed person.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1377045){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1377045.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unlawful removal

- **[[158.42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.42
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except as permitted under section
  158.52 or if prescribed circumstances exist, no person shall remove a
  vaping product from the premises of a vaping product licensee unless
  it is packaged and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the vaping product is intended for the duty-paid
    market,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} it is stamped to indicate that vaping duty has
      been paid, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if additional vaping duty in respect of a
      specified vaping province is imposed on the vaping product, it is
      stamped to indicate that the additional vaping duty has been paid;
      or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the vaping product is not intended for the
    duty-paid market, all vaping product markings that are required
    under this Act to be printed on, or affixed to, its container are so
    printed or affixed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a vaping product
  licensee that removes from their premises a vaping product if it is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} being removed for

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} delivery to another vaping product licensee,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} export, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} delivery to a person for analysis or
      destruction in accordance with subparagraph 158.66(a)(iv); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a vaping product drug.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal by Minister

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to the removal of a
  vaping product for analysis or destruction by the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1377063){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1377063.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- vaping products for sale

**[[158.43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.43 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
purchase or receive for sale a vaping product

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} from a manufacturer that the person knows, or ought
  to know, is not a vaping product licensee;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} that is required under this Act to be packaged and
  stamped unless it is packaged and stamped in accordance with this Act;
  or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} that the person knows, or ought to know, is
  fraudulently stamped.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1377070){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1377070.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unlawful possession or sale of vaping
products

- **[[158.44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.44
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except if prescribed circumstances
  exist, no person, other than a vaping product licensee, shall dispose
  of, sell, offer for sale, purchase or have in their possession a
  vaping product unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is packaged; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is stamped to indicate that vaping duty has been
    paid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unlawful possession or sale ---
  specified vaping province

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Except if prescribed circumstances exist, no person,
  other than a vaping product licensee, shall dispose of, sell, offer
  for sale, purchase or have in their possession a vaping product in a
  specified vaping province unless it is stamped to indicate that
  additional vaping duty in respect of the specified vaping province has
  been paid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- possession of vaping
  products

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (2) do not apply to the
  possession of vaping products

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of imported vaping products,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} by an excise warehouse licensee in their excise
      warehouse,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} by a sufferance warehouse licensee in their
      sufferance warehouse, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} by a customs bonded warehouse licensee in their
      customs bonded warehouse;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} by a prescribed person that is transporting the
    vaping products under prescribed circumstances and conditions;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} by a person that possesses the vaping products for
    analysis or destruction in accordance with subparagraph
    158.66(a)(iv);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} by an accredited representative for their personal
    or official use;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} by an individual who has imported the vaping
    products for their personal use in quantities not in excess of
    prescribed limits;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} by an individual who has manufactured the vaping
    products in accordance with subsection 158.35(3); or

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} if the vaping products are vaping product drugs.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- sale or offer for sale

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (2) do not apply to the disposal,
  sale, offering for sale or purchase of a vaping product if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the vaping product is an imported vaping product,
    an excise warehouse licensee or a customs bonded warehouse licensee
    sells or offers to sell the vaping product for export and the vaping
    product is exported by the licensee in accordance with this Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the vaping product is an imported vaping product
    and an excise warehouse licensee or a customs bonded warehouse
    licensee sells or offers to sell the vaping product to an accredited
    representative for their personal or official use; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the vaping product is a vaping product drug.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1377096){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1377096.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale or distribution by licensee

- **[[158.45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.45
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except if prescribed circumstances
  exist, no vaping product licensee shall distribute a vaping product or
  sell or offer for sale a vaping product to a person unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is packaged;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is stamped to indicate that vaping duty has been
    paid; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if additional vaping duty in respect of a specified
    vaping province is imposed on the vaping product, it is stamped to
    indicate that the additional vaping duty has been paid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to the distribution,
  sale or offering for sale of a vaping product by a vaping product
  licensee

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the distribution, sale or offering for sale is
    to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} another vaping product licensee, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an accredited representative for their personal
      or official use;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the vaping product is exported by the vaping
    product licensee in accordance with this Act; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the vaping product is a vaping product drug.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1377111){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1377111.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Packaging and stamping of vaping products

- **[[158.46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.46
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A vaping product licensee that
  manufactures a vaping product shall not enter the vaping product into
  the duty-paid market unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the vaping product has been packaged by the
    licensee;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the package has printed on it prescribed
    information; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} before the end of the second calendar month
    following the calendar month in which the licensee packages the
    vaping product,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the vaping product is stamped by the licensee to
      indicate that vaping duty has been paid, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the vaping product is to be entered in the
      duty-paid market of a specified vaping province, the vaping
      product is stamped by the licensee to indicate that additional
      vaping duty in respect of the specified vaping province has been
      paid.

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2024, c. 15, s. 146\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Stamping of imported packaged vaping
  products

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A vaping product licensee that imports a packaged
  vaping product for stamping shall not enter the vaping product into
  the duty-paid market unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the vaping product is packaged in a package that
    has printed on it prescribed information; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} before the end of the second calendar month
    following the calendar month in which the vaping product is released
    under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6),

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the vaping product is stamped by the licensee to
      indicate that vaping duty has been paid, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the vaping product is to be entered in the
      duty-paid market of a specified vaping province, the vaping
      product is stamped by the licensee to indicate that additional
      vaping duty in respect of the specified vaping province has been
      paid.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1377119){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1377119.html"
  role="button"}
- [2024, c. 15, s. 146](#1462441-1484217){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1462441-1484217.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-158.46-20221001.html "Link to previous version of section 158.46"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Packaging and stamping of imported vaping
products

- **[[158.47]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.47
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except if prescribed circumstances
  exist, if a vaping product is imported, it must, before it is released
  under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) for entry into the duty-paid
  market,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be packaged in a package that has printed on it
    prescribed information;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} be stamped to indicate that vaping duty has been
    paid; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the vaping product is to be entered in the
    duty-paid market of a specified vaping province, be stamped to
    indicate that additional vaping duty in respect of the specified
    vaping province has been paid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions for certain importations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a vaping product

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that is imported by a vaping product licensee for
    further manufacturing by the licensee;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} that is a packaged vaping product imported by a
    vaping product licensee for stamping by the licensee;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that a vaping product licensee is authorized to
    import under subsection 158.53(2); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that is imported by an individual for their
    personal use in quantities not in excess of prescribed limits.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1377132){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1377132.html"
  role="button"}
- [2024, c. 15, s. 147](#1462448-1484219){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1462448-1484219.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-158.47-20221001.html "Link to previous version of section 158.47"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice --- absence of stamping

- **[[158.48]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.48
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The absence on a vaping product of
  stamping that indicates that vaping duty has been paid is notice to
  all persons that vaping duty has not been paid on the vaping product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice --- specified vaping province

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The absence on a vaping product of stamping that
  indicates that additional vaping duty in respect of a specified vaping
  province has been paid is notice to all persons that additional vaping
  duty in respect of the specified vaping province has not been paid on
  the vaping product.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1366792){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1366792.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unstamped products to be warehoused

- **[[158.49]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.49
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If vaping products manufactured in
  Canada are not stamped by a vaping product licensee before the end of
  the particular calendar month that is the second calendar month
  following the calendar month in which the vaping product licensee
  packages the vaping products, the vaping product licensee must enter
  the vaping products into its excise warehouse before the end of the
  particular calendar month.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imported unstamped packaged products to
  be warehoused

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a vaping product licensee imports packaged vaping
  products for stamping but does not stamp the vaping products before
  the end of the particular calendar month that is the second calendar
  month following the calendar month in which the vaping products are
  released under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), the vaping product
  licensee must enter the vaping products into its excise warehouse
  before the end of the particular calendar month.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (2) do not apply

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of vaping product drugs; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in prescribed circumstances.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1377136){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1377136.html"
  role="button"}
- [2024, c. 15, s. 148](#1462452-1484232){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1462452-1484232.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-158.49-20221001.html "Link to previous version of section 158.49"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vaping product markings --- warehousing

- **[[158.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.5
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4), no person
  shall enter into an excise warehouse a container of vaping products
  unless the container has printed on it, or affixed to it, vaping
  product markings and other prescribed information.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vaping product markings --- exports and
  accredited representatives

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4), no person shall remove a
  container of vaping products that are not stamped from the premises of
  a vaping product licensee for export or for delivery to an accredited
  representative unless the container has printed on it, or affixed to
  it, vaping product markings and other prescribed information.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vaping product markings --- imports

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (3) and (4), no person shall
  deliver a container of imported vaping products that does not have
  printed on it, or affixed to it, vaping product markings and other
  prescribed information to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an accredited representative; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a customs bonded warehouse.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delivery of imported stamped vaping
  products

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A container of imported vaping products that were
  manufactured outside Canada and are stamped may be delivered to a
  customs bonded warehouse.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception in prescribed circumstances

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A container of vaping products does not require
  vaping product markings to be printed on it, or affixed to it, if
  prescribed circumstances exist.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1366802){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1366802.html"
  role="button"}
- [2024, c. 15, s. 149](#1462459-1484235){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1462459-1484235.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-158.5-20221001.html "Link to previous version of section 158.5"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-compliant imports

- **[[158.51]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.51
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If an imported vaping product
  intended for the duty-paid market is not stamped to indicate that
  vaping duty has been paid when it is reported under the [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), it shall be placed in a sufferance warehouse
  for the purpose of being so stamped.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-compliant imports --- specified
  vaping province

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If an imported vaping product intended for the
  duty-paid market of a specified vaping province is not stamped to
  indicate that additional vaping duty in respect of the province has
  been paid when it is reported under the [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), it shall be placed in a sufferance warehouse
  for the purpose of being so stamped.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (2) do not apply

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a packaged vaping product that is
    imported by a vaping product licensee for stamping by the vaping
    product licensee; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in prescribed circumstances.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1377149){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1377149.html"
  role="button"}
- [2024, c. 15, s. 150](#1462463-1484240){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1462463-1484240.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-158.51-20221001.html "Link to previous version of section 158.51"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imports for stamping --- delivery to
premises

**[[158.511]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.511 .sectionLabel}** If a vaping
product licensee imports a packaged vaping product for stamping by the
vaping product licensee, the vaping product licensee shall, immediately
after the vaping product is released under the [Customs
Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), deliver the vaping product to its premises for
stamping.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2024, c. 15, s. 151](#1462468-1484244){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1462468-1484244.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vaping products --- waste removal

- **[[158.52]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.52
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No person shall remove a vaping
  product that is waste from the premises of a vaping product licensee
  other than the licensee or a person authorized by the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal requirements

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a vaping product that is waste is removed from the
  premises of a vaping product licensee, it shall be dealt with in the
  manner authorized by the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1377156){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1377156.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Re-working or destruction of vaping
products

- **[[158.53]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.53
