  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A vaping product licensee may
  re-work or destroy a vaping product in the manner authorized by the
  Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Importation for re-working or
  destruction

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may authorize a vaping product licensee
  to import vaping products manufactured in Canada by the licensee for
  re-working or destruction by the licensee in accordance with
  subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1377163){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1377163.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Responsibility for Vaping Products]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1377164 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Responsibility --- vaping products
manufactured in Canada

- **[[158.54]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.54
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 158.55, a person
  is responsible for a vaping product manufactured in Canada at any time
  if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the vaping product licensee that owns the vaping
      product at that time, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the vaping product is not owned at that time
      by a vaping product licensee, the vaping product licensee that
      last owned it; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person is a prescribed person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Responsibility --- imported vaping
  products

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to sections 158.55 and 158.56, a person is
  responsible for an imported vaping product at any time if the person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} imported the vaping product; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is a prescribed person.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1377177){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1377177.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Person not responsible

**[[158.55]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.55 .sectionLabel}** A person that is
responsible for a vaping product ceases to be responsible for it if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is packaged and stamped and the duty on it is
  paid;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is consumed or used in the manufacturing of a
  vaping product that is

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a vaping product drug, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed vaping product;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} it is taken for use and the duty on it is paid;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} it is taken for use in accordance with any of
  subparagraphs 158.66(a)(i) to (iv);

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} it is exported;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} it is delivered to an accredited representative for
  their personal or official use;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} it is lost in prescribed circumstances and the person
  fulfils any prescribed conditions; or

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1377191){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1377191.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imports for personal use

**[[158.56]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.56 .sectionLabel}** An individual
that imports vaping products for their personal use in quantities not in
excess of prescribed limits is not responsible for those vaping
products.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1377195){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1377195.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Imposition and Payment of Duty on Vaping Products]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1377196 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imposition

**[[158.57]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.57 .sectionLabel}** Duty is imposed
on vaping products manufactured in Canada or imported in the amount
determined under Schedule 8 and is payable

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of vaping products manufactured in
  Canada, by the vaping product licensee that packaged the vaping
  products and at the time they are stamped;

- [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} in the case of packaged vaping products that are
  imported by a vaping product licensee for stamping by the vaping
  product licensee, by the vaping product licensee and at the time they
  are stamped; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of any other imported vaping products, by
  the importer, owner or other person that is liable under the [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) to pay duty levied under section 20 of the
  [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) or that would be liable to pay
  that duty on the vaping products if they were subject to that duty.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1377202){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1377202.html"
  role="button"}
- [2024, c. 15, s. 152](#1462471-1484247){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1462471-1484247.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-158.57-20221001.html "Link to previous version of section 158.57"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imposition --- additional vaping duty

**[[158.58]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.58 .sectionLabel}** In addition to
the duty imposed under section 158.57, a duty in respect of a specified
vaping province is imposed on vaping products manufactured in Canada, or
imported, in prescribed circumstances in the amount determined in a
prescribed manner and is payable

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of vaping products manufactured in
  Canada, by the vaping product licensee that packaged the vaping
  products and at the time they are stamped;

- [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} in the case of packaged vaping products that are
  imported by a vaping product licensee for stamping by the vaping
  product licensee, by the vaping product licensee and at the time they
  are stamped; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of any other imported vaping products, by
  the importer, owner or other person that is liable under the [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) to pay duty levied under section 20 of the
  [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) or that would be liable to pay
  that duty on the vaping products if they were subject to that duty.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1377208){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1377208.html"
  role="button"}
- [2024, c. 15, s. 153](#1462475-1484252){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1462475-1484252.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-158.58-20221001.html "Link to previous version of section 158.58"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of [Customs
Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6)

**[[158.59]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.59 .sectionLabel}** The duties
imposed under paragraphs 158.57(b) and 158.58(b) on imported vaping
products shall be paid and collected under the [Customs
Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), and interest and penalties shall be imposed,
calculated, paid and collected under that Act, as if the duties were a
duty levied under section 20 of the [Customs
Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011), and, for those purposes, the [Customs
Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) applies with any modifications that the
circumstances require.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1366806){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1366806.html"
  role="button"}
- [2024, c. 15, s. 154](#1462479-1484255){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1462479-1484255.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-158.59-20220623.html "Link to previous version of section 158.59"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty on vaping products taken for use

- **[[158.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.6
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a particular person is
  responsible for vaping products at a particular time when the vaping
  products are taken for use, the following rules apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the vaping products are packaged, they are
    relieved of the duty imposed under section 158.57; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} duty is imposed on the vaping products in the
    amount determined in respect of the vaping products under Schedule
    8.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Specified vaping province --- taken for
  use

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a particular person is responsible for vaping
  products at a particular time when the vaping products are taken for
  use, a duty in respect of a specified vaping province is imposed on
  the vaping products in prescribed circumstances in the amount
  determined in prescribed manner. That duty is in addition to the duty
  imposed under subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty payable

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The duty imposed under subsection (1) or (2) is
  payable at the particular time, and by the particular person, referred
  to in that subsection.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1377219){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1377219.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty on unaccounted vaping products

- **[[158.61]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.61
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a particular person that is
  responsible at a particular time for vaping products cannot account
  for the vaping products as being, at the particular time, in the
  possession of a vaping product licensee or in the possession of
  another person in accordance with subsection 158.44(3), the following
  rules apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the vaping products are packaged, they are
    relieved of the duty imposed under section 158.57; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} duty is imposed on the vaping products in the
    amount determined in respect of the vaping products under Schedule
    8.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Specified vaping province ---
  unaccounted vaping products

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a particular person that is responsible at a
  particular time for vaping products cannot account for the vaping
  products as being, at the particular time, in the possession of a
  vaping product licensee or in the possession of another person in
  accordance with subsection 158.44(3), a duty in respect of a specified
  vaping province is imposed on the vaping products in prescribed
  circumstances in the amount determined in prescribed manner. That duty
  is in addition to the duty imposed under subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty payable

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The duty imposed under subsection (1) or (2) is
  payable at the particular time, and by the particular person, referred
  to in that subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in circumstances in
  which the particular person referred to in that subsection is
  convicted of an offence under section 218.2.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1377232){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1377232.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty relieved --- unstamped vaping
products

- **[[158.62]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.62
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The duties imposed under sections
  158.57 and 158.58 are relieved on a vaping product that is not
  stamped.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vaping products imported for personal
  use

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to the importation of
  vaping products by an individual for their personal use to the extent
  that the quantity of the products imported exceeds the quantity
  permitted under Chapter 98 of the List of Tariff Provisions set out in
  the schedule to the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) to be
  imported without the payment of [[duties]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as
  defined in Note 4 to that Chapter.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1366813){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1366813.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty relieved --- importation by an
individual

- **[[158.63]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.63
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The duties imposed under sections
  158.57 and 158.58 are relieved on vaping products imported by an
  individual for their personal use if they were manufactured in Canada
  and are stamped.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty relieved --- reimportation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The duties imposed under sections 158.57 and 158.58
  are relieved on vaping products imported by an individual for their
  personal use if they were manufactured outside Canada, were previously
  imported into Canada and are stamped.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1366820){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1366820.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty relieved --- importation for
destruction

**[[158.64]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.64 .sectionLabel}** The duties
imposed under paragraphs 158.57(b) and 158.58(b) are relieved on a
stamped vaping product that was manufactured in Canada by a vaping
product licensee and that is imported for re-working or destruction in
accordance with section 158.53.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1366824){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1366824.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty relieved --- prescribed
circumstances

**[[158.65]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.65 .sectionLabel}** The duties
imposed under section 158.57 or 158.58 are relieved on a vaping product
in prescribed circumstances.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1366828){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1366828.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty not payable

**[[158.66]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.66 .sectionLabel}** Duty is not
payable on a vaping product

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} that is

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} taken for analysis, or destroyed, by the Minister,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} taken for analysis by a vaping product licensee in
    a manner approved by the Minister,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} destroyed by a vaping product licensee in a
    manner approved by the Minister,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} delivered by a vaping product licensee to another
    person for analysis or destruction by that person in a manner
    approved by the Minister,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a vaping product drug, or

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed vaping product; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in prescribed circumstances.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1366840){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1366840.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Excise Warehouses]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1366841 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction --- entering vaping products

**[[158.67]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.67 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
enter into an excise warehouse

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a vaping product that is stamped; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any other vaping product except in accordance with
  this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1366853){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1366853.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition on removal

- **[[158.68]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.68
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except if prescribed circumstances
  exist, no person shall remove from an excise warehouse vaping products
  manufactured in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal of Canadian manufactured vaping
  products

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, a vaping product
  manufactured in Canada may be removed from the excise warehouse of the
  vaping product licensee that manufactured it only if it is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for export by the licensee in accordance with this
    Act; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for delivery to an accredited representative for
    their official or personal use.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal from warehouse for re-working
  or destruction

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, vaping products
  manufactured in Canada may be removed from the excise warehouse of the
  vaping product licensee that manufactured them if they are removed for
  re-working or destruction by the licensee in accordance with section
  158.53.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1377243){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1377243.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal of imported vaping products

- **[[158.69]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158.69
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except if prescribed circumstances
  exist, no person shall remove imported vaping products from an excise
  warehouse.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, imported vaping products
  may be removed from an excise warehouse

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for delivery to another excise warehouse;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for delivery to an accredited representative for
    their official or personal use; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for export by the excise warehouse licensee in
    accordance with this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 59](#1356386-1377253){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356386-1377253.html"
  role="button"}
:::

## [PART 5]{.HLabel1}[General Provisions Concerning Duty and Other Amounts Payable]{.HTitleText1} {#h-180659 .Part}

### [Fiscal Periods]{.HTitleText2} {#h-180660 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination of fiscal months

- **[[159]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-159 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  fiscal months of a person other than a cannabis licensee or a vaping
  product licensee shall be determined in accordance with the following
  rules:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if fiscal months of the person have been determined
    under subsection 243(2) or (4) of the [Excise Tax
    Act](/eng/acts/E-15) for the purposes of Part IX of that Act, each
    of those fiscal months is a fiscal month of the person for the
    purposes of this Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if fiscal months of the person have not been
    determined under subsection 243(2) or (4) of the [Excise Tax
    Act](/eng/acts/E-15) for the purposes of Part IX of that Act, the
    person may select for the purposes of this Act fiscal months that
    meet the requirements set out in that subsection 243(2); and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if neither paragraph (a) nor paragraph (b) applies,
    each calendar month is a fiscal month of the person for the purposes
    of this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fiscal months --- cannabis or vaping
  product licensee

  [(1.01)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, the fiscal months of
  a cannabis licensee or a vaping product licensee are calendar months.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination of fiscal half-years

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} The fiscal half-years of a person shall be
  determined in accordance with the following rules:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the period beginning on the first day of the first
    fiscal month in a fiscal year of the person and ending on the
    earlier of the last day of the sixth fiscal month and the last day
    in the fiscal year is a fiscal half-year of the person; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the period, if any, beginning on the first day of
    the seventh fiscal month and ending on the last day in the fiscal
    year of the person is a fiscal half-year of the person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification of Minister

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who is required to file a return shall
  notify the Minister of their fiscal months in the prescribed form and
  manner.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 159
- 2010, c. 25, s. 110
- 2018, c. 12, s. 74
- [2022, c. 10, s. 60](#1356388-1366855){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356388-1366855.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-159-20180621.html "Link to previous version of section 159"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Reporting Periods]{.HTitleText2} {#h-180677 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reporting period --- general

- **[[159.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-159.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this section and section
  159.2, the reporting period of a person is a fiscal month.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reporting period --- semi-annual

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On application in the prescribed form and manner by a
  person, the Minister may, in writing, authorize the reporting period
  of that person to be a fiscal half-year in a particular fiscal year if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person is a licensee in one or more of the
    following categories:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an excise warehouse licensee who does not hold in
      their excise warehouse manufactured tobacco or cigars,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a spirits licensee,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a wine licensee, or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a licensed user;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person has been licensed for a period exceeding
    twelve consecutive fiscal months;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a category, the total of all duties
    payable under Part 4 by the person and any person associated with
    the person in the fiscal year ending immediately before the
    particular fiscal year did not exceed \$120,000;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a category, the total of all duties
    payable under Part 4 by the person and any person associated with
    the person in the particular fiscal year does not exceed \$120,000;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the person is an excise warehouse
    licensee, the liability of the person and any excise warehouse
    licensee associated with the person with respect to duty on alcohol
    entered into an excise warehouse

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} did not exceed \$120,000 in the fiscal year
      ending immediately before the particular fiscal year, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} does not exceed \$120,000 in the particular
      fiscal year;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the person is a licensed user,
    the liability of the person and any licensed user associated with
    the person with respect to duty on alcohol entered into their
    specified premises

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} did not exceed \$120,000 in the fiscal year
      ending immediately before the particular fiscal year, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} does not exceed \$120,000 in the particular
      fiscal year;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the volume of absolute ethyl alcohol added to the
    bulk spirits inventory of the person that is a spirits licensee and
    any spirits licensee associated with the person did not exceed in
    the fiscal year ending immediately before the particular fiscal
    year, and does not exceed in the particular fiscal year, the amount
    determined by the formula

    ::: {#180698 .Paragraph}
    A / B

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is \$120,000, and

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is the rate of duty on spirits set out in section 1 of Schedule
        4;
    :::

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the volume of wine added to the bulk wine inventory
    of the person that is a wine licensee and any wine licensee
    associated with the person did not exceed in the fiscal year ending
    immediately before the particular fiscal year, and does not exceed
    in the particular fiscal year, the amount determined by the formula

    ::: {#180703 .Paragraph}
    A / B

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is \$120,000, and

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is the rate of duty on wine set out in paragraph (c) of Schedule
        6; and
    :::

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person is in compliance with the Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed revocation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An authorization under subsection (2) is deemed to be
  revoked if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any of the conditions described in paragraphs
    (2)(d) to (h) is no longer met in respect of the person, which
    revocation is effective as of the first day after the end of the
    fiscal half-year in which the condition is no longer met; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an excise warehouse licensee holds in their excise
    warehouse manufactured tobacco or cigars, which revocation is
    effective as of the first day of the fiscal month in which the
    licensee begins to hold the tobacco or cigars.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation --- other

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may revoke an authorization if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person requests in writing the Minister to do
    so;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person fails to comply with the Act; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Minister considers that the authorization is no
    longer required.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of revocation

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister revokes an authorization under
  subsection (4), the Minister shall send a notice in writing of the
  revocation to the person and shall specify in the notice the fiscal
  month for which the revocation becomes effective.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed reporting period on revocation

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a revocation under paragraph (3)(b) or
  subsection (4) becomes effective before the last day of a fiscal
  half-year of a person that is authorized under subsection (2), the
  period beginning on the first day of the fiscal half-year and ending
  immediately before the first day of the fiscal month for which the
  revocation becomes effective is deemed to be a reporting period of the
  person.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2010, c. 25, s. 111
- [2022, c. 19, s. 98](#1383953-1385790){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1383953-1385790.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-159.1-20101215.html "Link to previous version of section 159.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[calendar
quarter]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[159.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-159.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[calendar
  quarter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a period of three months beginning
  on the first day of January, April, July or October.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reporting period --- calendar quarters

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On application by a cannabis licensee, the Minister
  may, in writing, authorize the reporting periods of the cannabis
  licensee to be calendar quarters, beginning on the first day of a
  calendar quarter.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application --- form and filing

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An application made by a cannabis licensee under
  subsection (2) is to be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} made in prescribed form containing prescribed
    information; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} filed with the Minister in prescribed manner on or
    before the last day of the first month of the calendar quarter for
    which the authorization is to take effect or any later day that the
    Minister may allow.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2024, c. 15, s. 155\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation by Minister

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may revoke an authorization under
  subsection (2) in respect of a cannabis licensee if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cannabis licensee requests in writing that the
    Minister do so;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the cannabis licensee fails to comply with the Act;
    or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Minister considers that the authorization is no
    longer required.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of revocation

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister revokes an authorization in respect
  of a cannabis licensee, the following rules apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Minister shall send a notice in writing of the
    revocation to the cannabis licensee and shall specify in the notice
    the fiscal month of the cannabis licensee for which the revocation
    becomes effective; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the revocation becomes effective before the last
    day of a calendar quarter, the period beginning on the first day of
    the calendar quarter and ending immediately before the first day of
    that fiscal month is deemed to be a reporting period of the cannabis
    licensee.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2024, c. 15, s. 155\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 19, s. 99](#1383958-1385814){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1383958-1385814.html"
  role="button"}
- [2024, c. 15, s. 155](#1462483-1484266){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1462483-1484266.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-159.2-20221215.html "Link to previous version of section 159.2"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Returns and Payment of Duty and Other Amounts]{.HTitleText2} {#h-180723 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Filing by licensee

**[[160]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-160 .sectionLabel}** Every person who is
licensed under this Act shall, not later than the last day of the first
month after each reporting period of the person,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} file a return with the Minister, in the prescribed
  form and manner, for that reporting period;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} calculate, in the return, the total amount of the
  duty payable, if any, by the person for that reporting period; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} pay that amount to the Receiver General.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 160
- 2007, c. 18, s. 107
- 2010, c. 25, s. 112
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-160-20070622.html "Link to previous version of section 160"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Filing by other persons

**[[161]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-161 .sectionLabel}** Every person who is not
licensed under this Act and who is required to pay duty under this Act
shall, not later than the last day of the first month after the fiscal
month of the person in which the duty became payable,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} file a return with the Minister, in the prescribed
  form and manner, for that fiscal month;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} calculate, in the return, the total amount of the
  duty payable by the person for that fiscal month; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} pay that amount to the Receiver General.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Set-off of refunds

**[[162]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-162 .sectionLabel}** If, at any time, a
person files a return in which the person reports an amount that is
required to be paid under this Act by them and the person claims a
refund payable to them at that time, in the return or in another return,
or in a separate application filed under this Act with the return, the
person is deemed to have paid at that time, and the Minister is deemed
to have refunded at that time, an amount equal to the lesser of the
amount required to be paid and the amount of the refund.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[electronic
payment]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[163]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-163 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section, [[electronic payment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any
  payment to the Receiver General that is made through electronic
  services offered by a person described in any of paragraphs (2)(a)
  to (e) or by any electronic means specified by the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Electronic payment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person that is required under this Act to pay
  any duty, interest or other amount to the Receiver General shall, if
  the amount is \$10,000 or more, make the payment by way of electronic
  payment, unless the person cannot reasonably pay the amount in that
  manner, to the account of the Receiver General at or through

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a bank;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an [[authorized foreign
    bank]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in section 2 of the [Bank
    Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01), that is not subject to the restrictions and
    requirements referred to in subsection 524(2) of that Act;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a credit union;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a corporation that is authorized under the laws of
    Canada or a province to carry on the business of offering its
    services as a trustee to the public; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a corporation that is authorized under the laws of
    Canada or a province to accept deposits from the public and that
    carries on the business of lending money on the security of real
    property or immovables or investing in mortgages on real property or
    hypothecs on immovables.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 163
- [2023, c. 26, s. 89](#1409137-1437405){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1409137-1437405.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-163-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 163"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authority for separate returns

- **[[164]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-164 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  licensee who engages in one or more activities in separate branches or
  divisions may file an application, in the prescribed form and manner,
  with the Minister for authority to file separate returns and
  applications for refunds under this Act in respect of a branch or
  division specified in the application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorization by Minister

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of the application, the Minister may, in
  writing, authorize the licensee to file separate returns and
  applications for refunds in relation to the specified branch or
  division, subject to any conditions that the Minister may at any time
  impose, if the Minister is satisfied that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the branch or division can be separately identified
    by reference to the location of the branch or division or the nature
    of the activities engaged in by it; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} separate records, books of account and accounting
    systems are maintained in respect of the branch or division.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation of authorization

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may revoke an authorization if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the licensee, in writing, requests the Minister to
    revoke the authorization;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the licensee fails to comply with any condition
    imposed in respect of the authorization or any provision of this
    Act;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Minister is no longer satisfied that the
    requirements of subsection (2) in respect of the licensee are met;
    or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the Minister considers that the authorization is no
    longer required.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of revocation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister revokes an authorization, the
  Minister shall send a notice in writing of the revocation to the
  licensee and shall specify in the notice the effective date of the
  revocation.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amounts owing of \$2 or less in total

- **[[165]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-165 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the Minister determines, at any time, that the total of all amounts
  owing by a person to Her Majesty under this Act does not exceed two
  dollars, those amounts are deemed to be nil.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amounts payable of \$2 or less in total

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If, at any time, the total of all amounts payable by
  the Minister to a person under this Act does not exceed two dollars,
  the Minister may apply those amounts against any amount owing, at that
  time, by the person to Her Majesty. However, if the person, at that
  time, does not owe any amount to Her Majesty, those amounts are deemed
  to be nil.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 165
- 2003, c. 15, s. 91
- 2006, c. 4, s. 113
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-165-20030619.html "Link to previous version of section 165"){.PITLink}
:::

Meaning of [[electronic filing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

- **[[166]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-166 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of this section, [[electronic
  filing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means using electronic media in a manner
  specified in writing by the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Filing of return by electronic filing

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person who is required to file with the Minister a
  return under this Act, and who meets the criteria specified in writing
  by the Minister for the purposes of this section, may file the return
  by way of electronic filing.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed filing

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, if a person files a
  return by way of electronic filing, it is deemed to be a return in the
  prescribed form filed with the Minister on the day the Minister
  acknowledges acceptance of it.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Execution of returns, etc.

**[[167]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-167 .sectionLabel}** A return, other than a
return filed by way of electronic filing under section 166, a
certificate or other document made by a person, other than an
individual, under this Act shall be signed on behalf of the person by an
individual duly authorized to do so by the person or the governing body
of the person. If the person is a corporation or an association or
organization that has duly elected or appointed officers, the president,
vice-president, secretary and treasurer, or other equivalent officers,
of the corporation, association or organization, are deemed to be so
duly authorized.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of time

- **[[168]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-168 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} At
  any time, the Minister may, in writing, extend the time limited under
  this Act for a person to file a return or provide information.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of extension

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister extends the time under subsection
  (1),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the return shall be filed, or the information shall
    be provided, within the time so extended;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any duty payable that the person is required to
    report in the return shall be paid within the time so extended;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any interest payable under section 170 on any
    amount payable in respect of the return shall be calculated as
    though the amount were required to be paid on the day on which the
    extended time expires; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any penalty payable under section 251.1 in respect
    of the return shall be calculated as though the return were required
    to be filed on the day on which the extended time expires.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 168
- 2006, c. 4, s. 114
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-168-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 168"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Demand for return

**[[169]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-169 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may, on
demand sent by the Minister, require any person to file, within any
reasonable time stipulated in the demand, a return under this Act for
any period designated in the demand.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 169
- 2012, c. 19, s. 46
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-169-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 169"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Interest]{.HTitleText2} {#h-180791 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compound interest on amounts not paid
when required

- **[[170]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-170 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  person fails to pay an amount to the Receiver General as and when
  required under this Act, the person shall pay to the Receiver General
  interest on the amount. The interest shall be compounded daily at the
  prescribed rate and computed for the period beginning on the first day
  after the day on which the amount was required to be paid and ending
  on the day the amount is paid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment of interest that is compounded

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), interest that is
  compounded on a particular day on an unpaid amount of a person is
  deemed to be required to be paid by the person to the Receiver General
  at the end of the particular day, and, if the person has not paid the
  interest so computed by the end of the day after the particular day,
  the interest shall be added to the unpaid amount at the end of the
  particular day.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period where interest not payable

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite any other provision of this Act, if the
  Minister notifies a person that the person is required to pay a
  specified amount under this Act and the person pays the specified
  amount in full before the end of the period that the Minister
  specifies with the notice, interest is not payable on the specified
  amount for the period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minimum interest and penalty

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If, at any time, a person pays an amount not less
  than the total of all amounts, other than interest and penalty payable
  under section 251.1, owing at that time to Her Majesty under this Act
  for a reporting period of the person and the total amount of interest
  and the penalty payable by the person under this Act for that period
  is not more than \$25.00, the Minister may waive the total amount.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2003, c. 15, s. 92\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 170
- 2003, c. 15, s. 92
- 2006, c. 4, s. 115
- 2010, c. 25, s. 113
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-170-20060622.html "Link to previous version of section 170"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compound interest on amounts payable by
Minister

**[[171]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-171 .sectionLabel}** Interest shall be
compounded daily at the prescribed rate on any amount payable by the
Minister to a person. The interest shall be computed for the period
beginning on the first day after the day on which the amount is required
to be paid and ending on the day on which the amount is paid or is
applied against an amount owed by the person to Her Majesty, unless this
Act provides otherwise.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 171
- 2003, c. 15, s. 93
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-171-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 171"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of interest provisions

**[[172]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-172 .sectionLabel}** For greater certainty,
if an amendment to this Act, or an amendment or enactment that relates
to this Act, comes into force on, or applies as of, a particular day
that is before the day on which the amendment or enactment is assented
to or promulgated, the provisions of this Act and of the [Customs
Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), as the case may be, that relate to the
calculation and payment of interest apply in respect of the amendment or
enactment as though it had been assented to or promulgated on the
particular day.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 172
- [2019, c 29, s. 82](#1155548-1173540){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1155548-1173540.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-172-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 172"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Waiving or reducing interest

**[[173]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-173 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may, on or
before the particular day that is 10 calendar years after the day an
amount was required to be paid by a person under this Act, or on
application by the person on or before the particular day, waive or
reduce any interest on the amount payable by the person under section
170.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 173
- 2006, c. 4, s. 116
- 2007, c. 18, s. 108
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-173-20070401.html "Link to previous version of section 173"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Refunds]{.HTitleText2} {#h-180814 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statutory recovery rights

**[[174]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-174 .sectionLabel}** Except as specifically
provided under this Act, the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), the
[Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) or the [Financial Administration
Act](/eng/acts/F-11), no person has a right to recover any money paid to
Her Majesty as or on account of, or that has been taken into account by
Her Majesty as, duty, interest or other amount payable under this Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Applications for refunds

- **[[175]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-175 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application for a refund under this Act shall be filed with the
  Minister in the prescribed form and manner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Single application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Only one application may be made under this Act for a
  refund with respect to any matter.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment if error

- **[[176]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-176 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  person has paid an amount as or on account of, or that was taken into
  account as, duty, interest or other amount payable under this Act in
  circumstances in which the amount was not payable by the person,
  whether the amount was paid by mistake or otherwise, the Minister
  shall pay a refund of that amount to the person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A refund in respect of an amount shall not be paid to
  a person to the extent that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount was taken into account as duty for a
    reporting period of the person and the Minister has assessed the
    person for the period under section 188; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amount paid was duty, interest or other amount
    assessed under that section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for refund

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A refund of an amount shall not be paid to a person
  unless the person files an application for the refund within two years
  after the person paid the amount.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 176
- 2010, c. 25, s. 114
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-176-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 176"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on refunds, etc.

**[[177]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-177 .sectionLabel}** A refund or a payment
of an amount under this Act shall not be paid to a person to the extent
that it can reasonably be regarded that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount has previously been refunded, remitted or
  paid to that person, or applied against an amount owed by the person
  to Her Majesty, under this or any other Act of Parliament; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person has applied for a refund, payment or
  remission of the amount under any other Act of Parliament.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 177
- 2007, c. 18, s. 109
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-177-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 177"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction re trustees

**[[178]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-178 .sectionLabel}** If a trustee is
appointed under the [Bankruptcy and Insolvency Act](/eng/acts/B-3) to
act in the administration of the estate of a bankrupt, a refund or any
other payment under this Act that the bankrupt was entitled to claim
before the appointment shall not be paid after the appointment unless
all returns required under this Act to be filed for reporting periods of
the bankrupt ending before the appointment have been filed and all
amounts required under this Act to be paid by the bankrupt in respect of
those reporting periods have been paid.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 178
- 2010, c. 25, s. 115
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-178-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 178"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Overpayment of refunds, etc.

- **[[179]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-179 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  an amount is paid to, or applied to a liability of, a person as a
  refund or other payment under this Act and the person is not entitled
  to the amount or the amount paid or applied exceeds the refund or
  other payment to which the person is entitled, the person shall pay to
  the Receiver General an amount equal to the refund, payment or excess
  on the day the amount is paid to, or applied to a liability of, the
  person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of reduction of refund, etc.

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), if a refund or
  other payment has been paid to a person in excess of the amount to
  which the person was entitled and the amount of the excess has, by
  reason of section 177, reduced the amount of any other refund or other
  payment to which the person would, but for the payment of the excess,
  be entitled, the person is deemed to have paid the amount of the
  reduction to the Receiver General.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No refund --- exportation

**[[180]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-180 .sectionLabel}** Subject to this Act,
the duty paid on any tobacco product, cannabis product, vaping product
or alcohol entered into the duty-paid market shall not be refunded on
the exportation of the tobacco product, cannabis product, vaping product
or alcohol.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 180
- 2018, c. 12, s. 75
- [2022, c. 10, s. 61](#1356393-1366860){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356393-1366860.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-180-20180621.html "Link to previous version of section 180"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[180.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-180.1 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2014,
c. 20, s. 73\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-180.1-20140619.html "Link to previous version of section 180.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refund of duty  --- destroyed tobacco
products

- **[[181]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-181 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may refund to a tobacco licensee the duty paid on a tobacco
  product that is re-worked or destroyed by the tobacco licensee in
  accordance with section 41 if the licensee applies for the refund
  within two years after the tobacco product is re-worked or destroyed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refund of inventory tax  --- destroyed
  domestic cigarettes

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may refund to a tobacco licensee the tax
  imposed and payable under Part 3.1 on taxed cigarettes, as defined in
  section 58.1, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the licensee provides evidence satisfactory to the
    Minister that the cigarettes were manufactured in Canada, that they
    were re-worked or destroyed by the licensee in accordance with
    section 41 and that either

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the cigarettes were taxed cigarettes of the
      licensee and the tax was paid by the licensee, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the cigarettes were taxed cigarettes of a
      particular person that is not the licensee, the tax was paid by
      that particular person and an amount equal to the tax was paid by
      the licensee to that particular person on account of that tax; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the licensee applies for the refund within two
    years after the taxed cigarettes are re-worked or destroyed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refund of inventory tax --- destroyed
  imported cigarettes

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may refund to a particular person the
  tax imposed and payable under Part 3.1 on taxed cigarettes, as defined
  in section 58.1, other than cigarettes in respect of which duty has
  been imposed under section 53, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the particular person provides evidence
    satisfactory to the Minister that the cigarettes were imported by
    the particular person, that they were destroyed by the particular
    person in accordance with the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) or the
    [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) and that either

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the cigarettes were taxed cigarettes of the
      particular person and the tax was paid by the particular person,
      or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the cigarettes were taxed cigarettes of another
      person that is not the particular person, the tax was paid by the
      other person and an amount equal to the tax was paid by the
      particular person to the other person on account of that tax; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the particular person applies for the refund within
    two years after the taxed cigarettes are destroyed.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 181
- 2014, c. 39, s. 100
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-181-20141216.html "Link to previous version of section 181"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refund of duty --- destroyed imported
tobacco

- **[[181.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-181.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may refund to a duty
  free shop licensee the duty under section 53 that was paid on imported
  manufactured tobacco that is destroyed by the licensee in accordance
  with the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) if the licensee applies for
  the refund within two years after the tobacco is destroyed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refund of inventory tax --- destroyed
  imported cigarettes

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may refund to a duty free shop licensee
  the tax imposed and payable under Part 3.1 on taxed cigarettes, as
  defined in section 58.1, of the licensee in respect of which duty has
  been imposed under section 53, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the licensee provides evidence satisfactory to the
    Minister that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the cigarettes were taxed cigarettes of the
      licensee and the tax was paid by the licensee, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the cigarettes were destroyed by the licensee in
      accordance with the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the licensee applies for the refund within two
    years after the cigarettes are destroyed.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 18, s. 110
- 2014, c. 39, s. 101
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-181.1-20070622.html "Link to previous version of section 181.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refund of tax to importer if foreign
taxes paid

- **[[182]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-182 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may refund, to a person who has imported into a foreign
  country a tobacco product (as defined in section 55) that was
  manufactured in Canada and exported by the tobacco licensee who
  manufactured it to the foreign country in accordance with paragraph
  50(4)(a), an amount determined in accordance with subsection (2) in
  respect of the product if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person provides evidence satisfactory to the
    Minister that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} all taxes and duties imposed on the product under
      the laws of the foreign country having national application have
      been paid, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the container containing the product has printed
      on it or affixed to it tobacco markings; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person applies to the Minister for the refund
    within two years after the product was exported to the foreign
    country.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination of refund

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The amount of the refund under subsection (1) is
  equal to the lesser of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the total of the taxes and duties referred to in
    subparagraph (1)(a)(i) that are paid in respect of the tobacco
    product, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the special duty imposed under
    paragraph 56(1)(a) in respect of the product that is paid by the
    tobacco licensee who manufactured it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Overpayment of refund or interest

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If an amount has been paid to a person as a refund in
  respect of a tobacco product exported by the tobacco licensee who
  manufactured it or as interest in respect of such a refund and a
  special duty under paragraph 56(1)(b) was imposed in respect of the
  product, the amount is deemed to be duty payable under this Act by the
  licensee that became payable during the fiscal month of the licensee
  in which the amount was paid to the person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refund of special duty to tobacco
  licensee if foreign taxes paid

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a refund under subsection (1) has been paid in
  respect of an exported tobacco product, the Minister may refund to the
  tobacco licensee who manufactured the product the amount, if any, by
  which the special duty imposed under paragraph 56(1)(a) in respect of
  the product and paid by the licensee exceeds the amount of the refund
  under subsection (1) if the licensee applies to the Minister for the
  refund under this subsection within two years after the product was
  exported.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refund of special duty to duty free shop
licensee

- **[[183]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-183 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  duty free shop licensee who holds a licence issued under section 22
  sells, in accordance with the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6),
  imported manufactured tobacco to an individual who is not a resident
  of Canada and who is about to depart Canada, the Minister may refund
  to the licensee the special duty paid under section 53 in respect of
  that portion of the total quantity of the tobacco exported by the
  individual on their departure that does not exceed

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of cigarettes, 200 cigarettes;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of tobacco sticks, 200 sticks; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of manufactured tobacco other than
    cigarettes and tobacco sticks, 200 g.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No refund shall be paid to a duty free shop licensee
  in respect of a sale of imported manufactured tobacco unless the
  licensee applies to the Minister for the refund within two years after
  the sale.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment if bad debt

- **[[184]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-184 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  an [ad valorem]{.otherLang lang="la"} duty under section 43 has been
  paid by a tobacco licensee in respect of an arm's length sale of
  cigars and the licensee has established that any debt owing to the
  licensee in respect of the sale has become in whole or in part a bad
  debt and has accordingly written off all or part of the debt as a bad
  debt in the licensee's books of account, an amount equal to the
  proportion of the amount of that duty that the amount of the debt
  written off is of the price for which the cigars were sold may,
  subject to this Act, be paid to that licensee if the licensee applies
  for a refund of the amount within two years after the end of the
  licensee's fiscal month during which the debt was so written off.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recovery of payment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a tobacco licensee recovers all or any part of a
  debt in respect of which an amount is paid to the licensee under
  subsection (1) (in this subsection referred to as the "refunded
  amount"), that licensee shall immediately pay to the Receiver General
  an amount equal to the proportion of the refunded amount that the
  amount of the debt so recovered is of the amount of the debt written
  off in respect of which the refunded amount was paid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[arm's length
  sale]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[arm's length
  sale]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in respect of cigars means a sale of cigars
  by a tobacco licensee to a person with whom the licensee is dealing at
  arm's length at the time of the sale.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refund --- imported bulk spirits

- **[[185]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-185 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  imported bulk spirits on which special duty has been paid are returned
  by a licensed user to the spirits licensee who supplied them to the
  licensed user, the Minister may refund the duty to the spirits
  licensee who paid the duty if the licensee applies for the refund
  within two years after the spirits are returned.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refund --- packaged imported spirits

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If imported spirits that are packaged and on which
  special duty has been paid are returned under prescribed conditions by
  a licensed user to the excise warehouse of the excise warehouse
  licensee who supplied them to the licensed user, the Minister may
  refund the duty to the excise warehouse licensee who paid the duty if
  the excise warehouse licensee applies for the refund within two years
  after the spirits are returned.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refund --- alcohol returned to warehouse

**[[186]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-186 .sectionLabel}** If packaged alcohol
that has been removed from the excise warehouse of an excise warehouse
licensee for entry into the duty-paid market is returned in accordance
with section 152 to the warehouse, the Minister may refund to the
licensee the duty paid on the alcohol if the licensee applies for the
refund within two years after the alcohol is returned.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refund --- alcohol in special container

**[[187]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-187 .sectionLabel}** If a marked special
container of alcohol is returned to the excise warehouse licensee who
paid duty on the alcohol, the Minister may refund to the licensee the
duty on the alcohol remaining in the container when it is returned if
the licensee

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} destroys the alcohol in the manner approved by the
  Minister; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} applies for the refund within two years after the
  container is returned.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refund of duty --- destroyed cannabis

**[[187.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-187.1 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may
refund to a cannabis licensee the duty paid on a cannabis product that
is re-worked or destroyed by the cannabis licensee in accordance with
section 158.16 if the cannabis licensee applies for the refund within
two years after the cannabis product is re-worked or destroyed.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 76
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refund of duty --- destroyed vaping
product

**[[187.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-187.2 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may
refund to a vaping product licensee the duty paid on a vaping product
that is re-worked or destroyed by the licensee in accordance with
section 158.53 if the licensee applies for the refund within two years
after the vaping product is re-worked or destroyed.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 62](#1356395-1366864){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356395-1366864.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Assessments]{.HTitleText2} {#h-180926 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assessments

- **[[188]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-188 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may assess

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the duty payable by a person for a reporting period
    of the person; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to section 190, interest and any other
    amount payable by a person under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reassessment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may reassess or make an additional
  assessment of any duty, interest or other amount that may be assessed
  under subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Allowance of unclaimed amounts

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If, in assessing the duty, interest or other amount
  payable by a person for a reporting period of the person or other
  amount payable by a person under this Act, the Minister determines
  that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a refund would have been payable to the person if
    it had been claimed in an application under this Act filed on the
    particular day that is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the assessment is in respect of duty payable
      for the reporting period, the day on which the return for the
      period was required to be filed, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the assessment is in respect of interest or
      other amount, the day on which the interest or other amount became
      payable by the person,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the refund was not claimed by the person in an
    application filed before the day on which notice of the assessment
    is sent to the person, and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the refund would be payable to the person if it
    were claimed in an application under this Act filed on the day on
    which notice of the assessment is sent to the person or would be
    disallowed if it were claimed in that application only because the
    period for claiming the refund expired before that day,

  the Minister shall apply all or part of the refund against that duty,
  interest or other amount that is payable as if the person had, on the
  particular day, paid the amount so applied on account of that duty,
  interest or other amount.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of overpayment

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If, in assessing the duty payable by a person for a
  reporting period of the person, the Minister determines that there is
  an overpayment of duty payable for the period, unless the assessment
  is made in the circumstances described in paragraph 191(4)(a) or (b)
  after the time otherwise limited for the assessment under paragraph
  191(1)(a), the Minister shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} apply

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} all or part of the overpayment

    against

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any amount (in this paragraph referred to as the
      "outstanding amount") that, on the particular day on which the
      person was required to file a return for the period, the person
      defaulted in paying under this Act and that remains unpaid on the
      day on which notice of the assessment is sent to the person,

    as if the person had, on the particular day, paid the amount so
    applied on account of the outstanding amount;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} apply

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} all or part of the overpayment that was not
      applied under paragraph (a) together with interest on the
      overpayment at the prescribed rate, computed for the period
      beginning on the day that is 30 days after the latest of

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the particular day,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the return for the reporting
        period was filed, and

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an overpayment that is
        attributable to a payment made on a day subsequent to the days
        referred to in clauses (A) and (B), that subsequent day,

      and ending on the day on which the person defaulted in paying the
      outstanding amount referred to in subparagraph (ii)

    against

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any amount (in this paragraph referred to as the
      "outstanding amount") that, on a day (in this paragraph referred
      to as the "later day") after the particular day, the person
      defaulted in paying under this Act and that remains unpaid on the
      day on which notice of the assessment is sent to the person,

    as if the person had, on the later day, paid the amount and interest
    so applied on account of the outstanding amount; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} refund to the person the part of the overpayment
    that was not applied under paragraphs (a) and (b) together with
    interest on the refund at the prescribed rate, computed for the
    period beginning on the day that is 30 days after the latest of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the particular day,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the return for the reporting
      period was filed, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an overpayment that is
      attributable to a payment made on a day subsequent to the days
      referred to in subparagraphs (i) and (ii), that subsequent day,

    and ending on the day on which the refund is paid to the person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of payment

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If, in assessing the duty payable by a person for a
  reporting period of the person or an amount (in this subsection
  referred to as the "overdue amount") payable by a person under this
  Act, all or part of a refund is not applied under subsection (3)
  against that duty payable or overdue amount, except if the assessment
  is made in the circumstances described in paragraph 191(4)(a) or (b)
  after the time otherwise limited for the assessment under paragraph
  191(1)(a), the Minister shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} apply

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} all or part of the refund that was not applied
      under subsection (3)

    against

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any other amount (in this paragraph referred to
      as the "outstanding amount") that, on the particular day that is

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} if the assessment is in respect of duty payable
        for the reporting period, the day on which the return for the
        period was required to be filed, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} if the assessment is in respect of an overdue
        amount, the day on which the overdue amount became payable by
        the person,

      the person defaulted in paying under this Act and that remains
      unpaid on the day on which notice of the assessment is sent to the
      person,

    as if the person had, on the particular day, paid the refund so
    applied on account of the outstanding amount;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} apply

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} all or part of the refund that was not applied
      under subsection (3) or paragraph (a) together with interest on
      the refund at the prescribed rate, computed for the period
      beginning on the day that is 30 days after the later of

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the particular day, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} if the assessment is in respect of duty payable
        for the reporting period, the day on which the return for the
        period was filed,

      and ending on the day on which the person defaulted in paying the
      outstanding amount referred to in subparagraph (ii)

    against

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any amount (in this paragraph referred to as the
      "outstanding amount") that, on a day (in this paragraph referred
      to as the "later day") after the particular day, the person
      defaulted in paying under this Act and that remains unpaid on the
      day on which notice of the assessment is sent to the person,

    as if the person had, on the later day, paid the refund and interest
    so applied on account of the outstanding amount; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} refund to the person the part of the refund that
    was not applied under any of subsection (3) and paragraphs (a)
    and (b) together with interest on the refund at the prescribed rate,
    computed for the period beginning on the day that is 30 days after
    the later of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the particular day, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the assessment is in respect of duty payable
      for the reporting period, the day on which the return for the
      period was filed,

    and ending on the day on which the refund is paid to the person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation on refunding overpayments

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} An overpayment of duty payable for a reporting period
  of a person and interest on the overpayment shall not be applied under
  paragraph (4)(b) or refunded under paragraph (4)(c) unless the person
  has, before the day on which notice of the assessment is sent to the
  person, filed all returns and other records of which the Minister has
  knowledge and that the person was required to file with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Minister under this Act, the [Excise
    Act](/eng/acts/E-14), the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15), the
    [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3), the [Air Travellers Security
    Charge Act](/eng/acts/A-10.5), the [Underused Housing Tax
    Act](/eng/acts/U-0.5), the [Select Luxury Items Tax
    Act](/eng/acts/S-8.35), the [Digital Services Tax
    Act](/eng/acts/D-1.65) and the [Global Minimum Tax
    Act](/eng/acts/G-3.3); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Minister of Public Safety and Emergency
    Preparedness under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} A refund or a part of the refund that was not applied
  under subsection (3) and interest on the refund under paragraphs
  (5)(b) and (c)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} shall not be applied under paragraph (5)(b) against
    an amount (in this paragraph referred to as the "outstanding
    amount") that is payable by a person unless the refund would have
    been payable to the person as a refund if the person had claimed it
    in an application under this Act filed on the day on which the
    person defaulted in paying the outstanding amount and, in the case
    of a payment under section 176, if that section allowed the person
    to claim the payment within four years after the person paid the
    amount in respect of which the payment would be so payable; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} shall not be refunded under paragraph (5)(c) unless

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the refund would have been payable to the person
      as a refund if the person had claimed it in an application under
      this Act filed on the day on which notice of the assessment is
      sent to the person, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the person has, before the day on which notice
      of the assessment is sent to the person, filed all returns and
      other records of which the Minister has knowledge and that the
      person was required to file with

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the Minister under this Act, the [Excise
        Act](/eng/acts/E-14), the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15), the
        [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3), the [Air Travellers Security
        Charge Act](/eng/acts/A-10.5), the [Underused Housing Tax
        Act](/eng/acts/U-0.5), the [Select Luxury Items Tax
        Act](/eng/acts/S-8.35), the [Digital Services Tax
        Act](/eng/acts/D-1.65) and the [Global Minimum Tax
        Act](/eng/acts/G-3.3), or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the Minister of Public Safety and Emergency
        Preparedness under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed claim or application

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} If, in assessing any duty, interest or other amount
  payable by a person under this Act, the Minister applies or refunds an
  amount under subsection (3), (4) or (5),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person is deemed to have claimed the amount in
    a return or application filed under this Act; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to the extent that an amount is applied against any
    duty, interest or other amount payable by the person, the Minister
    is deemed to have refunded or paid the amount to the person and the
    person is deemed to have paid the duty, interest or other amount
    payable against which it was applied.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refund on reassessment

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} If a person has paid an amount on account of any
  duty, interest or other amount assessed under this section in respect
  of a reporting period and the amount paid exceeds the amount
  determined on reassessment to have been payable by the person, the
  Minister may refund to the person the amount of the excess, together
  with interest on the excess amount at the prescribed rate for the
  period that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} begins on the day that is 30 days after the latest
    of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the person was required to file
      a return for the reporting period,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the person filed a return for
      the reporting period, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the amount was paid by the
      person; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} ends on the day on which the refund is paid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refund of interest or penalty

  [(9.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (9), if a person has paid an
  amount of interest or penalty and the Minister waives or reduces that
  amount under section 173 or 255.1, as the case may be, the Minister
  shall refund the amount of the waiver or reduction to the person,
  together with interest on the amount of the waiver or reduction at the
  prescribed rate for the period beginning on the day that is 30 days
  after the day on which the Minister received a request in a manner
  satisfactory to the Minister to apply that section and ending on the
  day on which the refund is paid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Meaning of [[overpayment of duty
  payable]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[overpayment of duty
  payable]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of a person for a reporting period of
  the person means the amount, if any, by which the total of all amounts
  paid by the person on account of duty payable for the period exceeds
  the total of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the duty payable for the period, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} all amounts paid to the person under this Act as a
    refund for the period.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assessment of refund

- **[[189]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-189 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On
  receipt of an application made by a person for a refund under this
  Act, the Minister shall, without delay, consider the application and
  assess the amount of the refund, if any, payable to the person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reassessment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may reassess or make an additional
  assessment of the amount of a refund despite any previous assessment
  of the amount of the refund.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If on assessment under this section the Minister
  determines that a refund is payable to a person, the Minister shall
  pay the refund to the person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A refund shall not be paid until the person has filed
  with the Minister or the Minister of Public Safety and Emergency
  Preparedness all returns and other records of which the Minister has
  knowledge and that are required to be filed under this Act, the
  [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14), the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15),
  the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), the [Income Tax
  Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3), the [Air Travellers Security Charge
  Act](/eng/acts/A-10.5), the [Underused Housing Tax
  Act](/eng/acts/U-0.5), the [Select Luxury Items Tax
  Act](/eng/acts/S-8.35), the [Digital Services Tax
  Act](/eng/acts/D-1.65) and the [Global Minimum Tax
  Act](/eng/acts/G-3.3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interest

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a refund is paid to a person, the Minister shall
  pay interest at the prescribed rate to the person on the refund for
  the period beginning on the day that is 30 days after the day on which
  the application for the refund is filed with the Minister and ending
  on the day on which the refund is paid.
