[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[coordinated cannabis duty
system]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[304.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-304.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[coordinated
  cannabis duty system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the system providing
  for the payment, collection and remittance of duty imposed under any
  of sections 158.2 and 158.22 and subsections 158.25(2) and 158.26(2)
  and any provisions relating to duty imposed under those provisions or
  to refunds in respect of any such duty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Coordinated cannabis duty system
  regulations --- transition

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations, in
  relation to the joining of a province to the coordinated cannabis duty
  system,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing transitional measures, including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a tax on the inventory of cannabis products held
      by a cannabis licensee or any other person, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a duty or tax on cannabis products that are
      delivered prior to the province joining that system; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} generally to effect the implementation of that
    system in relation to the province.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Coordinated cannabis duty system
  regulations --- rate variation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing rules in respect of whether, how and
    when a change in the rate of duty for a specified province applies
    (in this section any such change in the rate of duty is referred to
    as a "rate variation"), including rules deeming, in specified
    circumstances and for specified purposes, the status of anything to
    be different than what it would otherwise be, including when duty is
    imposed or payable and when duty is required to be reported and
    accounted for;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if a manner of determining an amount of duty is to
    be prescribed in relation to the coordinated cannabis duty system,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} specifying the circumstances or conditions under
      which a change in the manner applies, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} prescribing transitional measures in respect of
      a change in the manner, including

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} a tax on the inventory of cannabis products
        held by a cannabis licensee or any other person, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} a duty or tax on cannabis products that are
        delivered prior to the change; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} prescribing amounts and rates to be used to
    determine any refund that relates to, or is affected by, the
    coordinated cannabis duty system, excluding amounts that would
    otherwise be included in determining any such refund, and specifying
    circumstances under which any such refund shall not be paid or made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Coordinated cannabis duty system
  regulations --- general

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of facilitating the implementation,
  application, administration and enforcement of the coordinated
  cannabis duty system or a rate variation or the joining of a province
  to the coordinated cannabis duty system, the Governor in Council may
  make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing rules in respect of whether, how and
    when that system applies and rules in respect of other aspects
    relating to the application of that system in relation to a
    specified province, including rules deeming, in specified
    circumstances and for specified purposes, the status of anything to
    be different than what it would otherwise be, including when duty is
    imposed or payable and when duty is required to be reported and
    accounted for;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribing rules related to the movement of
    cannabis products between provinces, including a duty, tax or refund
    in respect of such movement;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} providing for refunds relating to the application
    of that system in relation to a specified province;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} adapting any provision of this Act or of the
    regulations made under this Act to the coordinated cannabis duty
    system or modifying any provision of this Act or those regulations
    to adapt it to the coordinated cannabis duty system;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} defining, for the purposes of this Act or the
    regulations made under this Act, or any provision of this Act or
    those regulations, in its application to the coordinated cannabis
    duty system, words or expressions used in this Act or those
    regulations including words or expressions defined in a provision of
    this Act or those regulations;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} providing that a provision of this Act or of the
    regulations made under this Act, or a part of such a provision, does
    not apply to the coordinated cannabis duty system;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} prescribing compliance measures, including
    penalties and anti-avoidance rules; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} generally in respect of the application of that
    system in relation to a province.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conflict

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a regulation made under this Act in respect of the
  coordinated cannabis duty system states that it applies despite any
  provision of this Act, in the event of a conflict between the
  regulation and this Act, the regulation prevails to the extent of the
  conflict.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 92
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[cannabis duty
system]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[304.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-304.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[cannabis duty
  system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the system providing for the
  payment, collection and remittance of duty imposed under Part 4.1 and
  any provisions relating to duty imposed under that Part or to refunds
  in respect of any such duty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional cannabis duty system
  regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of facilitating the implementation,
  application, administration or enforcement of the cannabis duty
  system, the Governor in Council may make regulations adapting any
  provision of this Act or of the regulations made under this Act to
  take into account the making of regulations under the [Cannabis
  Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5) or amendments to those regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retroactive effect

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection 304(2), regulations made under
  subsection (2) may, if they so provide, be retroactive and have effect
  with respect to any period before they are made.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 92
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[coordinated vaping duty
system]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[304.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-304.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[coordinated
  vaping duty system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the system providing
  for the payment, collection and remittance of duty imposed under any
  of section 158.58 and subsections 158.6(2) and 158.61(2) and any
  provisions relating to duty imposed under those provisions or to
  refunds in respect of any such duty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Coordinated vaping duty system
  regulations --- transition

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations, in
  relation to the joining of a province to the coordinated vaping duty
  system,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing transitional measures, including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a tax on the inventory of vaping products held by
      a vaping product licensee or any other person, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a duty or tax on vaping products that are
      delivered prior to the province joining that system; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} generally to effect the implementation of that
    system in relation to the province.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Coordinated vaping duty system
  regulations --- rate variation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing rules in respect of whether, how and
    when a change in the rate of duty for a specified vaping province
    applies (in this section any such change in the rate of duty is
    referred to as a "rate variation"), including rules deeming, in
    specified circumstances and for specified purposes, the status of
    anything to be different than what it would otherwise be, including
    when duty is imposed or payable and when duty is required to be
    reported and accounted for;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if a manner of determining an amount of duty is to
    be prescribed in relation to the coordinated vaping duty system,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} specifying the circumstances or conditions under
      which a change in the manner applies, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} prescribing transitional measures in respect of
      a change in the manner, including

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} a tax on the inventory of vaping products held
        by a vaping product licensee or any other person, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} a duty or tax on vaping products that are
        delivered prior to the change; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} prescribing amounts and rates to be used to
    determine any refund that relates to, or is affected by, the
    coordinated vaping duty system, excluding amounts that would
    otherwise be included in determining any such refund, and specifying
    circumstances under which any such refund shall not be paid or made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Coordinated vaping duty system
  regulations --- general

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of facilitating the implementation,
  application, administration and enforcement of the coordinated vaping
  duty system or a rate variation or the joining of a province to the
  coordinated vaping duty system, the Governor in Council may make
  regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing rules in respect of whether, how and
    when that system applies and rules in respect of other aspects
    relating to the application of that system in relation to a
    specified vaping province, including rules deeming, in specified
    circumstances and for specified purposes, the status of anything to
    be different than what it would otherwise be, including when duty is
    imposed or payable and when duty is required to be reported and
    accounted for;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribing rules related to the movement of vaping
    products between provinces, including a duty, tax or refund in
    respect of such movement;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} providing for refunds relating to the application
    of that system in relation to a specified vaping province;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} adapting any provision of this Act or of the
    regulations made under this Act to the coordinated vaping duty
    system or modifying any provision of this Act or those regulations
    to adapt it to the coordinated vaping duty system;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} defining, for the purposes of this Act or the
    regulations made under this Act, or any provision of this Act or
    those regulations, in its application to the coordinated vaping duty
    system, words or expressions used in this Act or those regulations
    including words or expressions defined in a provision of this Act or
    those regulations;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} providing that a provision of this Act or of the
    regulations made under this Act, or a part of such a provision, does
    not apply to the coordinated vaping duty system;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} prescribing compliance measures, including
    penalties and anti-avoidance rules; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} generally in respect of the application of that
    system in relation to a province.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conflict

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a regulation made under this Act in respect of the
  coordinated vaping duty system states that it applies despite any
  provision of this Act, in the event of a conflict between the
  regulation and this Act, the regulation prevails to the extent of the
  conflict.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 79](#1356452-1366941){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356452-1366941.html"
  role="button"}
:::

## [PART 8]{.HLabel1}[Transitional Provisions and Consequential, Related and Coordinating Amendments]{.HTitleText1} {#h-182661 .Part}

### [Transitional Provisions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-182662 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Meaning of [[implementation
date]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

**[[305]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-305 .sectionLabel}** In sections 306 to 320,
[[implementation date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the day on which Parts
3 and 4 come into force.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional treatment of duties on
packaged spirits

**[[306]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-306 .sectionLabel}** The following rules
apply to packaged spirits on which a duty, at a rate determined by the
application of section 1 of Part I of the schedule to the [Excise
Act](/eng/acts/E-14), was imposed under that Act or levied under the
[Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) but that had not become payable
before the implementation date:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} as of that day, the duty is relieved;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} as of that day, the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14)
  ceases to apply in respect of the spirits;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of imported packaged spirits that have
  not been released under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), this Act,
  the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) and the [Customs
  Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) apply in respect of them as though they
  were imported on that day; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of any other packaged spirits, this Act
  applies in respect of them as though

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} they were produced and packaged in Canada on that
    day by the person having possession of them immediately before that
    day and the person were permitted under this Act to produce and
    package them, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the spirits are in the possession of a duty
    free shop or an accredited representative or delivered as ships'
    stores in accordance with the [Ships' Stores
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-40), they had been entered into
    an excise warehouse and then removed from the warehouse in
    accordance with paragraph 147(1)(a) on that day.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional treatment of duties on bulk
spirits

- **[[307]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-307 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following rules apply to bulk spirits on which a duty, at a rate
  determined by the application of section 1 of Part I of the schedule
  to the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14), was imposed under that Act or
  levied under the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) but that had not
  become payable before the implementation date:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} as of that day, the duty is relieved;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} as of that day, the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14)
    ceases to apply in respect of the spirits;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of imported bulk spirits that have not
    been released under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), this Act,
    the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) and the [Customs
    Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) apply in respect of them as though they
    were imported on that day; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of any other bulk spirits, this Act
    applies in respect of them as though they were produced in Canada on
    that day by the person having possession of them immediately before
    that day.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional treatment of bulk spirits
  imported for bottling or blending

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The following rules apply to bulk spirits on which a
  duty, at a rate determined by the application of section 1 of Part I
  of the schedule to the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14), was levied under
  the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) and remitted under the
  [Distilled Spirits for Bottling in Bond Remission
  Order](/eng/regulations/SOR-97-321) or the [Imported Spirits for
  Blending Remission Order](/eng/regulations/SI-83-151) before the
  implementation date:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} as of that day, the duty imposed on the spirits
    under subsection 135(1) of the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14) when
    they were entered into a distillery is relieved;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} as of that day, the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14)
    ceases to apply in respect of the spirits; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} this Act applies in respect of them as though they
    were produced in Canada on that day by the person having possession
    of them immediately before that day.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional treatment of excise taxes on
wine

**[[308]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-308 .sectionLabel}** The following rules
apply to wine on which tax was imposed under section 27 of the [Excise
Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15) but had not become payable before the
implementation date:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} as of that day, the tax is relieved;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} as of that day, Parts III, VI and VII of the [Excise
  Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15) cease to apply in respect of the wine;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of imported wine that has not been
  released under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), this Act, the
  [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) and the [Customs
  Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) apply in respect of the wine as though it
  were imported on that day;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of bulk wine to which paragraph (c) does
  not apply, this Act applies in respect of it as though it were
  produced in Canada on that day

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the wine is located in a ferment-on-premises
    facility or at the residence of an individual, by the individual who
    owned the wine immediately before that day, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, by the person having possession
    of it immediately before that day; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of wine to which neither paragraph (c)
  nor (d) apply, this Act applies in respect of it as though

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} it were produced and packaged in Canada on that day
    by the person having possession of it immediately before that day
    and the person were permitted under this Act to produce and package
    it, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of wine in the possession of a duty
    free shop or an accredited representative or delivered as ships'
    stores in accordance with the [Ships' Stores
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-40), it had been entered into
    an excise warehouse and then removed from the warehouse in
    accordance with paragraph 147(1)(a) on that day.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional treatment of packaged wine
in inventory of small manufacturers

- **[[309]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-309 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  tax under Part IV of the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15) is not
  payable in respect of packaged wine because it was produced by a
  person who is exempt from payment of excise tax under the [Small
  Manufacturers or Producers Exemption
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-82-498), subsection 135(1) does not
  apply to the wine if it was packaged before the implementation date.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination of sales for transitional
  purposes

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the period beginning on the implementation date
  and ending on the day that is one year after that day, the words
  "products subject to duty under subsection (1), or would have been so
  subject to duty in the absence of this subsection," in paragraph
  135(2)(b) shall be read as "goods referred to in paragraph 2(1)(a) of
  the [Small Manufacturers or Producers Exemption
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-82-498)".

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of Act to tax-paid packaged
wine

- **[[310]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-310 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  packaged wine on which tax imposed under section 27 of the [Excise Tax
  Act](/eng/acts/E-15) became payable before the implementation date is
  entered into the excise warehouse of an excise warehouse licensee on
  or before the end of six months after that day, this Act applies in
  respect of the wine as though it were produced and packaged in Canada
  by the licensee and the licensee were permitted under this Act to
  produce and package it on the day it is entered into the warehouse.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refund of excise tax paid on packaged
  wine

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the tax imposed under section 27 of the [Excise
  Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15) in respect of the wine entered into the
  warehouse has been paid, the licensee may apply to the Minister for a
  refund of the tax.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No refund shall be paid under this section unless the
  application for the refund is filed with the Minister in the
  prescribed form and manner within one year after the implementation
  date.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of Act to tax-paid bulk wine

- **[[311]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-311 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  bulk wine on which tax imposed under section 27 of the [Excise Tax
  Act](/eng/acts/E-15) became payable before the implementation date is
  entered into the specified premises of a licensed user on that day,
  this Act applies in respect of the wine as though it were produced in
  Canada on that day by the user and the user were permitted to produce
  the wine.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refund of excise tax paid on bulk wine

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the tax imposed under section 27 of the [Excise
  Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15) in respect of the wine entered into the
  specified premises of a licensed user has been paid, the user may
  apply to the Minister for a refund of the tax.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No refund shall be paid under this section unless the
  application for the refund is filed with the Minister in the
  prescribed form and manner within one year after the implementation
  date.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[312]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-312 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  definitions in this subsection apply in this section.

  [[bonded manufacturer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[bonded manufacturer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who
      holds, before the implementation date, a licence under subsection
      182(1) of the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14). ([fabricant
      entrepositaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[licensed pharmacist]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[licensed pharmacist]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who
      holds, before the implementation date, a licence under subsection
      136(2) of the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14). ([pharmacien titulaire
      de licence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of Act to spirits in
  possession of bonded manufacturer or licensed pharmacist

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If, on the implementation date, a bonded manufacturer
  or a licensed pharmacist possesses, in accordance with their licence,
  spirits that were produced before that day, the following rules apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} as of that day, the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14)
    ceases to apply in respect of the spirits; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} this Act applies in respect of them as though

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of bulk spirits, the spirits were
      produced in Canada on that day by the manufacturer or the
      pharmacist and, if they are a licensed user, they were permitted
      to produce the spirits, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of packaged spirits, the spirits
      were produced and packaged in Canada on that day by the
      manufacturer or the pharmacist and they were permitted to produce
      and package the spirits.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refund of duty paid by bonded
  manufacturer or licensed pharmacist

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If, on the implementation date, a bonded manufacturer
  or licensed pharmacist possesses spirits on which duty at a rate
  determined by the application of subsection 1(2) or (3) of Part I of
  the schedule to the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14) was paid, the
  manufacturer or pharmacist may apply to the Minister for a refund of
  the duty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} No refund shall be paid under this section unless the
  application for the refund is filed with the Minister in the
  prescribed form and manner within one year after the implementation
  date.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of Act to spirits to be used
for scientific purposes

**[[313]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-313 .sectionLabel}** If a person described
by any of paragraphs 135(2)(a) to (d) of the [Excise
Act](/eng/acts/E-14) possesses, on the implementation date, spirits in
respect of which a drawback under subsection 135(2) of that Act is
granted at any time, the following rules apply:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} as of that day, the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14)
  ceases to apply in respect of the spirits;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} this Act applies in respect of them as though

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of bulk spirits, the spirits were
    produced in Canada on that day by the person and, if the person is a
    registered user, the person were permitted to produce the spirits,
    or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of packaged spirits,

    - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the spirits were produced and packaged in Canada
      on that day by the person,

    - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the person were permitted to produce and package
      the spirits, and

    - [(C)]{.lawlabel} if the person is a registered user, the spirits
      were, on that day, entered into an excise warehouse and then
      removed from the warehouse in accordance with subparagraph
      147(1)(a)(iii); and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the spirits are contained in a special container
  and the person is a registered user

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person shall, despite subsection 78(1), mark
    the container on that day, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the container is deemed to have been entered into
    an excise warehouse and then removed from the warehouse in
    accordance with paragraph 147(2)(a) on that day.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of Act to alcohol in
bottle-your-own premises

**[[314]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-314 .sectionLabel}** The following rules
apply to alcohol contained in a special container located on a person's
bottle-your-own premises on the implementation date:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person shall, despite subsections 78(1) and
  83(1), mark the container on that day;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of spirits, this Act applies in respect
  of them as though the duty, at a rate determined by the application of
  section 1 of Part I of the schedule to the [Excise
  Act](/eng/acts/E-14), that had become payable before that day in
  respect of them were imposed and, if the duty is paid, paid under this
  Act; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of wine,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of subsection 135(1), section 82
    does not apply to the marking of the container under paragraph (a),
    and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} this Act applies in respect of the wine as though
    the tax under section 27 of the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15)
    that had become payable before that day in respect of it were a duty
    that was imposed and, if the tax is paid, paid under this Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal of alcohol from customs bonded
warehouse

- **[[315]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  packaged alcohol is located in a customs bonded warehouse on the
  implementation date,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the alcohol shall be removed from the warehouse;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any duty on the alcohol that is imposed under this
    Act or levied under section 21.2 of the [Customs
    Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) as a result of the operation of section
    306 or 308 is payable on that day unless the alcohol is without
    delay entered into an excise warehouse.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply if the alcohol in the
  customs bonded warehouse is to be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} exported in accordance with this Act; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} delivered

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to an accredited representative for their
      personal or official use,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to a duty free shop for sale in accordance with
      the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6),

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} as ships' stores in accordance with the [Ships'
      Stores Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-40), or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} to an air carrier that is licensed under section
      69 or 73 of the [Canada Transportation Act](/eng/acts/C-10.4) to
      operate an international air service.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional application of Distillery
Regulations

- **[[315.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If, during the period beginning on
  July 1, 2003 and ending on July 1, 2009, sections 7, 8, 9, 12 and 15
  of the Distillery Regulations, C.R.C., c. 569, would have applied in
  any circumstance had those sections, as they read on June 30, 2003,
  been in force and section 1.1 of the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14) not
  been enacted, those sections apply, with any modifications that the
  circumstances require.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional application of Distillery
  Departmental Regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If, during the period beginning on July 1, 2003 and
  ending on July 1, 2009, sections 13 and 14 of the Distillery
  Departmental Regulations, C.R.C., c. 570, would have applied in any
  circumstance had those sections, as they read on June 30, 2003, been
  in force and section 1.1 of the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14) not been
  enacted, those sections apply, with any modifications that the
  circumstances require.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 18, s. 131
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional treatment of Canadian
manufactured tobacco products

- **[[316]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-316 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following rules apply to a tobacco product manufactured in Canada
  before the implementation date:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if tax on the product imposed under section 23 of
    the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15) had not become payable before
    that day,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the tax is relieved,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if duty on the product imposed under the [Excise
      Act](/eng/acts/E-14) had not become payable before that day, the
      duty is relieved, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} this Act applies in respect of the product as
      though it were manufactured in Canada on that day by the
      manufacturer to the same extent that the product was manufactured
      immediately before that day;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the product was stamped or marked under the
    [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14), the product is deemed to be stamped or
    marked, as the case may be, under this Act; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14) and Parts III, VI
    and VII of the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15) cease to apply in
    respect of the product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refund of duty paid

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If duty imposed under the [Excise
  Act](/eng/acts/E-14) on a tobacco product manufactured in Canada
  before the implementation date had become payable before that day but
  tax under section 23 of the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15) had not
  become payable before that day, the manufacturer of the product may
  apply to the Minister for a refund of the duty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No refund shall be paid under this section unless the
  application for the refund is filed with the Minister in the
  prescribed form and manner within one year after the implementation
  date.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refund for re-worked or destroyed tobacco
product

**[[316.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-316.1 .sectionLabel}** If duty imposed
under the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14) and tax imposed under section 23
of the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15) on a tobacco product
manufactured in Canada had become payable before the implementation date
and a tobacco licensee under this Act who was, before that day, licensed
under those Acts to manufacture the product re-works or destroys, on or
after that day, the product in a manner authorized by the Minister,
section 181 applies as though that duty and tax were duty paid under
this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 18, s. 132
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional treatment of imported
tobacco products

**[[317]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-317 .sectionLabel}** The following rules
apply to an imported tobacco product:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} if duty levied under section 21 of the [Customs
  Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) and tax imposed under section 23 of the
  [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15) on the product had not become payable
  before the implementation date,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the duty and tax are relieved, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} this Act and the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6)
    apply in respect of the product as though it were imported into
    Canada on that day by the importer;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the product was stamped or marked under the
  [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14), the product is deemed to be stamped or
  marked, as the case may be, under this Act; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14) and Parts III, VI
  and VII of the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15) cease to apply in
  respect of the product.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imported tobacco delivered to duty free
shop before implementation date

**[[317.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-317.1 .sectionLabel}** If, on the
implementation date, imported manufactured tobacco on which tax under
section 23.12 of the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15) was paid is
possessed by a duty free shop licensee and no application for a refund
of the tax has been made under that Act, this Act applies in respect of
the tobacco as though the tax were special duty under section 53.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 18, s. 133
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional treatment of imported raw
leaf tobacco

**[[318]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-318 .sectionLabel}** If, on the
implementation date, a person possesses raw leaf tobacco that was
imported before that day, this Act applies as though the person imported
the tobacco on that day.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal of cigars from customs bonded
warehouse

**[[319]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-319 .sectionLabel}** If cigars manufactured
in Canada are located in a customs bonded warehouse on the
implementation date, they shall be removed from the warehouse and
entered into an excise warehouse on that day.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal of tobacco products from bonding
warehouse of manufacturer

- **[[320]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-320 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  tobacco product manufactured in Canada is, on the implementation date,
  located in a bonding warehouse of a person who is licensed under
  subsection 196(1) of the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14), the product
  shall be removed from the warehouse and entered into an excise
  warehouse on that day.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal of tobacco products from
  bonding warehouse of authorized distributor

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a tobacco product manufactured in Canada is, on
  the implementation date, located in a bonding warehouse of a person
  who is licensed under paragraph 50(1)(c) of the [Excise
  Act](/eng/acts/E-14), the product shall, on that day, be removed from
  the warehouse and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} entered into the person's special excise warehouse,
    if the person is a special excise warehouse licensee and the product
    is one that the person is permitted under this Act to distribute; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} returned to the excise warehouse of the tobacco
    licensee who manufactured the product.

## [SCHEDULE 1]{.scheduleLabel}[(Sections 42, 43.1 and 58.2, subsections 216(2) and (3) and section 240)]{.OriginatingRef}[Rates of Duty on Tobacco Products]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-182824 .scheduleLabel}
:::

- [1]{.lawlabel} Cigarettes: for each five cigarettes or fraction of
  five cigarettes contained in any package,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} \$0.92883; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the rate referred to in paragraph (a) has been
    adjusted under subsection 43.1(2), the adjusted rate.

- [2]{.lawlabel} Tobacco sticks: per stick,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} \$0.18576; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the rate referred to in paragraph (a) has been
    adjusted under subsection 43.1(2), the adjusted rate.

- [3]{.lawlabel} Manufactured tobacco other than cigarettes and tobacco
  sticks: per 50 grams or fraction of 50 grams contained in any package,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} \$11.61031; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the rate referred to in paragraph (a) has been
    adjusted under subsection 43.1(2), the adjusted rate.

- [4]{.lawlabel} Cigars: per 1,000 cigars,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} \$40.43121; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the rate referred to in paragraph (a) has been
    adjusted under subsection 43.1(2), the adjusted rate.

- [5]{.lawlabel} Raw leaf tobacco: \$1.572 per kilogram.

::: HistoricalNote

## [SCHEDULE 2]{.scheduleLabel}[(Sections 43 and 43.1 and subsections 216(2) and (3))]{.OriginatingRef}[Additional Duty on Cigars]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-182841 .scheduleLabel}
:::

Cigars: per cigar, the greater of

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} either

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} \$0.14533, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the rate referred to in subparagraph (i) has
    been adjusted under subsection 43.1(2), the adjusted rate; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amount obtained by multiplying the sale price, in
  the case of cigars manufactured in Canada, or the duty-paid value, in
  the case of imported cigars, by 88%.

::: HistoricalNote

## [SCHEDULE 3]{.scheduleLabel}[(Sections 53, 54, 56, 58.2 and 240)]{.OriginatingRef}[Rates of Special Duties on Certain Manufactured Tobacco]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-182850 .scheduleLabel}
:::

- [1]{.lawlabel} Special duty on imported manufactured tobacco:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in dollars per cigarette, the rate determined by
    the formula, rounded to the nearest one-hundred-thousandth, or, if
    the rate is equidistant from two consecutive
    one-hundred-thousandths, to the higher one-hundred-thousandth,

    ::: {#182853 .Provision}
    A/5

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the rate of duty applicable under section 1 of Schedule 1 for
        each five cigarettes;
    :::

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in dollars per tobacco stick, the rate of duty
    applicable under section 2 of Schedule 1 per tobacco stick; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in dollars per 50 grams, or fraction of 50 grams,
    contained in any package of manufactured tobacco other than
    cigarettes or tobacco sticks, the rate of duty applicable under
    section 3 of Schedule 1 per 50 grams.

- [2]{.lawlabel} Special duty on traveller's tobacco:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in dollars per cigarette, the rate determined by
    the formula, rounded to the nearest one-hundred-thousandth, or, if
    the rate is equidistant from two consecutive
    one-hundred-thousandths, to the higher one-hundred-thousandth,

    ::: {#182860 .Provision}
    A/5

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the rate of duty applicable under section 1 of Schedule 1 for
        each five cigarettes;
    :::

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in dollars per tobacco stick, the rate of duty
    applicable under section 2 of Schedule 1 per tobacco stick; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in dollars per 50 grams, or fraction of 50 grams,
    contained in any package of manufactured tobacco other than
    cigarettes or tobacco sticks, the rate of duty applicable under
    section 3 of Schedule 1 per 50 grams.

- [3]{.lawlabel} Special duty on unstamped tobacco products:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in dollars per cigarette, the rate determined by
    the formula, rounded to the nearest one-hundred-thousandth, or, if
    the rate is equidistant from two consecutive
    one-hundred-thousandths, to the higher one-hundred-thousandth,

    ::: {#182867 .Provision}
    A/5

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the rate of duty applicable under section 1 of Schedule 1 for
        each five cigarettes;
    :::

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in dollars per tobacco stick, the rate of duty
    applicable under section 2 of Schedule 1 per tobacco stick; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in dollars per kilogram of tobacco products other
    than cigarettes or tobacco sticks, the rate determined by the
    formula, rounded to the nearest one-hundred-thousandth, or, if the
    rate is equidistant from two consecutive one-hundred-thousandths, to
    the higher one-hundred-thousandth,

    ::: {#182872 .Provision}
    A × 20

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the rate of duty applicable under section 3 of Schedule 1 per
        50 grams of manufactured tobacco.
    :::

- [4]{.lawlabel} Special duty on stamped tobacco products:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} \$0.095724 per cigarette, in the case of
    cigarettes;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} \$0.095724 per stick, in the case of tobacco
    sticks; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} \$5.98275 per 50 grams or fraction of 50 grams
    contained in any package, in the case of tobacco products other than
    cigarettes or tobacco sticks.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, Sch. 3
- 2003, c. 15, ss. 51 to 54
- 2008, c. 28, ss. 65 to 68
- 2013, c. 33, ss. 57 to 60
- 2014, c. 20, s. 81

## [SCHEDULE 4]{.scheduleLabel}[(Sections 122, 123, 123.1, 159.1, 217 and 218)]{.OriginatingRef}[Rates of Duty on Spirits]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-182882 .scheduleLabel}
:::

- [1]{.lawlabel} Spirits: per litre of absolute ethyl alcohol contained
  in the spirits,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} \$11.930; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the rate referred to in paragraph (a) has been
    adjusted under subsection 123.1(2), the adjusted rate.

- [2]{.lawlabel} Spirits containing not more than 7% absolute ethyl
  alcohol by volume: per litre,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} \$0.301; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the rate referred to in paragraph (a) has been
    adjusted under subsection 123.1(2), the adjusted rate.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, Sch. 4
- 2006, c. 4, s. 48
- 2017, c. 20, s. 64

:::

Special duty on spirits:

\$0.12 per litre of absolute ethyl alcohol contained in the spirits.

## [SCHEDULE 6]{.scheduleLabel}[(Sections 134, 135, 135.1, 159.1, 217, 218, 242, 243 and 243.1)]{.OriginatingRef}[Rates of Duty on Wine]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-182896 .scheduleLabel}
:::

Wine:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of wine that contains not more than 1.2%
  of absolute ethyl alcohol by volume, per litre,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} \$0.0209, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the rate referred to in subparagraph (i) has
    been adjusted under subsection 135.1(2), the adjusted rate;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of wine that contains more than 1.2% of
  absolute ethyl alcohol by volume but not more than 7% of absolute
  ethyl alcohol by volume, per litre,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} \$0.301, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the rate referred to in subparagraph (i) has
    been adjusted under subsection 135.1(2), the adjusted rate; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of wine that contains more than 7% of
  absolute ethyl alcohol by volume, per litre,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} \$0.63, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the rate referred to in subparagraph (i) has
    been adjusted under subsection 135.1(2), the adjusted rate.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, Sch. 6
- 2006, c. 4, s. 49
- 2017, c. 20, s. 65

## [SCHEDULE 7]{.scheduleLabel}[(Sections 2, 158.19, 158.21, 158.24 to 158.27, 158.31, 158.34, 218.1, 233.1, 234.1 and 238.1)]{.OriginatingRef}[Duty on Cannabis Products]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-182910 .scheduleLabel}
:::

- [1]{.lawlabel} Any cannabis product produced in Canada or imported:
  the amount equal to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of dried cannabis, fresh cannabis,
    cannabis plants and cannabis plant seeds, the total of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} \$0.25 per gram of flowering material included in
      the cannabis product or used in the production of the cannabis
      product,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} \$0.075 per gram of non-flowering material
      included in the cannabis product or used in the production of the
      cannabis product,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} \$0.25 per viable seed included in the cannabis
      product or used in the production of the cannabis product, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} \$0.25 per vegetative cannabis plant included in
      the cannabis product or used in the production of the cannabis
      product; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, \$0.0025 per milligram of the
    total THC of the cannabis product.

- [2]{.lawlabel} Any cannabis product produced in Canada: the amount
  obtained by multiplying the dutiable amount for the cannabis product
  by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of dried cannabis, fresh cannabis,
    cannabis plants and cannabis plant seeds, 2.5%; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, 0%.

- [3]{.lawlabel} Any imported cannabis product: the amount obtained by
  multiplying the value of the cannabis product by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of dried cannabis, fresh cannabis,
    cannabis plants and cannabis plant seeds, 2.5%; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, 0%.

- [4]{.lawlabel} Any cannabis product taken for use or unaccounted for:
  the amount obtained by multiplying the fair market value of the
  cannabis product by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of dried cannabis, fresh cannabis,
    cannabis plants and cannabis plant seeds, 2.5%; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, 0%.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, Sch. 7
- 2018, c. 12, s. 93
- [2019, c. 29, s. 86](#1155569-1173756){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1155569-1173756.html"
  role="button"}
:::

## [SCHEDULE 8]{.scheduleLabel}[(Sections 158.57, 158.6, 158.61, 218.2, 233.2, 233.3, 234.2, 237, 238.01 and 249.1)]{.OriginatingRef}[Duty on Vaping Products]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-1366943 .scheduleLabel}
:::

- [1]{.lawlabel} Vaping products that are vaping devices that contain
  vaping substances or that are vaping substances in immediate
  containers: for each vaping device or immediate container of vaping
  substance

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the vaping substance is in liquid form, the
    amount equal to the total of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for the first 10 millilitres of vaping substance
      in the vaping device or immediate container, \$1.12 per 2
      millilitres of vaping substance or fraction thereof, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for any additional amount of vaping substance in
      the vaping device or immediate container, \$1.12 per 10
      millilitres of vaping substance or fraction thereof; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the vaping substance is in solid form, the
    amount equal to the total of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for the first 10 grams of vaping substance in the
      vaping device or immediate container, \$1.12 per 2 grams of vaping
      substance or fraction thereof, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for any additional amount of vaping substance in
      the vaping device or immediate container, \$1.12 per 10 grams of
      vaping substance or fraction thereof.

- [2]{.lawlabel} Vaping products that are vaping substances not in any
  vaping device or immediate container:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the vaping substance is in liquid form, the
    amount equal to the total of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for the first 10 millilitres of vaping substance,
      \$1.12 per 2 millilitres of vaping substance or fraction thereof,
      and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for any additional amount of vaping substance,
      \$1.12 per 10 millilitres of vaping substance or fraction thereof;
      and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the vaping substance is in solid form, the
    amount equal to the total of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for the first 10 grams of vaping substance,
      \$1.12 per 2 grams of vaping substance or fraction thereof, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for any additional amount of vaping substance,
      \$1.12 per 10 grams of vaping substance or fraction thereof.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 80](#1356454-1366959){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1356454-1366959.html"
  role="button"}

## RELATED PROVISIONS {#related-provisions .scheduleTitleText}

- --- 2006, c. 4, s. 42

  - [42]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of applying the provisions of the
    [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) that provide for the payment of, or
    the liability to pay, interest in respect of any amount, the amount
    shall be determined and interest shall be computed on it as though
    sections 37 to 41 had come into force on July 1, 2006.

- --- 2006, c. 4, s. 50

  - [50]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of applying the provisions of the
    [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) and the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14)
    that provide for the payment of, or the liability to pay, interest
    in respect of any amount, the amount shall be determined and
    interest shall be computed on it as though sections 43, 48 and 49
    had come into force on July 1, 2006.

- --- 2006, c. 4, s. 114(2)

  - - [114]{.lawlabel} [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) applies in
      respect of any extension of time that expires on or after April 1,
      2007.

- --- 2006, c. 4, s. 115(2)

  - - [115]{.lawlabel} [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) applies in
      respect of any fiscal month of a person that ends on or after
      April 1, 2007.

- --- 2006, c. 4, ss. 119(2) to (4)

  - - [119]{.lawlabel} [(2)]{.lawlabel} Section 251 of the Act, as
      enacted by subsection (1), applies in respect of any demand under
      section 169 of the Act served by the Minister of National Revenue
      on or after April 1, 2007.

    - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Section 251.1 of the Act, as enacted by
      subsection (1), applies

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of any return that is required to be
        filed under the Act on or after April 1, 2007; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of any return that is required to be
        filed under the Act before that day if it is not filed on or
        before March 31, 2007, in which case the day on or before which
        the return is required to be filed is deemed to be March 31,
        2007 for the purposes of calculating any penalty under that
        section.

    - [(4)]{.lawlabel} Section 251.2 of the Act, as enacted by
      subsection (1), applies in respect of any instrument that is
      dishonoured on or after April 1, 2007.

- --- 2006, c. 4, s. 123(2)

  - - [123]{.lawlabel} [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) applies in
      respect of any certificate made under subsection 288(1) of the Act
      in respect of amounts that became payable to the Receiver General
      on or after April 1, 2007.

- --- 2007, c. 2, s. 56(2)

  - - [56]{.lawlabel} [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) applies to wine
      taken for use after June 2006.

- --- 2007, c. 2, s. 57(2)

  - - [57]{.lawlabel} [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) applies to wine
      packaged after June 2006.

- --- 2007, c. 18, s. 157(2)

  - - [157]{.lawlabel} [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) applies to wine
      packaged after June 2006.

- --- 2007, c. 35, s. 209

    [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) that provide for the payment of, or
    the liability to pay, interest in respect of any amount, the amount
    shall be determined and interest shall be computed on it as though
    sections 204 to 208 had come into force on January 1, 2008.

- --- 2008, c. 28, ss. 49(2), (3)

  - - [49]{.lawlabel} [(2)]{.lawlabel} With respect to fermented liquor
      that is brewed by a brewer in whole or in part from malt, grain or
      any saccharine matter without any process of distillation and that
      has an alcoholic strength in excess of 11.9% absolute ethyl
      alcohol by volume, a valid licence held by the brewer under the
      [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14) is deemed to be a valid spirits
      licence issued under section 14 of the [Excise Act,
      2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1) until the day that is 30 days after the
      day on which this Act is assented to.

    - [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of applying the provisions of
      the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1) and of the [Customs
      Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) that provide for the payment of, or the
      liability to pay, interest in respect of any amount, the amount
      shall be determined and interest shall be computed on it as though
      this section had come into force on February 27, 2008.

- --- 2008, c. 28, s. 56(2)

  - - [56]{.lawlabel} [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) applies to
      imported manufactured tobacco that is delivered after February 26,
      2008.

- --- 2008, c. 28, s. 58(2)

  - - [58]{.lawlabel} [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) applies to
      imported manufactured tobacco that is black stock and that is,
      after February 26, 2008,

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} delivered to a duty free shop or customs bonded
        warehouse or to a person for use as ships' stores in accordance
        with the [Ships' Stores
        Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-40); or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} exported for delivery to a foreign duty free
        shop or as foreign ships' stores.

- --- 2008, c. 28, s. 69

- --- 2008, c. 28, s. 70(2)

  - - [70]{.lawlabel} [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of applying the
      provisions of the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1) and of the
      [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) that provide for the payment of,
      or the liability to pay, interest in respect of any amount, the
      amount shall be determined and interest shall be computed on it as

- --- 2010, c. 12, s. 54

  - ###### Meaning of [[implementation date]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} {#meaning-of-implementation-date .MarginalNote}

    - [54]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[implementation
      date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the first day of the month that
      follows the thirtieth day after the day on which this Act receives
      Royal Assent.

    - ###### Application {#application .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} Sections 38 to 46, subsections 47(1) and (3) and
      sections 48 to 53 apply as of the implementation date, except
      that, for the purposes of section 34 or 35 of the [Excise Act,
      2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1), a tobacco product may, on or after the
      implementation date but before April 2011, be entered into the
      duty-paid market or be released under the [Customs
      Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) for entry into the duty-paid market, as the
      case may be, if it is stamped in one of the following manners:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with the rules applicable under
        the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1) as those rules read on
        the day before the day on which this Act receives Royal Assent;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with the rules applicable under
        the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1) as those rules read on
        the implementation date or as they are amended from time to time
        after that date; or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the manner described in paragraphs (a) and
        (b).

    - ###### Effect --- paragraph (2)(a) {#effect-paragraph-2a .MarginalNote}

      [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a tobacco product is stamped in the manner
      described in paragraph (2)(a), the rules applicable under the
      [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1) as those rules read on the
      day before the day on which this Act receives Royal Assent apply
      in respect of the tobacco product.

    - ###### Effect --- paragraph (2)(b) or (c) {#effect-paragraph-2b-or-c .MarginalNote}

      [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a tobacco product is stamped in the manner
      described in paragraph (2)(b) or (c), the rules applicable under
      the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1) as those rules read on
      the implementation date, or as they are amended from time to time
      after that date, apply in respect of the tobacco product.

- --- 2013, c. 33, s. 61

- --- 2018, c. 12, s. 68(1)

  - - [68]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} If Bill C-45, introduced in the
      1st session of the 42nd Parliament and entitled the [Cannabis
      Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5) (referred to in this section and section
      118 as the "other Act"), receives royal assent, then for the
      purposes of this section and sections 117 and 118, [[commencement
      day]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the day on which subsection 204(1)
      of the other Act comes into force.

- --- 2018, c. 12, s. 117

  - - [117]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} In applying sections 14 and 23
      of the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1) in respect of a
      cannabis licence that is issued to a person before commencement
      day, the following rules apply beginning on the day on which the
      cannabis licence is issued until commencement day:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subsection 14(1.2) of that Act, as enacted by
        section 71, is to be read as follows:

        > ::: ReadAsText
        > - [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} A cannabis licence issued to a person
        >   shall not have effect before a licence or permit issued to
        >   the person under subsection 62(1) of the [Cannabis
        >   Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5) or a licence issued to the person
        >   under section 35 of the [Access to Cannabis for Medical
        >   Purposes Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2016-230) comes
        >   into effect.
        > :::

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 23(2.1)(a.1) of that Act, as enacted
        by subsection 72(1), is to be read as follows:

        > ::: ReadAsText
        > - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a cannabis licence, a
        >   licence or permit issued to the person under subsection
        >   62(1) of the [Cannabis Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5) or a licence
        >   issued to the person under section 35 of the [Access to
        >   Cannabis for Medical Purposes
        >   Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2016-230) is amended,
        >   suspended or revoked; or
        > :::

    - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Section 158.13 of the [Excise Act,
      2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1), as enacted by section 73, and section 84
      only apply to cannabis products that are entered into the
      duty-paid market on or after commencement day, including cannabis
      products that are delivered at any time to a purchaser for sale or
      distribution on or after commencement day.

    - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Sections 158.19 and 158.2 of the [Excise Act,
      2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1), as enacted by section 73, only apply to
      packaged cannabis products that are delivered to a purchaser on or
      after commencement day.

    - [(4)]{.lawlabel} Sections 158.21 and 158.22 of the [Excise Act,
      2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1), as enacted by section 73, only apply to
      cannabis products that are imported into Canada or released (as
      defined in the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6)) on or after
      commencement day.

    - [(5)]{.lawlabel} Section 158.25 of the [Excise Act,
      2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1), as enacted by section 73, only applies to
      cannabis products that are taken for use on or after commencement
      day.

    - [(6)]{.lawlabel} Section 158.26 of the [Excise Act,
      2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1), as enacted by section 73, only applies to
      cannabis products that, on or after commencement day, cannot be
      accounted for as being in the possession of a cannabis licensee or
      in the possession of a person in accordance with subsection
      158.11(3) or paragraph 158.11(5)(a) of that Act, as enacted by
      section 73.

- --- 2018, c. 12, s. 118

  - - [118]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[transitional
      period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the period beginning on the
      first day on which both this Act and the other Act have received
      royal assent and ending at the end of the day preceding
      commencement day.

    - [(2)]{.lawlabel} If, at any time during the transitional period,
      provisions of the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1), as enacted
      or amended by this Part, rely on or incorporate provisions or
      concepts found in provisions of the other Act (other than
      subsection 204(1) of the other Act) that are not in force at that
      time, those provisions of the other Act are deemed, despite
      subsection 226(1) of the other Act, to be in force at that time
      but only for the purposes of applying the [Excise Act,
      2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1).

- --- 2019, c. 29, s. 86(2)

- --- 2022, c. 10, ss. 128(2) to (4), as amended by 2022, c. 19, s. 102

  - - [128]{.lawlabel} [(2)]{.lawlabel} Sections 158.41, 158.57 and
      158.58 of the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1), as enacted by

      [[released]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} (as defined in subsection 2(1)

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} vaping products that are imported into Canada
        or [[released]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} (as defined in subsection

        which this Act receives royal assent but before October 1, 2022

    - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Sections 158.42 to 158.47 and 158.49 and
      subsection 158.5(2) of the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1),
      as enacted by section 59, subsection 63(1) and sections 107 to 109
      come into force on October 1, 2022. However, those provisions of

      sections 107 to 109 do not apply before 2023 in respect of

        2(1) of the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6)) before October 1,

    - [(3.1)]{.lawlabel} Sections 158.54 to 158.56, 158.6 and 158.61 of

      59, come into force on October 1, 2022. However, those sections of

      of

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} vaping products manufactured in Canada that are
        packaged before October 1, 2022 and that are not stamped; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} vaping products that are imported into Canada
        or [[released]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} (as defined in subsection
        2(1) of the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6)) before October 1,
        2022 and that are not stamped.

    - [(4)]{.lawlabel} In applying sections 158.57 and 158.58 of the

      paragraph 14(1)(f) of the Act, as it read immediately before that
      day, also authorizes the person under subparagraph 14(1)(f)(ii) of
      the Act, as enacted by subsection (1), as of that day.

- --- 2024, c. 15, ss. 146(3), (4)

  - - [146]{.lawlabel} [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) applies in
      respect of vaping products manufactured in Canada that are
      packaged after 2023.

    - [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) applies in respect of vaping
      products that are imported into Canada or
      [[released]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in subsection 2(1)
      of the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), after 2023.

- --- 2024, c. 15, s. 147(2)
